        "from google.colab import drive\n",
        "import os, sqlite3\n",
        "\n",
        "MOUNT = \"/gdrive\"\n",
        "DB    = f\"{MOUNT}/MyDrive/trading/ai_trades.sqlite\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# /gdrive won't exist, so no \"mountpoint contains files\" conflict\n",
        "os.makedirs(MOUNT, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "drive.mount(MOUNT, force_remount=False)\n",
        "assert os.path.ismount(MOUNT), \"❌ Drive mount failed\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# Sanity: require the DB file to already exist on Drive (no local creation)\n",
        "if not os.path.exists(DB):\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\n",
        "        f\"DB not found on Drive at {DB}. \"\n",
        "        \"Put your ai_trades.sqlite there. I will not create a new one.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "# Quick read test (no WAL changes, no writes)\n",
        "con = sqlite3.connect(f\"file:{DB}?mode=rw\", uri=True)\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    cur = con.cursor()\n",
        "    tables = [t for (t,) in cur.execute(\"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\")]\n",
        "    print(\"✅ Drive & DB OK | tables:\", tables)\n",
        "finally:\n",
        "    con.close()"

        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def pipi(*args):\n",
        "    subprocess.check_call([sys.executable, \"-m\", \"pip\", \"install\", *args])\n",
        "\n",
        "# Stable pins confirmed in our session\n",
        "pins = [\n",

        "]\n",

        "import pkg_resources\n",
        "\n",

        "    try:\n",
        "        installed = pkg_resources.get_distribution(pkg_name).version\n",
        "        if installed != pkg_ver:\n",

        "# === SINGLE CONSOLIDATED CELL: Step 3 (no duplicate functionality, no override of your working helpers) ===\n",
        "import os, re, json, requests, pandas as pd, numpy as np, datetime as dt, threading\n",
        "from dateutil import parser as dtparse\n",
        "\n",
        "# from IPython.display import clear_output\n",
        "import time\n",
        "\n",
        "# def auto_clear_logs():\n",
        "#     while True:\n",
        "#         time.sleep(120)\n",

        "#         print(\"🧹 Console cleared automatically\")\n",
        "\n",

        "# log_cleaner.start()\n",
        "\n",
        "SESSION = requests.Session()\n",
        "from requests.adapters import HTTPAdapter\n",
        "from urllib3.util.retry import Retry\n",
        "_retry = Retry(total=3, backoff_factor=0.5, status_forcelist=[429,500,502,503,504])\n",
        "SESSION.mount(\"https://\", HTTPAdapter(max_retries=_retry))\n",
        "\n",
        "# === destructive-action confirmations ===\n",
        "CONFIRM = {}          # maps chat_id -> {\"action\": str, \"expires\": float}\n",
        "CONFIRM_TTL = 30.0    # seconds\n",

        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Config / env\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "IST          = dt.timezone(dt.timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))\n",
        "BASE         = \"https://apiconnect.angelone.in\"\n",

        "\n",
        "API_KEY      = os.environ.get(\"ANGEL_API_KEY\",\"\").strip()\n",
        "USER         = os.environ.get(\"ANGEL_CLIENT_CODE\",\"\").strip()\n",
        "PWD          = os.environ.get(\"ANGEL_PASSWORD\",\"\").strip()\n",
        "TOTP_SEC     = os.environ.get(\"ANGEL_TOTP_SECRET\",\"\").strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "UNDERLYINGS  = [\"NIFTY\", \"SENSEX\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "STRIKES_BAND = int(os.environ.get(\"STRIKES_BAND\",\"8\"))\n",
        "INCLUDE_BOTH = os.environ.get(\"INCLUDE_BOTH\",\"1\").strip().upper() in (\"1\",\"YES\",\"TRUE\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- ensure broker globals always exist, even before login / on failures\n",
        "JWT = globals().get(\"JWT\", None)\n",
        "REFRESH = globals().get(\"REFRESH\", None)\n",
        "smart = globals().get(\"smart\", None)\n",
        "FEED_TOKEN = globals().get(\"FEED_TOKEN\", None)\n",
        "LIVE_BUFFER = {}\n",
        "CURRENT_CANDLE = {}\n",
        "DATA_LOCK = threading.Lock()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# SmartAPI login (only if JWT missing)\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "def update_live_price(token, ltp):\n",
        "    with DATA_LOCK:\n",
        "        now = dt.datetime.now(IST)\n",
        "        minute_key = now.replace(second=0, microsecond=0)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if token not in CURRENT_CANDLE:\n",
        "            CURRENT_CANDLE[token] = {\n",
        "                \"minute\": minute_key,\n",
        "                \"open\": ltp,\n",
        "                \"high\": ltp,\n",
        "                \"low\": ltp,\n",
        "                \"close\": ltp\n",
        "            }\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        candle = CURRENT_CANDLE[token]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if candle[\"minute\"] == minute_key:\n",
        "            candle[\"high\"] = max(candle[\"high\"], ltp)\n",
        "            candle[\"low\"] = min(candle[\"low\"], ltp)\n",
        "            candle[\"close\"] = ltp\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            append_candle_to_df(token, candle)\n",
        "            CURRENT_CANDLE[token] = {\n",
        "                \"minute\": minute_key,\n",
        "                \"open\": ltp,\n",
        "                \"high\": ltp,\n",
        "                \"low\": ltp,\n",
        "                \"close\": ltp\n",
        "            }\n",
        "SYMBOL_DATA = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---- Index Candle Cache (rate-limit protection) ----\n",
        "INDEX_CANDLE_CACHE = {}\n",
        "INDEX_CANDLE_LAST_FETCH = {}\n",
        "INDEX_CACHE_TTL = 300  # seconds (5 minutes)\n",
        "\n",
        "def append_candle_to_df(token, candle):\n",
        "  with DATA_LOCK:\n",
        "    df = SYMBOL_DATA.get(token)\n",
        "    row = {\n",
        "        \"time\": candle[\"minute\"],\n",
        "        \"open\": candle[\"open\"],\n",
        "        \"high\": candle[\"high\"],\n",
        "        \"low\": candle[\"low\"],\n",
        "        \"close\": candle[\"close\"],\n",
        "    }\n",
        "    if df is None:\n",
        "        SYMBOL_DATA[token] = pd.DataFrame([row])\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        SYMBOL_DATA[token] = pd.concat([df, pd.DataFrame([row])]).tail(500)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _ensure_login():\n",
        "    global smart, JWT, REFRESH, FEED_TOKEN\n",
        "    if 'JWT' in globals() and JWT:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "    assert API_KEY and USER and PWD, \"Missing ANGEL creds in env.\"\n",
        "    from SmartApi import SmartConnect\n",
        "    sc = SmartConnect(API_KEY)\n",
        "    if TOTP_SEC:\n",
        "        import pyotp\n",
        "        login = sc.generateSession(USER, PWD, pyotp.TOTP(TOTP_SEC).now())\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        login = sc.generateSession(USER, PWD)\n",
        "    assert login.get(\"status\"), f\"Login failed: {login}\"\n",
        "    tok = sc.generateToken(login[\"data\"][\"refreshToken\"])\n",
        "    JWT = tok[\"data\"][\"jwtToken\"]\n",
        "    REFRESH = tok[\"data\"][\"refreshToken\"]\n",
        "    FEED_TOKEN = login[\"data\"][\"feedToken\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    smart = sc\n",
        "    print(\"✅ Login OK | JWT len:\", len(JWT))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ---- Sync open positions from broker ----\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        pos = smart.position()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if pos.get(\"status\") and pos.get(\"data\"):\n",
        "            for p in pos[\"data\"]:\n",
        "                net = int(p.get(\"netqty\", 0))\n",
        "                sym = p.get(\"tradingsymbol\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                if net != 0 and sym:\n",
        "                    engine._positions[sym] = True\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(\"✅ Synced broker positions:\", list(engine._positions.keys()))\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(\"Position sync failed:\", e)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Only log in if needed (for ATM via LTP and candles)\n",
        "if not JWT:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        _ensure_login()\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(\"ℹ️ Proceeding without JWT (median ATM fallback).\", e)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- START WEBSOCKET AFTER LOGIN ----------\n",
        "from SmartApi.smartWebSocketV2 import SmartWebSocketV2\n",
        "def on_data(wsapp, message):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        token = message.get(\"token\")\n",
        "        ltp_raw = message.get(\"last_traded_price\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if token is None or ltp_raw is None:\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        ltp = float(ltp_raw) / 100.0\n",
        "\n",
        "        #print(f\"TICK: {token} {ltp}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        update_live_price(token, ltp)\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(\"WS data error:\", e)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _start_ws():\n",
        "    global ws\n",
        "    ws = SmartWebSocketV2(JWT, API_KEY, USER, FEED_TOKEN)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def on_open(wsapp):\n",
        "        print(\"✅ WebSocket Connected\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        tokens = list(TOKMAP.values()) if \"TOKMAP\" in globals() else []\n",
        "        if tokens:\n",
        "            nfo_tokens = []\n",
        "            bfo_tokens = []\n",
        "\n",
        "            for sym, tok in TOKMAP.items():\n",
        "\n",
        "                if \"SENSEX\" in sym:\n",
        "                    bfo_tokens.append(tok)\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    nfo_tokens.append(tok)\n",
        "\n",
        "            subs = []\n",
        "\n",
        "            if nfo_tokens:\n",
        "                subs.append({\n",
        "                    \"exchangeType\": 2,\n",
        "                    \"tokens\": nfo_tokens\n",
        "                })\n",
        "\n",
        "            if bfo_tokens:\n",
        "                subs.append({\n",
        "                    \"exchangeType\": 4,\n",
        "                    \"tokens\": bfo_tokens\n",
        "                })\n",
        "\n",
        "            ws.subscribe(\n",
        "                correlation_id=\"ws_1\",\n",
        "                mode=1,\n",
        "                token_list=subs\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"✅ Subscribed NFO:{len(nfo_tokens)} BFO:{len(bfo_tokens)}\")\n",
        "            print(f\"✅ Subscribed to {len(tokens)} tokens\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    ws.on_open = on_open\n",
        "    ws.on_data = on_data\n",
        "    ws.on_error = lambda ws, err: print(\"WS ERROR:\", err)\n",
        "    ws.on_close = lambda ws: print(\"⚠️ WebSocket Closed\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    import threading\n",
        "    global ws_thread\n",
        "    ws_thread = threading.Thread(target=ws.connect, daemon=True)\n",
        "    ws_thread.start()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Internal helpers (one copy only)\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "_MMAP   = {\"JAN\":1,\"FEB\":2,\"MAR\":3,\"APR\":4,\"MAY\":5,\"JUN\":6,\"JUL\":7,\"AUG\":8,\"SEP\":9,\"OCT\":10,\"NOV\":11,\"DEC\":12}\n",
        "_SYM_RX = re.compile(r'^(NIFTY|BANKNIFTY|FINNIFTY|SENSEX)(\\d{2})([A-Z]{3})(\\d{2})(\\d+)(CE|PE)$', re.IGNORECASE)\n",
        "\n",
        "def _to_dt(x):\n",
        "    if pd.isna(x): return pd.NaT\n",
        "    try: return dtparse.parse(str(x), dayfirst=True, fuzzy=True)\n",
        "    except Exception: return pd.NaT\n",
        "\n",
        "def _expiry_from_symbol(ts: str):\n",
        "    if not isinstance(ts, str): return pd.NaT\n",
        "    m = _SYM_RX.match(ts.strip().upper())\n",
        "    if not m: return pd.NaT\n",
        "    dd, mmm, yy = int(m.group(2)), m.group(3).upper(), int(m.group(4))\n",
        "    mon = _MMAP.get(mmm)\n",
        "    if not mon: return pd.NaT\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        return dt.date(2000+yy, mon, dd)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return pd.NaT\n",
        "\n",
        "def _first_col(df: pd.DataFrame, *alts):\n",
        "    low = {c.lower(): c for c in df.columns}\n",
        "    for a in alts:\n",
        "        if a.lower() in low: return low[a.lower()]\n",
        "    return None\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Minimal header builder (define only if not already defined)\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "if \"_hdr\" not in globals():\n",
        "    def _hdr(jwt_):\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            \"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {jwt_}\",\n",
        "            \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n",
        "            \"Accept\": \"application/json\",\n",
        "            \"X-UserType\":\"USER\",\"X-SourceID\":\"WEB\",\n",
        "            \"X-ClientLocalIP\":\"127.0.0.1\",\"X-ClientPublicIP\":\"127.0.0.1\",\n",
        "            \"X-MACAddress\":\"AA:BB:CC:DD:EE:FF\",\"X-PrivateKey\": API_KEY,\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Normalize UNIMAP, or build from Scrip Master if missing\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "def _normalize_from_dataframe(df_raw: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n",
        "    df = df_raw.copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "    c_exch   = _first_col(df, \"exchange\",\"exch\",\"exch_seg\")\n",
        "    c_inst   = _first_col(df, \"instrument\",\"instrumenttype\",\"inst_type\")\n",
        "    c_opt    = _first_col(df, \"optionType\",\"optiontype\",\"cp\",\"opttype\")\n",
        "    c_strike = _first_col(df, \"strike\",\"strikeprice\",\"strikePrice\")\n",
        "    c_lot    = _first_col(df, \"lotSize\",\"lotsize\",\"lot_size\")\n",
        "    c_ts     = _first_col(df, \"tradingsymbol\",\"tradingSymbol\",\"symbol\",\"ts\",\"symbolname\")\n",
        "    c_tok    = _first_col(df, \"symboltoken\",\"instrumenttoken\",\"token\",\"scripcode\")\n",
        "    c_und    = _first_col(df, \"underlying\",\"underlyingSymbol\",\"underlyingname\",\"name\")\n",
        "    c_exp    = _first_col(df, \"expiry_dt\",\"expiry\",\"expiryDate\",\"expirydate\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    def S(col, default=\"\"):\n",
        "        return df[col] if col else pd.Series([default]*len(df), index=df.index)\n",
        "\n",
        "    out = pd.DataFrame(index=df.index)\n",
        "\n",
        "    out[\"exchange\"]   = S(c_exch,\"NFO\").astype(str).str.upper().replace({\"NSEFO\":\"NFO\"})\n",
        "    out[\"instrument\"] = S(c_inst,\" \").astype(str).str.upper()\n",
        "    out[\"optionType\"] = S(c_opt,\" \").astype(str).str.upper().replace({\"CALL\":\"CE\",\"PUT\":\"PE\"})\n",
        "    out[\"strike\"]     = pd.to_numeric(S(c_strike, pd.NA), errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "    out[\"lotSize\"] = pd.to_numeric(S(c_lot, np.nan), errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "    out = out[out[\"lotSize\"].notna() & (out[\"lotSize\"] > 0)]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    out[\"symbol\"]      = S(c_ts,\" \").astype(str)\n",
        "    out[\"symbolToken\"] = S(c_tok,\" \").astype(str)\n",
        "    out[\"underlying\"]  = S(c_und,\" \").astype(str)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # expiry\n",
        "    if c_exp:\n",
        "        d = pd.to_datetime(df[c_exp].astype(str), errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "        out[\"expiry_dt\"] = d.dt.date\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        out[\"expiry_dt\"] = pd.NaT\n",
        "\n",
        "    opt = out[\n",
        "        (out[\"exchange\"].isin([\"NFO\",\"BFO\"]))&\n",
        "        (out[\"instrument\"].str.contains(\"OPT\", na=False)) &\n",
        "        (out[\"optionType\"].isin([\"CE\",\"PE\"])) &\n",
        "        (~out[\"expiry_dt\"].isna()) &\n",
        "        (~out[\"strike\"].isna()) &\n",
        "        (out[\"symbol\"].str.len()>0) &\n",
        "        (out[\"symbolToken\"].str.len()>0)\n",
        "    ].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return opt.reset_index(drop=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _normalize_unimap_any(unimap_obj) -> pd.DataFrame:\n",
        "    if isinstance(unimap_obj, pd.DataFrame):\n",
        "        return _normalize_from_dataframe(unimap_obj)\n",
        "    elif isinstance(unimap_obj, dict):\n",
        "        rows = []\n",
        "        for k, v in unimap_obj.items():\n",
        "            if not isinstance(v, dict): continue\n",
        "            row = {\"tradingsymbol\": v.get(\"tradingsymbol\", k)}\n",
        "            row[\"exchange\"]     = v.get(\"exchange\") or v.get(\"exch\") or v.get(\"exch_seg\")\n",
        "            row[\"instrument\"]   = v.get(\"instrument\") or v.get(\"instrumenttype\") or v.get(\"inst_type\")\n",
        "            row[\"optionType\"]   = v.get(\"optionType\") or v.get(\"optiontype\") or v.get(\"cp\") or v.get(\"opttype\")\n",
        "            row[\"strike\"]       = v.get(\"strike\") or v.get(\"strikeprice\") or v.get(\"strikePrice\")\n",
        "            row[\"symboltoken\"]  = v.get(\"symboltoken\") or v.get(\"instrumenttoken\") or v.get(\"token\") or v.get(\"scripcode\")\n",
        "            row[\"underlying\"]   = v.get(\"underlying\") or v.get(\"underlyingSymbol\") or v.get(\"underlyingname\") or v.get(\"name\")\n",
        "            row[\"expiry\"]       = v.get(\"expiry_dt\") or v.get(\"expiry\") or v.get(\"expiryDate\") or v.get(\"expirydate\")\n",
        "            rows.append(row)\n",
        "        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n",
        "        return _normalize_from_dataframe(df)\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        raise TypeError(\"UNIMAP must be a DataFrame or dict.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# LTP fetch for ATM straddle (safe if JWT missing)\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "def ltp_quote(triples):\n",
        "    if 'JWT' not in globals() or not JWT: return {}\n",
        "    if not triples: return {}\n",
        "    out={}\n",
        "    for i in range(0, len(triples), 50):\n",
        "        chunk = triples[i:i+50]\n",
        "        by_ex = {}\n",
        "        for ex, ts, tok in chunk:\n",
        "            by_ex.setdefault(ex, []).append(str(tok))\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            r = SESSION.post(f\"{BASE}/rest/secure/angelbroking/market/v1/quote/\",\n",
        "                              headers=_hdr(JWT), json={\"mode\":\"LTP\",\"exchangeTokens\":by_ex}, timeout=20)\n",
        "            js = r.json()\n",
        "            fetched = ((js or {}).get(\"data\") or {}).get(\"fetched\") or []\n",
        "            for row in fetched:\n",
        "                ts = row.get(\"tradingSymbol\") or row.get(\"tradingsymbol\") or \"\"\n",
        "                out[ts] = row.get(\"ltp\")\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "    return out\n",
        "\n",
        "def _nearest_expiry(df, u):\n",
        "    today = dt.datetime.now(IST).date()\n",
        "    exps = sorted([d for d in df.loc[df[\"underlying\"].eq(u), \"expiry_dt\"].dropna().unique().tolist() if d >= today])\n",
        "    return exps[0] if exps else None\n",
        "\n",
        "def _atm_from_straddle(chain_df):\n",
        "    triples = [(row[\"exchange\"], row[\"symbol\"], str(row[\"symbolToken\"])) for _, row in chain_df.iterrows()]\n",
        "    m = ltp_quote(triples)\n",
        "    if not m: return None\n",
        "    tmp = chain_df[[\"strike\",\"optionType\",\"symbol\"]].copy()\n",
        "    tmp[\"ltp\"] = tmp[\"symbol\"].map(m)\n",
        "    piv = tmp.pivot_table(index=\"strike\", columns=\"optionType\", values=\"ltp\", aggfunc=\"last\")\n",
        "    if not {\"CE\",\"PE\"}.issubset(set(piv.columns)): return None\n",
        "    diff = (piv[\"CE\"] - piv[\"PE\"]).abs().dropna()\n",
        "    if diff.empty: return None\n",
        "    return float(diff.idxmin())\n",
        "\n",
        "def _atm_fallback(chain_df):\n",
        "    s = pd.Series(chain_df[\"strike\"].dropna())\n",
        "    return float(s.median()) if not s.empty else None\n",
        "\n",
        "def filter_near_atm(symbols, spot, step=50, strikes=2):\n",
        "    filtered = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for sym in symbols:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            strike = int(sym[-7:-2])  # extract strike\n",
        "            if abs(strike - spot) <= strikes * step:\n",
        "                filtered.append(sym)\n",
        "        except:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    return filtered\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Build DF_UNIVERSE / UNIMAP / UNIVERSE (once)\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "def _build_from_master():\n",
        "    r = SESSION.get(MASTER_URL, timeout=60)\n",
        "    r.raise_for_status()\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        items = r.json()\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\"Failed to decode scrip master JSON\")\n",
        "    def g(x,*keys):\n",
        "        for k in keys:\n",
        "            if k in x and x[k] not in (\"\", None):\n",
        "                return x[k]\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "    rows = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for x in items:\n",
        "\n",
        "        ts = str(g(x, \"tradingsymbol\", \"tradingSymbol\", \"symbol\") or \"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        strike_val = float(g(x, \"strike\", \"strikePrice\") or 0)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Angel sometimes stores strike *100\n",
        "        if strike_val > 100000:\n",
        "            strike_val = strike_val / 100\n",
        "\n",
        "        rows.append({\n",
        "            \"exchange\":      str(g(x,\"exch_seg\",\"exchange\") or \"\").upper(),\n",
        "            \"symbol\":        ts,\n",
        "            \"symbolToken\":   str(g(x,\"token\",\"symboltoken\",\"symbolToken\") or \"\"),\n",
        "            \"underlying\":    (g(x,\"name\",\"underlying\") or \"\").upper(),\n",
        "            \"instrument\":    str(g(x,\"instrumenttype\",\"instrumentType\") or \"\").upper(),\n",
        "            \"optionType\":    str(g(x,\"optiontype\",\"optionType\") or \"\").upper(),\n",
        "            \"expiry\":        g(x,\"expiry\",\"expiryDate\"),\n",
        "            \"strike\":        strike_val,\n",
        "            \"lotSize\":       int(g(x,\"lotsize\",\"lotSize\") or 0),\n",
        "        })\n",
        "    dfo = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n",
        "    dfo[\"expiry_dt\"] = dfo[\"expiry\"].apply(_to_dt)\n",
        "    return dfo\n",
        "\n",
        "# Prefer existing UNIMAP if available; otherwise build from master\n",
        "# This section has been refactored to fix the IndentationError and logical flow.\n",
        "# Always build from master — UNIMAP is unreliable\n",
        "\n",
        "# ===============================\n",
        "# TRADE UNDERLYING CONTROL\n",
        "# ===============================\n",
        "\n",
        "TRADE_UNDERLYING = TRADE_UNDERLYING.upper()\n",
        "\n",
        "if TRADE_UNDERLYING == \"NIFTY\":\n",
        "    UNDERLYINGS = [\"NIFTY\"]\n",
        "elif TRADE_UNDERLYING == \"SENSEX\":\n",
        "    UNDERLYINGS = [\"SENSEX\"]\n",
        "elif TRADE_UNDERLYING == \"BOTH\":\n",
        "    UNDERLYINGS = [\"NIFTY\",\"SENSEX\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"🎯 Trading Underlying:\", UNDERLYINGS)\n",
        "dfo = _build_from_master()\n",
        "dfo[\"underlying\"] = dfo[\"underlying\"].astype(str).str.upper()\n",
        "\n",
        "# Filter to NFO options and requested underlyings\n",
        "dfo = dfo[\n",
        "    (dfo[\"exchange\"].isin([\"NFO\", \"BFO\"])) &\n",
        "    (dfo[\"instrument\"].str.contains(\"OPT\", na=False)) &\n",
        "    (dfo[\"underlying\"].isin(UNDERLYINGS))\n",
        "].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# CE/PE fallback\n",
        "suf = dfo[\"symbol\"].str[-2:].str.upper()\n",
        "dfo[\"optionType\"] = dfo[\"optionType\"].str.upper()\n",
        "dfo.loc[~dfo[\"optionType\"].isin([\"CE\",\"PE\"]), \"optionType\"] = suf\n",
        "\n",
        "_norm = _normalize_from_dataframe(dfo)\n",
        "FULL_NORM = _norm.copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Available underlyings in master:\",\n",
        "      sorted(_norm[\"underlying\"].unique()))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Build ATM-centered picks per underlying\n",
        "picks = []\n",
        "logs  = []\n",
        "for u in UNDERLYINGS:\n",
        "    chain_all = _norm[_norm[\"underlying\"].eq(u)].copy()\n",
        "    if chain_all.empty:\n",
        "        logs.append(f\"  {u}: no options rows\")\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "    exp = _nearest_expiry(chain_all, u)\n",
        "    if exp is None:\n",
        "        logs.append(f\"  {u}: no expiry found\")\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "    chain = chain_all[chain_all[\"expiry_dt\"].eq(exp)].copy()\n",
        "    if chain.empty:\n",
        "        logs.append(f\"  {u}: empty chain @ {exp}\")\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    atm = _atm_from_straddle(chain) or _atm_fallback(chain)\n",
        "    if atm is None:\n",
        "        logs.append(f\"  {u}: ATM not found @ {exp}\")\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    strikes = sorted(chain[\"strike\"].dropna().unique().tolist())\n",
        "    i = min(range(len(strikes)), key=lambda j: abs(strikes[j]-atm))\n",
        "    chosen = [strikes[i]]\n",
        "    for k in range(1, STRIKES_BAND+1):\n",
        "        if i-k >= 0:            chosen.append(strikes[i-k])\n",
        "        if i+k < len(strikes):  chosen.append(strikes[i+k])\n",
        "    chosen = sorted(set(chosen))\n",
        "\n",
        "    sub = chain[chain[\"strike\"].isin(chosen)]\n",
        "    if not INCLUDE_BOTH:\n",
        "        sub = sub[sub[\"optionType\"].eq(\"CE\")]\n",
        "    picks.append(sub.sort_values([\"strike\",\"optionType\"]))\n",
        "    logs.append(f\"  {u}: ATM\\u2248{atm:.2f} | picked {sub.shape[0]} @ {exp}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "DF_UNIVERSE = (pd.concat(picks, ignore_index=True) if picks else\n",
        "               pd.DataFrame(columns=[\"symbol\",\"exchange\",\"symbolToken\",\"strike\",\"optionType\",\"expiry_dt\",\"underlying\",\"instrument\"]))\n",
        "# Safety validation: lot sizes must be valid\n",
        "if not DF_UNIVERSE.empty:\n",
        "    bad = DF_UNIVERSE.loc[DF_UNIVERSE[\"lotSize\"] <= 0]\n",
        "    if not bad.empty:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\n",
        "            f\"Invalid lot sizes detected for symbols: {bad['symbol'].tolist()[:5]}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "UNIVERSE = DF_UNIVERSE[\"symbol\"].tolist()\n",
        "TOKMAP   = {row[\"symbol\"]: str(row.get(\"symbolToken\",\"\")) for _, row in DF_UNIVERSE.iterrows()}\n",
        "# ---- Initialize candle storage for all tokens ----\n",
        "for tok in TOKMAP.values():\n",
        "    SYMBOL_DATA.setdefault(tok, pd.DataFrame())\n",
        "globals()[\"TOKMAP\"] = TOKMAP\n",
        "globals()[\"UNIVERSE\"] = UNIVERSE\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- HARD GUARD: ensure universe exists before AI loads ---\n",
        "if not UNIVERSE or not TOKMAP:\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"Universe not built correctly before AI import\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def rebuild_universe(selected_underlyings):\n",
        "    global DF_UNIVERSE, UNIVERSE, TOKMAP\n",
        "\n",
        "    selected_underlyings = [u.upper() for u in selected_underlyings]\n",
        "\n",
        "    picks = []\n",
        "    logs = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for u in selected_underlyings:\n",
        "        chain_all = FULL_NORM[FULL_NORM[\"underlying\"].eq(u)].copy()\n",
        "        if chain_all.empty:\n",
        "            logs.append(f\"{u}: no options rows\")\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        exp = _nearest_expiry(chain_all, u)\n",
        "        if exp is None:\n",
        "            logs.append(f\"{u}: no expiry found\")\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        chain = chain_all[chain_all[\"expiry_dt\"].eq(exp)].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "        atm = _atm_from_straddle(chain) or _atm_fallback(chain)\n",
        "        if atm is None:\n",
        "            logs.append(f\"{u}: ATM not found\")\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        strikes = sorted(chain[\"strike\"].dropna().unique().tolist())\n",
        "        i = min(range(len(strikes)), key=lambda j: abs(strikes[j]-atm))\n",
        "\n",
        "        chosen = [strikes[i]]\n",
        "        for k in range(1, STRIKES_BAND+1):\n",
        "            if i-k >= 0: chosen.append(strikes[i-k])\n",
        "            if i+k < len(strikes): chosen.append(strikes[i+k])\n",
        "\n",
        "        chosen = sorted(set(chosen))\n",
        "        sub = chain[chain[\"strike\"].isin(chosen)]\n",
        "        picks.append(sub.sort_values([\"strike\",\"optionType\"]))\n",
        "        logs.append(f\"{u}: ATM≈{atm:.2f} | picked {sub.shape[0]}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    DF_UNIVERSE = pd.concat(picks, ignore_index=True) if picks else pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if DF_UNIVERSE.empty:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\"Universe rebuild produced empty set\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    UNIVERSE = DF_UNIVERSE[\"symbol\"].tolist()\n",
        "    TOKMAP = {row[\"symbol\"]: str(row[\"symbolToken\"]) for _, row in DF_UNIVERSE.iterrows()}\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not isinstance(TOKMAP, dict) or not TOKMAP:\n",
        "      raise RuntimeError(\"TOKMAP not initialized before engine start\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Universe rebuilt:\", selected_underlyings)\n",
        "    print(\"\\n\".join(logs))\n",
        "INDEX_TOKENS = {\n",
        "    \"NIFTY\": \"99926000\",\n",
        "    \"SENSEX\": \"99919000\"\n",
        "\n",
        "}\n",
        "globals()[\"TOKMAP\"] = TOKMAP\n",
        "globals()[\"UNIVERSE\"] = UNIVERSE\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---- Add Index Spot Tokens to WebSocket Universe ----\n",
        "for name, tok in INDEX_TOKENS.items():\n",
        "    TOKMAP[name] = tok\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def restart_ws():\n",
        "    global ws\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if \"ws\" in globals() and ws:\n",
        "            ws.close()\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        _start_ws()\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(\"WebSocket restart failed:\", e)\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------- LOT SIZE RESOLVER (FROM MASTER) --------\n",
        "def lot_size_for(sym: str) -> int:\n",
        "    if \"DF_UNIVERSE\" not in globals():\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\"DF_UNIVERSE not initialized\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    row = DF_UNIVERSE.loc[DF_UNIVERSE[\"symbol\"] == sym]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if row.empty:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(f\"Lot size not found for {sym}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lot = row[\"lotSize\"].iloc[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if pd.isna(lot) or lot <= 0:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(f\"Invalid lot size for {sym}: {lot}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return int(lot)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"ACTIVE_UNDER:\", UNDERLYINGS, \"| band:\", STRIKES_BAND, \"| both sides:\", INCLUDE_BOTH)\n",
        "if logs: print(\"\\n\".join(logs))\n",
        "\n",
        "def bootstrap_historical(symboltoken):\n",

        "print(\"Preview:\", \", \".join(UNIVERSE[:12]))\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------- START LIVE WEBSOCKET --------\n",
        "AUTO_START_WS = True\n",
        "\n",
        "if AUTO_START_WS and JWT:\n",
        "    if \"ws_thread\" not in globals() or not ws_thread.is_alive():\n",
        "        ws_thread = threading.Thread(target=_start_ws, daemon=True)\n",
        "        ws_thread.start()\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"WebSocket not started\")\n",
        "def auto_rebuild_universe():\n",
        "\n",
        "    while True:\n",
        "\n",
        "        time.sleep(600)  # every 10 minutes\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            rebuild_universe([\"NIFTY\",\"SENSEX\"])\n",
        "            restart_ws()\n",
        "            print(\"🔁 Universe rebuilt (ATM shifted)\")\n",
        "        except Exception as e:\n",
        "            print(\"Universe rebuild error:\", e)\n",
        "\n",
        "threading.Thread(target=auto_rebuild_universe, daemon=True).start()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Resolver + Quote helpers (define only if missing in your env)\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Symbol resolver map for batch quotes\n",
        "_LOOK = {rec[\"symbol\"]: (rec[\"exchange\"], rec[\"symbol\"], rec[\"symbolToken\"]) for _, rec in DF_UNIVERSE.iterrows()}\n",
        "\n",
        "def resolve_symbol(sym: str):\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Index spot\n",
        "    if sym in INDEX_TOKENS:\n",
        "        return (\"NSE\", sym, INDEX_TOKENS[sym])\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Options universe\n",
        "    hit = _LOOK.get(sym)\n",
        "    if hit:\n",
        "        return hit\n",
        "\n",
        "    return None\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _quote_mode(mode: str, pairs):\n",
        "    # try to ensure login once if missing\n",
        "    global JWT\n",
        "    if not JWT:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            _ensure_login()\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "    if not JWT or not pairs:\n",
        "        return [], {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    by_ex = {}\n",
        "    for ex, tok in pairs:\n",
        "        by_ex.setdefault(ex, []).append(str(tok))\n",
        "    payload = {\"mode\": mode.upper(), \"exchangeTokens\": by_ex}\n",
        "    r = SESSION.post(f\"{BASE}/rest/secure/angelbroking/market/v1/quote/\",\n",
        "                     headers=_hdr(JWT), json=payload, timeout=25)\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        js = r.json()\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return [], {}\n",
        "    fetched = ((js or {}).get(\"data\") or {}).get(\"fetched\") or []\n",
        "    return fetched, js\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _triples(symbols):\n",
        "    out=[]\n",
        "    for s in symbols:\n",
        "        res = resolve_symbol(s)\n",
        "        if res:\n",
        "            out.append((s, res[0], res[2]))\n",
        "    return out\n",
        "\n",
        "# Only define these if you don't already have them\n",
        "if 'ltp_batch' not in globals():\n",
        "    def ltp_batch(symbols):\n",
        "        triples = _triples(symbols)\n",
        "        pairs   = [(ex, tok) for (_, ex, tok) in triples if ex and tok]\n",
        "        fetched, _ = _quote_mode(\"LTP\", pairs) if pairs else ([], {})\n",
        "        tok2ltp = {str(r.get(\"symbolToken\")): r.get(\"ltp\") for r in fetched}\n",
        "        rows=[]\n",
        "        for s, ex, tok in triples:\n",
        "            rows.append({\"symbol\": s, \"exchange\": ex, \"symbolToken\": tok, \"ltp\": tok2ltp.get(str(tok)) if tok else None})\n",
        "        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n",
        "        if not df.empty and \"symbol\" in df.columns:\n",
        "            df = df.set_index(\"symbol\")\n",
        "        return df\n",
        "\n",
        "if 'ohlc_batch' not in globals():\n",
        "    def ohlc_batch(symbols):\n",
        "        triples = _triples(symbols)\n",
        "        pairs   = [(ex, tok) for (_, ex, tok) in triples if ex and tok]\n",
        "        fetched, _ = _quote_mode(\"OHLC\", pairs) if pairs else ([], {})\n",
        "        tok2 = {str(r.get(\"symbolToken\")): r for r in fetched}\n",
        "        rows=[]\n",
        "        for s, ex, tok in triples:\n",
        "            r = tok2.get(str(tok), {}) if tok else {}\n",
        "            rows.append({\"symbol\": s, \"exchange\": ex, \"symbolToken\": tok,\n",
        "                         \"open\": r.get(\"open\"), \"high\": r.get(\"high\"),\n",
        "                         \"low\": r.get(\"low\"), \"close\": r.get(\"close\"), \"ltp\": r.get(\"ltp\")})\n",
        "        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n",
        "        if not df.empty and \"symbol\" in df.columns:\n",
        "            df = df.set_index(\"symbol\")\n",
        "        return df\n",
        "\n",
        " # ---------- DB-backed snapshot for /pos and /status (safe in DRY + after restart) ----------\n",
        "# Place this right after ohlc_batch and before the Candles section in Step 3.\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _s3_guess_init_budget(default=10000.0):\n",
        "    # 1) today\\u2019s persisted value (what /budget set)\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        v = _meta_get(_today_key(\"budget_init\"), None)\n",
        "        if v is not None: return float(v)\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 2) legacy fallbacks...\n",
        "    import os\n",
        "    def _as_float_safe(x):\n",
        "        try: return float(x() if callable(x) else x)\n",
        "        except Exception: return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    eng = globals().get(\"engine\")\n",
        "    candidates = [\n",
        "        _as_float_safe(os.environ.get(\"AI_START_BUDGET\")),\n",
        "        _as_float_safe(globals().get(\"AI_START_BUDGET\")),\n",
        "        _as_float_safe(getattr(eng, \"_starting_capital\", None) if eng else None),\n",
        "        _as_float_safe(getattr(eng, \"_remaining_capital\", None) if eng else None),\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "    return next((x for x in candidates if isinstance(x,(int,float)) and x and x>0), float(default))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Drive-only resolver (already in your file; keep it above the helpers) ---\n",
        "def _db_path() -> str:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        return getattr(engine.db, \"path\")\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return AI_DB_PATH  # set earlier to /gdrive/MyDrive/trading/ai_trades.sqlite\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- S3 helpers: always use the one true DB path on Drive (no local fallback) ---\n",
        "def _s3_db_open_rows(db_path: str | None = None):\n",
        "    import sqlite3\n",
        "    db_path = db_path or _db_path()\n",
        "    if not str(db_path).startswith(\"/gdrive/\"):\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(f\"Refusing to use non-Drive DB: {db_path}\")\n",
        "    con = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)\n",
        "    cur = con.cursor()\n",
        "    cur.execute(\"SELECT id, symbol, qty, entry_price FROM trades WHERE status='OPEN' ORDER BY id\")\n",
        "    rows = [{\"id\": i, \"symbol\": s, \"qty\": int(q), \"entry\": float(ep)} for (i, s, q, ep) in cur.fetchall()]\n",
        "    con.close()\n",
        "    return rows\n",
        "\n",
        "def _s3_db_realized_today(db_path: str | None = None):\n",
        "    import sqlite3\n",
        "    db_path = db_path or _db_path()\n",
        "    if not str(db_path).startswith(\"/gdrive/\"):\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(f\"Refusing to use non-Drive DB: {db_path}\")\n",
        "    today_ist = dt.datetime.now(IST).date().isoformat()\n",
        "    con = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)\n",
        "    cur = con.cursor()\n",
        "    cur.execute(\n",
        "        \"SELECT COALESCE(SUM(pnl),0) FROM trades WHERE status!='OPEN' AND ts LIKE ?||'%'\",\n",
        "        (today_ist,)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    v = cur.fetchone()[0] or 0.0\n",
        "    con.close()\n",
        "    return float(v)\n",
        "\n",
        "def acct_snapshot_from_db_s3(init_budget: float | None = None, db_path: str | None = None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Returns: dict {used, realized, remaining, pos_count, lines}\n",
        "      used      : capital locked by OPEN rows (entry * qty * lot)\n",
        "      realized  : today's realized P&L from CLOSED rows\n",
        "      remaining : init_budget - used + realized\n",
        "      lines     : pretty strings for each open position (with live LTP-based P/L)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    db_path = db_path or _db_path()\n",
        "    if not str(db_path).startswith(\"/gdrive/\"):\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(f\"Refusing to use non-Drive DB: {db_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    init_budget = _s3_guess_init_budget() if init_budget is None else float(init_budget)\n",
        "    opens = _s3_db_open_rows(db_path)\n",
        "    pos_count = len(opens)\n",
        "    realized = _s3_db_realized_today(db_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if pos_count == 0:\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            \"used\": 0.0, \"realized\": realized,\n",
        "            \"remaining\": init_budget + realized, \"pos_count\": 0, \"lines\": []\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    # get LTPs once\n",
        "    syms = [r[\"symbol\"] for r in opens]\n",
        "    q = ltp_batch(syms) if syms else pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines, used = [], 0.0\n",
        "    for r in opens:\n",
        "        lot = lot_size_for(r[\"symbol\"])\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            ltp_val = q.loc[r[\"symbol\"], \"ltp\"] if r[\"symbol\"] in q.index else None\n",
        "            ltp = float(ltp_val) if ltp_val is not None else r[\"entry\"]\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            ltp = r[\"entry\"]\n",
        "        pnl  = (ltp - r[\"entry\"]) * (r[\"qty\"] * lot)\n",
        "        used += (r[\"entry\"] * r[\"qty\"] * lot)\n",
        "        lines.append(f\"{r['symbol']} qty(lots)={r['qty']} entry={r['entry']:.2f} ltp={ltp:.2f} P/L={pnl:.2f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    remaining = max(0.0, init_budget - used + realized)\n",
        "    return {\"used\": used, \"realized\": realized, \"remaining\": remaining,\n",
        "            \"pos_count\": pos_count, \"lines\": lines}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Convenience helpers you will call from Step 6 command handlers\n",
        "def send_pos_from_db_s3(init_budget: float=None):\n",
        "    snap = acct_snapshot_from_db_s3(init_budget)\n",
        "    txt = \"No positions.\" if snap[\"pos_count\"] == 0 else \"\\n\".join(snap[\"lines\"])\n",
        "    try: tg_send(txt)\n",
        "    except Exception: print(txt)\n",
        "\n",
        "def send_status_from_db_s3(init_budget: float=None, *,\n",
        "                           auto_on=True, ai_on=True, dry_on=True,\n",
        "                           live_poll_sec=2, universe_sz=0, style=\"SCALP\",\n",
        "                           profit_target=0.10, halt_mode=\"none\"):\n",
        "    snap = acct_snapshot_from_db_s3(init_budget)\n",
        "    msg = (\n",
        "        f\"Status | AUTO={'ON' if auto_on else 'OFF'} | AI={'ON' if ai_on else 'OFF'} | DRY={'ON' if dry_on else 'OFF'} \"\n",
        "        f\"| live={live_poll_sec}s | pos={snap['pos_count']} | universe={universe_sz} (NIFTY)\\n\"\n",
        "        f\"Budget: mode=STATIC | init \\u20b9{int(_s3_guess_init_budget())} | used \\u20b9{int(round(snap['used']))} \"\n",
        "        f\"| realized \\u20b9{int(round(snap['realized']))} | remaining \\u20b9{int(round(snap['remaining']))}\\n\"\n",
        "        f\"Profit target: {int(profit_target*100)}% | halt={halt_mode}\\n\"\n",
        "        f\"Style: {style}\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    try: tg_send(msg)\n",
        "    except Exception: print(msg)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------- Local candle access (NO SmartAPI polling) --------\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def last_candles(symbol: str, tf_min: int, count: int):\n",
        "\n",
        "    # --- HANDLE INDEX SPOT ---\n",
        "    if symbol in INDEX_TOKENS:\n",
        "        now = dt.datetime.now(IST)\n",
        "\n",
        "        last_fetch = INDEX_CANDLE_LAST_FETCH.get(symbol)\n",
        "        if last_fetch and (now - last_fetch).total_seconds() < INDEX_CACHE_TTL:\n",
        "            return INDEX_CANDLE_CACHE.get(symbol, [])\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not smart:\n",
        "            return []\n",
        "\n",
        "        token = INDEX_TOKENS[symbol]\n",
        "\n",
        "        interval_map = {\n",
        "            1: \"ONE_MINUTE\",\n",
        "            5: \"FIVE_MINUTE\",\n",
        "            15: \"FIFTEEN_MINUTE\"\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        interval = interval_map.get(tf_min, \"ONE_MINUTE\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"⛔ Index historical disabled (rate limit protection)\")\n",
        "        return INDEX_CANDLE_CACHE.get(symbol, [])\n",
        "\n",
        "    res = resolve_symbol(symbol)\n",
        "    if not res:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    _, _, token = res\n",
        "\n",
        "    with DATA_LOCK:\n",
        "        df = SYMBOL_DATA.get(token)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if df is None or df.empty:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "    # --- OPTIONS (existing logic) ---\n",
        "    res = resolve_symbol(symbol)\n",
        "    if not res:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    _, _, token = res\n",
        "\n",
        "    df = None\n",
        "    with DATA_LOCK:\n",
        "        df = SYMBOL_DATA.get(token)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if df is None:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    if df.empty:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(df) < 3:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    df = df.copy()\n",
        "    df[\"time\"] = pd.to_datetime(df[\"time\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    if tf_min == 1:\n",
        "        use_df = df\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        use_df = df.resample(f\"{tf_min}min\", on=\"time\").agg({\n",
        "            \"open\": \"first\",\n",
        "            \"high\": \"max\",\n",
        "            \"low\": \"min\",\n",
        "            \"close\": \"last\"\n",
        "        }).dropna()\n",
        "\n",
        "    rows = use_df.tail(count)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return [\n",
        "        {\n",
        "            \"time\": row.name,\n",
        "            \"open\": row.open,\n",
        "            \"high\": row.high,\n",
        "            \"low\": row.low,\n",
        "            \"close\": row.close,\n",
        "            \"volume\": 0\n",
        "        }\n",
        "        for _, row in rows.iterrows()\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Quick self-test (read-only)\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "if len(UNIVERSE):\n",
        "    sample = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    sample += [s for s in UNIVERSE if \"NIFTY\" in s][:4]\n",
        "    sample += [s for s in UNIVERSE if \"SENSEX\" in s][:4]\n",
        "    print(\"\\n[LTP sample]\")\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        print(ltp_batch(sample).to_string(index=False))\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(\"LTP sample error:\", e)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n[OHLC sample]\")\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        print(ohlc_batch(sample).to_string(index=False))\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(\"OHLC sample error:\", e)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        cc = last_candles(sample[0], 15, 2)\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n[Candles {sample[0]} 15m last 2]\")\n",
        "        for r in cc: print(r)\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(\"Candles error:\", e)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n\\u2705 Consolidated universe build OK if UNIVERSE > 0 and samples print without errors.\")\n",
        "print(\"Norm size:\", len(_norm))\n",
        "print(_norm[[\"symbol\",\"lotSize\"]].head())"
      ]

        "@dataclass\n",

        "    round_qty_to_lot: bool = True\n",

        "class Callbacks:\n",
        "    get_now_ist: Callable[[], datetime]\n",
        "    get_universe: Callable[[], List[str]]\n",
        "    get_spot: Callable[[str], float]  # (symbol) -> float spot/ltp; kept for API parity\n",
        "    get_ltp_batch: Callable[[List[str]], pd.DataFrame]   # index=symbol, col ['ltp']\n",
        "    get_ohlc_batch: Callable[[List[str]], pd.DataFrame]  # index=symbol, cols ['o','h','l','c']\n",
        "    get_candles: Callable[[str, int, int], List[Dict[str, Any]]]  # (symbol, tf_min, count)\n",
        "    place_order: Callable[[str, str, int], Dict[str, Any]]\n",
        "    flatten_all: Callable[[str], Any]\n",
        "    notify: Callable[[str], Any]\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------------- Minimal DB for logs & PnL ----------------\n",
        "class DB:\n",
        "    def __init__(self, path: str):\n",
        "        self.path = path\n",
        "        self._init()\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _conn(self):\n",
        "        c = sqlite3.connect(self.path, timeout=5.0, isolation_level=None)\n",
        "        c.execute(\"PRAGMA journal_mode=DELETE;\")\n",
        "        c.execute(\"PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL;\")\n",
        "        return c\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _init(self):\n",
        "        import os, sqlite3\n",
        "        d = os.path.dirname(self.path)\n",
        "        if d:\n",
        "            os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ---------- 1) Open ONE connection/cursor and keep it alive until the end\n",
        "        con = sqlite3.connect(self.path, timeout=5.0)\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            cur = con.cursor()\n",
        "\n",
        "            # Set PRAGMAs OUTSIDE of executescript (avoids oddities)\n",
        "            cur.execute(\"PRAGMA journal_mode=DELETE;\")\n",
        "            cur.execute(\"PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL;\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            # ---------- 2) Core schema (executescript OK here, no PRAGMAs in it)\n",
        "            core_sql = \"\"\"\n",
        "    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trades (\n",
        "        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "        ts TEXT, symbol TEXT, side TEXT, qty INTEGER,\n",
        "        entry_price REAL, sl REAL, tp REAL,\n",
        "        strategy TEXT, confidence REAL, status TEXT,\n",
        "        exit_price REAL, pnl REAL, reason TEXT\n",
        "    );\n",
        "\n",
        "    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS decisions (\n",
        "        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "        ts TEXT, reason TEXT, details TEXT\n",
        "    );\n",
        "\n",
        "    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta (\n",
        "        key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n",
        "        value TEXT\n",
        "    );\n",
        "\n",
        "    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trades_audit (\n",
        "        audit_id     INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "        at_utc       TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "        ctx          TEXT,\n",
        "        op           TEXT NOT NULL,     -- INSERT | UPDATE | DELETE\n",
        "        trade_id     INTEGER,\n",
        "        ts_old       TEXT, ts_new       TEXT,\n",
        "        symbol_old   TEXT, symbol_new   TEXT,\n",
        "        side_old     TEXT, side_new     TEXT,\n",
        "        qty_old      INTEGER, qty_new   INTEGER,\n",
        "        entry_old    REAL,   entry_new  REAL,\n",
        "        exit_old     REAL,   exit_new   REAL,\n",
        "        pnl_old      REAL,   pnl_new    REAL,\n",
        "        status_old   TEXT,   status_new TEXT,\n",
        "        reason_old   TEXT,   reason_new TEXT\n",
        "    );\n",
        "    -- Backtest OHLC storage (idempotent)\n",
        "    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS backtest_ohlc (\n",
        "        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "        symbol TEXT NOT NULL,\n",
        "        expiry TEXT NOT NULL,           -- store as TEXT to ensure consistent date format\n",
        "        strike REAL,\n",
        "        option_type TEXT,               -- 'CE' / 'PE'\n",
        "        date TEXT NOT NULL,             -- trading day (YYYY-MM-DD)\n",
        "        time TEXT,                      -- candle time (HH:MM:SS)\n",
        "        open REAL,\n",
        "        high REAL,\n",
        "        low REAL,\n",
        "        close REAL,\n",
        "        volume INTEGER,\n",
        "        open_interest INTEGER DEFAULT 0,\n",
        "        timeframe TEXT NOT NULL,        -- '1m', '5m', '15m', '1d', etc.\n",
        "        UNIQUE(symbol, expiry, strike, option_type, date, timeframe)\n",
        "    );\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    -- Helpful indexes (safe if run multiple times)\n",
        "    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_backtest_date\n",
        "        ON backtest_ohlc(date, timeframe);\n",
        "\n",
        "    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_backtest_sym\n",
        "        ON backtest_ohlc(symbol, expiry, strike, option_type, timeframe);\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trades_audit_trade_id ON trades_audit(trade_id);\n",
        "    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trades_audit_at_utc   ON trades_audit(at_utc);\n",
        "    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trades_audit_op       ON trades_audit(op);\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "            cur.executescript(core_sql)\n",
        "\n",
        "            # ---------- 3) Build trigger bodies as *single statements* (no executescript)\n",
        "            _ctx_sql = \"(SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key='audit_ctx' LIMIT 1)\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            trg_ins = f\"\"\"\n",
        "    CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS trg_trades_ins\n",
        "    AFTER INSERT ON trades\n",
        "    BEGIN\n",
        "      INSERT INTO trades_audit(\n",
        "        at_utc, ctx, op, trade_id,\n",
        "        ts_old, ts_new,\n",
        "        symbol_old, symbol_new,\n",
        "        side_old, side_new,\n",
        "        qty_old, qty_new,\n",
        "        entry_old, entry_new,\n",
        "        exit_old, exit_new,\n",
        "        pnl_old, pnl_new,\n",
        "        status_old, status_new,\n",
        "        reason_old, reason_new\n",
        "      )\n",
        "      VALUES(\n",
        "        strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%f','now','utc'),\n",
        "        {_ctx_sql}, 'INSERT', NEW.id,\n",
        "        NULL, NEW.ts,\n",
        "        NULL, NEW.symbol,\n",
        "        NULL, NEW.side,\n",
        "        NULL, NEW.qty,\n",
        "        NULL, NEW.entry_price,\n",
        "        NULL, NEW.exit_price,\n",
        "        NULL, NEW.pnl,\n",
        "        NULL, NEW.status,\n",
        "        NULL, NEW.reason\n",
        "      );\n",
        "    END;\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "            trg_upd = f\"\"\"\n",
        "    CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS trg_trades_upd\n",
        "    AFTER UPDATE ON trades\n",
        "    BEGIN\n",
        "      INSERT INTO trades_audit(\n",
        "        at_utc, ctx, op, trade_id,\n",
        "        ts_old, ts_new,\n",
        "        symbol_old, symbol_new,\n",
        "        side_old, side_new,\n",
        "        qty_old, qty_new,\n",
        "        entry_old, entry_new,\n",
        "        exit_old, exit_new,\n",
        "        pnl_old, pnl_new,\n",
        "        status_old, status_new,\n",
        "        reason_old, reason_new\n",
        "      )\n",
        "      VALUES(\n",
        "        strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%f','now','utc'),\n",
        "        {_ctx_sql}, 'UPDATE', NEW.id,\n",
        "        OLD.ts, NEW.ts,\n",
        "        OLD.symbol, NEW.symbol,\n",
        "        OLD.side, NEW.side,\n",
        "        OLD.qty, NEW.qty,\n",
        "        OLD.entry_price, NEW.entry_price,\n",
        "        OLD.exit_price, NEW.exit_price,\n",
        "        OLD.pnl, NEW.pnl,\n",
        "        OLD.status, NEW.status,\n",
        "        OLD.reason, NEW.reason\n",
        "      );\n",
        "    END;\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "            trg_del = f\"\"\"\n",
        "    CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS trg_trades_del\n",
        "    AFTER DELETE ON trades\n",
        "    BEGIN\n",
        "      INSERT INTO trades_audit(\n",
        "        at_utc, ctx, op, trade_id,\n",
        "        ts_old, ts_new,\n",
        "        symbol_old, symbol_new,\n",
        "        side_old, side_new,\n",
        "        qty_old, qty_new,\n",
        "        entry_old, entry_new,\n",
        "        exit_old, exit_new,\n",
        "        pnl_old, pnl_new,\n",
        "        status_old, status_new,\n",
        "        reason_old, reason_new\n",
        "      )\n",
        "      VALUES(\n",
        "        strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%f','now','utc'),\n",
        "        {_ctx_sql}, 'DELETE', OLD.id,\n",
        "        OLD.ts, NULL,\n",
        "        OLD.symbol, NULL,\n",
        "        OLD.side, NULL,\n",
        "        OLD.qty, NULL,\n",
        "        OLD.entry_price, NULL,\n",
        "        OLD.exit_price, NULL,\n",
        "        OLD.pnl, NULL,\n",
        "        OLD.status, NULL,\n",
        "        OLD.reason, NULL\n",
        "      );\n",
        "    END;\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "            # Execute each trigger separately (keeps cursor state clean)\n",
        "            cur.execute(trg_ins)\n",
        "            cur.execute(trg_upd)\n",
        "            cur.execute(trg_del)\n",
        "\n",
        "            con.commit()\n",
        "        finally:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                cur.close()\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "            con.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    def set_audit_context(self, **kwargs):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        Tag audit entries with a short context string, e.g:\n",
        "        engine.db.set_audit_context(user='bot', source='AI', cmd='/perf yesterday')\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        s = \" | \".join(f\"{k}={v}\" for k, v in kwargs.items())\n",
        "        con = sqlite3.connect(self.path)\n",
        "        con.execute(\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT)\")\n",
        "        con.execute(\"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO meta(key,value) VALUES('audit_ctx', ?)\", (s,))\n",
        "        con.commit(); con.close()\n",
        "        return s\n",
        "\n",
        "    def audit_tail(self, limit=20):\n",
        "        \"\"\"Quick peek at most recent audit rows.\"\"\"\n",
        "        con = self._conn(); con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "        rows = con.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT audit_id, at_utc, ctx, op, trade_id,\n",
        "                   ts_old, ts_new, symbol_old, symbol_new,\n",
        "                   status_old, status_new, pnl_old, pnl_new\n",
        "            FROM trades_audit\n",
        "            ORDER BY audit_id DESC\n",
        "            LIMIT ?\n",
        "        \"\"\", (int(limit),)).fetchall()\n",
        "        con.close()\n",
        "        return [dict(r) for r in rows]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    def insert_trade(self, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> int:\n",
        "        with DB_LOCK:\n",
        "            c = self._conn(); cur = c.cursor()\n",
        "            cur.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                INSERT INTO trades (ts, symbol, side, qty, entry_price, sl, tp, strategy, confidence, status, reason)\n",
        "                VALUES (:ts,:symbol,:side,:qty,:entry_price,:sl,:tp,:strategy,:confidence,:status,:reason)\n",
        "            \"\"\", row)\n",
        "            tid = cur.lastrowid\n",
        "            c.commit(); c.close()\n",
        "            return tid\n",
        "\n",
        "    def update_trade_exit(self, trade_id: int, exit_price: float, pnl: float, status: str):\n",
        "        with DB_LOCK:\n",
        "          c = self._conn(); cur = c.cursor()\n",
        "          cur.execute(\"UPDATE trades SET exit_price=?, pnl=?, status=? WHERE id=?\",\n",
        "                      (exit_price, pnl, status, trade_id))\n",
        "          c.commit(); c.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "    def insert_decision(self, reason: str, details: Dict[str, Any]):\n",
        "        with DB_LOCK:\n",
        "          c = self._conn(); cur = c.cursor()\n",
        "          cur.execute(\"INSERT INTO decisions (ts, reason, details) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\",\n",
        "                      (datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), reason, json.dumps(details)[:4000]))\n",
        "          c.commit(); c.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "    def today_pnl(self) -> float:\n",
        "        c = self._conn(); cur = c.cursor()\n",
        "        today = datetime.now(timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))).date().isoformat()\n",
        "        cur.execute(\"SELECT COALESCE(SUM(pnl),0) FROM trades WHERE status!='OPEN' AND ts LIKE ?||'%'\", (today,))\n",
        "        v = cur.fetchone()[0] or 0.0\n",
        "        c.close(); return float(v)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def fetch_history(self, n=10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n",
        "        c = self._conn(); cur = c.cursor()\n",
        "        cur.execute(\"\"\"SELECT id, ts, symbol, side, qty, entry_price, sl, tp, strategy,\n",
        "                              confidence, status, exit_price, pnl, reason\n",
        "                       FROM trades ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?\"\"\", (n,))\n",
        "        rows = cur.fetchall(); c.close()\n",
        "        cols = [\"id\",\"ts\",\"symbol\",\"side\",\"qty\",\"entry_price\",\"sl\",\"tp\",\"strategy\",\"confidence\",\n",
        "                \"status\",\"exit_price\",\"pnl\",\"reason\"]\n",
        "        return [dict(zip(cols, r)) for r in rows]\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------------- Simple indicators ----------------\n",
        "def atr(high: pd.Series, low: pd.Series, close: pd.Series, n: int = 14):\n",
        "    tr1 = high - low\n",
        "    tr2 = (high - close.shift()).abs()\n",
        "    tr3 = (low - close.shift()).abs()\n",
        "    tr = pd.concat([tr1, tr2, tr3], axis=1).max(axis=1)\n",
        "    return tr.rolling(n).mean()\n",
        "\n",
        "def adx(high: pd.Series, low: pd.Series, close: pd.Series, n: int = 14):\n",
        "    plus_dm = high.diff()\n",
        "    minus_dm = low.diff().abs()\n",
        "\n",
        "    plus_dm = plus_dm.where((plus_dm > minus_dm) & (plus_dm > 0), 0.0)\n",
        "    minus_dm = minus_dm.where((minus_dm > plus_dm) & (minus_dm > 0), 0.0)\n",
        "\n",
        "    tr = atr(high, low, close, n)\n",
        "    plus_di = 100 * (plus_dm.rolling(n).sum() / tr)\n",
        "    minus_di = 100 * (minus_dm.rolling(n).sum() / tr)\n",
        "    dx = (abs(plus_di - minus_di) / (plus_di + minus_di)) * 100\n",
        "    return dx.rolling(n).mean()\n",
        "\n",
        "def ema(s: pd.Series, n: int) -> pd.Series:\n",
        "    return s.ewm(span=n, adjust=False).mean()\n",
        "\n",
        "def rsi(close: pd.Series, n: int = 14) -> pd.Series:\n",
        "    d = close.diff()\n",
        "    up = (d.where(d > 0, 0.0)).rolling(n).mean()\n",
        "    dn = (-d.where(d < 0, 0.0)).rolling(n).mean()\n",
        "    rs = up / (dn + 1e-9)\n",
        "    return 100 - (100 / (1 + rs))\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------------- Trade idea & selector ----------------\n",
        "@dataclass\n",
        "class TradeIdea:\n",
        "    symbol: str\n",
        "    side: str\n",
        "    qty: int\n",
        "    strategy: str\n",
        "    confidence: float\n",
        "    sl: float\n",
        "    tp: float\n",
        "    reason: str\n",
        "\n",
        "CANDLE_CACHE = {}\n",
        "CANDLE_CACHE_TIME = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "def cached_candles(cb, symbol, tf, count):\n",
        "\n",
        "    now = time.time()\n",
        "\n",
        "    key = f\"{symbol}_{tf}_{count}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if key in CANDLE_CACHE:\n",
        "        if now - CANDLE_CACHE_TIME[key] < 15:\n",
        "            return CANDLE_CACHE[key]\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        candles = cb.get_candles(symbol, tf, count)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if candles:\n",
        "            CANDLE_CACHE[key] = candles\n",
        "            CANDLE_CACHE_TIME[key] = now\n",
        "\n",
        "        return candles\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return CANDLE_CACHE.get(key, [])\n",
        "\n",
        "def index_momentum_signal(cb, index_symbol):\n",
        "\n",
        "    candles = cached_candles(cb, index_symbol, 1, 10)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not candles or len(candles) < 5:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    df = pd.DataFrame(candles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    close = pd.to_numeric(df[\"close\"], errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    ema_fast = close.ewm(span=5).mean().iloc[-1]\n",
        "    ema_slow = close.ewm(span=20).mean().iloc[-1]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if ema_fast > ema_slow:\n",
        "        return \"BULLISH\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if ema_fast < ema_slow:\n",
        "        return \"BEARISH\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return None\n",
        "\n",
        "class StrategySelector:\n",
        "    def __init__(self, cb: Callbacks, cfg: AIConfig):\n",
        "        self.cb, self.cfg = cb, cfg\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    def choose(self) -> Tuple[Optional[TradeIdea], Dict[str, Any]]:\n",
        "        trend = index_momentum_signal(self.cb, \"NIFTY\")\n",
        "        uni = self.cb.get_universe() or []\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not uni:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"No universe\", \"details\": {}}\n",
        "        universe = uni\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            ohlc = self.cb.get_ohlc_batch(uni)\n",
        "            ltp  = self.cb.get_ltp_batch(uni)\n",
        "        except Exception as e:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"Data fetch error\", \"details\": {\"error\": str(e)}}\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ---- normalize shapes/column names ----\n",
        "        if ohlc is None or ltp is None:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"No valid prices\", \"details\": {}}\n",
        "\n",
        "        # If providers return columns like open/high/low/close, rename to o/h/l/c\n",
        "        col_map = {\"open\": \"o\", \"high\": \"h\", \"low\": \"l\", \"close\": \"c\", \"Open\": \"o\", \"High\": \"h\", \"Low\": \"l\", \"Close\": \"c\"}\n",
        "        ohlc = ohlc.rename(columns={k: v for k, v in col_map.items() if k in ohlc.columns})\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Ensure index is symbol (if symbol column present)\n",
        "        if \"symbol\" in ohlc.columns:\n",
        "            ohlc = ohlc.set_index(\"symbol\")\n",
        "        if \"symbol\" in ltp.columns:\n",
        "            ltp = ltp.set_index(\"symbol\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Keep only expected columns\n",
        "        need_ohlc = [c for c in [\"o\", \"h\", \"l\", \"c\"] if c in ohlc.columns]\n",
        "        if len(need_ohlc) < 2:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"OHLC columns missing\", \"details\": {\"cols\": list(ohlc.columns)}}\n",
        "\n",
        "        # coerce numerics & drop NaNs\n",
        "        for c in need_ohlc:\n",
        "            ohlc[c] = pd.to_numeric(ohlc[c], errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "        if \"ltp\" not in ltp.columns:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"LTP column missing\", \"details\": {\"cols\": list(ltp.columns)}}\n",
        "\n",
        "        ltp[\"ltp\"] = pd.to_numeric(ltp[\"ltp\"], errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        ohlc = ohlc.dropna(subset=[\"o\", \"c\"], how=\"any\")\n",
        "        ltp  = ltp.dropna(subset=[\"ltp\"], how=\"any\")\n",
        "        if ohlc.empty or ltp.empty:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"No valid prices\", \"details\": {\"ohlc_rows\": len(ohlc), \"ltp_rows\": len(ltp)}}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Keep only needed columns\n",
        "        ohlc = ohlc[[c for c in [\"o\", \"h\", \"l\", \"c\"] if c in ohlc.columns]]\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Normalize LTP\n",
        "        if \"ltp\" not in ltp.columns:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"LTP column missing\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "        ltp = ltp[[\"ltp\"]]\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Merge safely\n",
        "        merged = ohlc.join(ltp, how=\"inner\")\n",
        "        if merged.empty:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"No intersection between OHLC and LTP\", \"details\": {}}\n",
        "\n",
        "        # compute change safely\n",
        "        denom = merged[\"o\"].replace(0, np.nan)\n",
        "        merged[\"chg\"] = (merged[\"c\"] - merged[\"o\"]) / denom\n",
        "        merged = merged.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).dropna(subset=[\"chg\"])\n",
        "        if merged.empty:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"No merged data\", \"details\": {}}\n",
        "\n",
        "        winners = merged.sort_values(\"chg\", ascending=False).head(20)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # pick first candidate and compute a light momentum/RSI score on 5m candles\n",
        "        sample = winners.index[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ---------- PCR Market Bias Filter ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "        market_bias = None\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            pcr_resp = SESSION.get(\n",
        "                f\"{BASE}/rest/secure/angelbroking/marketData/v1/putCallRatio\",\n",
        "                headers=_hdr(JWT),\n",
        "                timeout=5\n",
        "            ).json()\n",
        "\n",
        "            for row in pcr_resp.get(\"data\", []):\n",
        "                if \"NIFTY\" in row.get(\"tradingSymbol\", \"\"):\n",
        "                    pcr = float(row.get(\"pcr\", 1))\n",
        "\n",
        "                    if pcr > 1.1:\n",
        "                        market_bias = \"BULLISH\"\n",
        "                    elif pcr < 0.8:\n",
        "                        market_bias = \"BEARISH\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ---- Greeks filter ----\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            greeks = SESSION.post(\n",
        "                f\"{BASE}/rest/secure/angelbroking/marketData/v1/optionGreek\",\n",
        "                headers=_hdr(JWT),\n",
        "                json={\"name\": \"NIFTY\", \"expirydate\": \"\"},\n",
        "                timeout=10\n",
        "            ).json()\n",
        "\n",
        "            for g in greeks.get(\"data\", []):\n",
        "                if g.get(\"strikePrice\") in sample:\n",
        "                    delta = float(g.get(\"delta\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "                    if delta < 0.30 or delta > 0.70:\n",
        "                        return None, {\"reason\": \"Bad delta filter\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            candles = self.cb.get_candles(sample, 1, 50)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not candles or len(candles) < 15:\n",
        "                return None, {\"reason\": \"Insufficient candles\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "            df = pd.DataFrame(candles)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if df.empty or \"close\" not in df:\n",
        "                raise ValueError(\"No candle data\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            close = pd.to_numeric(df[\"close\"], errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            ema_fast = ema(close, 5).iloc[-1]\n",
        "            ema_mid  = ema(close, 9).iloc[-1]\n",
        "            ema_slow = ema(close, 21).iloc[-1]\n",
        "\n",
        "            mom_bias = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "            if ema_fast > ema_mid > ema_slow:\n",
        "                mom_bias = 1\n",
        "            elif ema_fast < ema_mid < ema_slow:\n",
        "                mom_bias = -1\n",
        "            rsi_now = float(rsi(close, 14).iloc[-1])\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            mom_bias, rsi_now = np.sign(winners[\"chg\"].mean()), 50.0\n",
        "\n",
        "            confidence = 0.5\n",
        "\n",
        "            # momentum strength\n",
        "            if abs(mom_bias) == 1:\n",
        "                confidence += 0.2\n",
        "\n",
        "            # RSI momentum\n",
        "            if 55 < rsi_now < 70:\n",
        "                confidence += 0.15\n",
        "\n",
        "            # strong breakout\n",
        "            # strong breakout\n",
        "            if \"close\" in locals() and len(close) >= 3:\n",
        "                last_move = abs(close.iloc[-1] - close.iloc[-3]) / close.iloc[-3]\n",
        "                if last_move > 0.002:\n",
        "                    confidence += 0.15\n",
        "\n",
        "            confidence = min(confidence, 0.95)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "        if trend == \"BULLISH\":\n",
        "            prefer_call = True\n",
        "        elif trend == \"BEARISH\":\n",
        "            prefer_call = False\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            prefer_call = mom_bias >= 0\n",
        "        picks = [s for s in winners.index if (\"CE\" in s if prefer_call else \"PE\" in s)]\n",
        "        if not picks:\n",
        "            picks = [s for s in winners.index if (\"PE\" in s if prefer_call else \"CE\" in s)]\n",
        "        if not picks:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"No CE/PE candidates\", \"details\": {\"prefer_call\": bool(prefer_call)}}\n",
        "\n",
        "        sym = picks[0]\n",
        "        # Reject trades against PCR bias\n",
        "        if market_bias == \"BULLISH\" and \"PE\" in sym:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"PCR bullish - avoiding PE\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "        if market_bias == \"BEARISH\" and \"CE\" in sym:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"PCR bearish - avoiding CE\"}\n",
        "        last = float(ltp.loc[sym, \"ltp\"])\n",
        "        # Avoid extremely cheap or illiquid options\n",
        "        if last < 20:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"Option price too low\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Decide side based on option type\n",
        "        side = \"BUY\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        # If we prefer puts (bearish momentum), ensure we are buying PE\n",
        "        if not prefer_call and \"PE\" in sym:\n",
        "            side = \"BUY\"\n",
        "        elif prefer_call and \"CE\" in sym:\n",
        "            side = \"BUY\"\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"Option type mismatch with bias\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "        # confidence blends momentum and absolute change\n",
        "        rsi_score = 1.0 - abs(rsi_now - (65.0 if prefer_call else 35.0)) / 65.0\n",
        "        mom_score = float(min(max(abs(winners[\"chg\"].mean()) / 0.01, 0.0), 1.0))\n",
        "        regime = getattr(self.cb, \"current_regime\", \"UNKNOWN\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        base_conf = 0.5 * rsi_score + 0.5 * mom_score\n",
        "\n",
        "        if regime == \"STRONG_TREND\":\n",
        "            conf = min(1.0, base_conf + 0.1)\n",
        "        elif regime == \"RANGE\":\n",
        "            conf = base_conf * 0.8\n",
        "        elif regime == \"VOL_SHOCK\":\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"Vol shock - blocked\"}\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            conf = base_conf\n",
        "\n",
        "        conf = float(np.clip(conf, 0.0, 1.0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        # simple SL/TP bands on option price\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            candles = self.cb.get_candles(sym, 5, 50)\n",
        "            df2 = pd.DataFrame(candles)\n",
        "            high = pd.to_numeric(df2[\"high\"])\n",
        "            low  = pd.to_numeric(df2[\"low\"])\n",
        "            close = pd.to_numeric(df2[\"close\"])\n",
        "            atr_val = atr(high, low, close).iloc[-1]\n",
        "            atr_val = min(atr_val, last * 0.10)   # cap SL to 10% premium\n",
        "            # --- volatility compression filter (avoid sideways markets) ---\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                atr_mean = atr(high, low, close).rolling(20).mean().iloc[-1]\n",
        "                atr_ratio = atr_val / (atr_mean + 1e-9)\n",
        "\n",
        "                if atr_ratio < 0.6:\n",
