        "                    return None, {\"reason\": \"Low volatility compression\"}\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            atr_val = last * 0.02\n",
        "        sl = round(last - atr_val*1.2, 2)\n",
        "        tp = round(last + atr_val*2.0, 2)\n",
        "        qty = lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "\n",
        "        idea = TradeIdea(\n",
        "            symbol=sym,\n",
        "            side=side,\n",
        "            qty=qty,\n",
        "            strategy=\"TF_Momentum\",\n",
        "            confidence=confidence,\n",
        "            sl=sl,\n",
        "            tp=tp,\n",
        "            reason=f\"bias={mom_bias}, rsi={round(rsi_now,1)}\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "        diag = {\"prefer_call\": bool(prefer_call), \"rsi\": rsi_now,\n",
        "                \"avg_chg\": float(winners[\"chg\"].mean()), \"selected\": sym,\n",
        "                \"confidence\": confidence}\n",
        "        if getattr(self.cfg, \"debug\", False):\n",
        "          print(\"\\n====== SELECTOR DEBUG ======\")\n",
        "          print(\"Prefer Call:\", prefer_call)\n",
        "          print(\"Selected Symbol:\", sym)\n",
        "          print(\"Side:\", side)\n",
        "          print(\"Confidence:\", conf)\n",
        "          print(\"Threshold:\", self.cfg.confidence_threshold)\n",
        "          print(\"Regime:\", regime)\n",
        "          print(\"============================\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "        if np.isnan(last) or np.isnan(sl) or np.isnan(tp):\n",
        "            return None, {\"reason\": \"NaN detected in trade parameters\"}\n",
        "        return idea, diag\n",
        "class RegimeEngine:\n",
        "    def __init__(self, cb):\n",
        "        self.cb = cb\n",
        "        self.last_regime = \"UNKNOWN\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    def detect(self):\n",
        "        symbol = \"NIFTY\"  # use spot index if available\n",
        "        candles = self.cb.get_candles(symbol, 15, 100)\n",
        "        if not candles:\n",
        "            return self.last_regime\n",
        "\n",
        "        df = pd.DataFrame(candles)\n",
        "        high = pd.to_numeric(df[\"high\"])\n",
        "        low = pd.to_numeric(df[\"low\"])\n",
        "        close = pd.to_numeric(df[\"close\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "        adx_val = adx(high, low, close).iloc[-1]\n",
        "        atr_series = atr(high, low, close)\n",
        "        atr_now = atr_series.iloc[-1]\n",
        "        atr_mean = atr_series.rolling(20).mean().iloc[-1]\n",
        "\n",
        "        atr_ratio = atr_now / (atr_mean + 1e-9)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Gap logic (correct)\n",
        "        prev_close = close.iloc[-2]\n",
        "        today_open = df[\"open\"].iloc[-1]\n",
        "        regime = \"NORMAL\"\n",
        "        if atr_ratio > 1.8:\n",
        "            regime = \"VOL_SHOCK\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        # smoothing: require same regime twice\n",
        "        if regime != self.last_regime:\n",
        "            self.last_regime = regime\n",
        "        return self.last_regime\n",
        "\n",
        "class TrendFuturesModule:\n",
        "    def __init__(self, cb, cfg):\n",
        "        self.cb = cb\n",
        "        self.cfg = cfg\n",
        "\n",
        "    def generate(self):\n",
        "        symbol = \"NIFTY\"\n",
        "        candles = self.cb.get_candles(symbol, 1, 120)\n",
        "        if not candles:\n",
        "            return None\n",
        "\n",
        "        df = pd.DataFrame(candles)\n",
        "        close = pd.to_numeric(df[\"close\"])\n",
        "        high = pd.to_numeric(df[\"high\"])\n",
        "        low = pd.to_numeric(df[\"low\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "        ema20 = ema(close, 20).iloc[-1]\n",
        "        ema50 = ema(close, 50).iloc[-1]\n",
        "\n",
        "        side = \"BUY\" if ema20 > ema50 else \"SELL\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        atr_val = atr(high, low, close).iloc[-1]\n",
        "        entry = close.iloc[-1]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if side == \"BUY\":\n",
        "            sl = entry - atr_val\n",
        "            tp = entry + (2 * atr_val)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            sl = entry + atr_val\n",
        "            tp = entry - (2 * atr_val)\n",
        "\n",
        "        atr_val = atr(high, low, close).iloc[-1]\n",
        "        entry = close.iloc[-1]\n",
        "        sl = entry - atr_val\n",
        "        tp = entry + (2 * atr_val)\n",
        "\n",
        "        universe = self.cb.get_universe() or []\n",
        "        print(\"DEBUG scan universe size:\", len(universe))\n",
        "        print(\"DEBUG first 10:\", universe[:10])\n",
        "        if not universe:\n",
        "            return None\n",
        "\n",
        "        symbol = universe[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        return TradeIdea(\n",
        "            symbol=symbol,\n",
        "            side=side,\n",
        "            qty=1,\n",
        "            strategy=\"TrendOptions\",\n",
        "            confidence=0.8,\n",
        "            sl=sl,\n",
        "            tp=tp,\n",
        "            reason=f\"EMA20 {'>' if side=='BUY' else '<'} EMA50 trend\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "class StrategyRouter:\n",
        "    def __init__(self, cb, cfg):\n",
        "        self.cb = cb\n",
        "        self.cfg = cfg\n",
        "        self.trend_fut = TrendFuturesModule(self.cb, self.cfg)\n",
        "        self.selector = StrategySelector(self.cb, self.cfg)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    def route(self, regime):\n",
        "        idea = None\n",
        "\n",
        "        if regime in [\"STRONG_TREND\", \"NORMAL_TREND\"]:\n",
        "            idea = self.trend_fut.generate()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not idea:\n",
        "            idea, _ = self.selector.choose()\n",
        "\n",
        "        return idea\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------------- AI Engine (thread-only) ----------------\n",
        "class AIEngine:\n",
        "    def __init__(self, callbacks: Callbacks, config: AIConfig):\n",
        "        self._traded_today = set()\n",
        "        self._positions = {}\n",
        "        self.cb, self.cfg = callbacks, config\n",
        "        self.db = DB(self.cfg.db_path)\n",
        "        self.regime_engine = RegimeEngine(self.cb)\n",
        "        self.router = StrategyRouter(self.cb, self.cfg)\n",
        "        self.round_qty_to_lot = getattr(self.cfg, \"round_qty_to_lot\", True)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "        self._ai_enabled = False\n",
        "        self._cooldown_until: Optional[datetime] = None\n",
        "        self._open: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n",
        "        self._last_decision: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n",
        "        self._lock = threading.RLock()\n",
        "\n",
        "        self._scan_interval = self.cfg.scan_interval_seconds\n",
        "        self._mon_interval  = self.cfg.monitor_interval_seconds\n",
        "        self._remaining_capital = 0.0\n",
        "        self._daily_loss_cap = self.cfg.daily_loss_limit_abs\n",
        "        self._trades_today = 0\n",
        "        self._threads_started = False\n",
        "        self._loss_tripped = False\n",
        "        self._resume_open_trades()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # --- knobs ---\n",
        "    def enable_ai(self, on: bool):                 self._ai_enabled = bool(on)\n",
        "    def set_scan_interval_seconds(self, n:int):    self._scan_interval = max(20, int(n))\n",
        "    def set_monitor_interval_seconds(self, n:int): self._mon_interval  = max(1, int(n))\n",
        "    def set_remaining_capital(self, amt: float):\n",
        "        self._remaining_capital = max(0.0, float(amt))\n",
        "        if not hasattr(self, \"_start_capital\") or not np.isfinite(getattr(self, \"_start_capital\", float(\"nan\"))) or getattr(self, \"_start_capital\", 0.0) <= 0:\n",
        "            self._start_capital = float(self._remaining_capital)\n",
        "    def set_daily_loss_cap(self, amt: float):      self._daily_loss_cap = max(0.0, float(amt))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # --- threads ---\n",
        "    def start_background_threads(self):\n",
        "        if self._threads_started: return\n",
        "        self._threads_started = True\n",
        "        if not hasattr(self, \"_scan_thread\"):\n",
        "            self._scan_thread = threading.Thread(\n",
        "                target=self._scan_loop,\n",
        "                daemon=True,\n",
        "                name=\"AI-Scan-Thread\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            self._scan_thread.start()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not hasattr(self, \"_monitor_thread\"):\n",
        "            self._monitor_thread = threading.Thread(\n",
        "                target=self._monitor_loop,\n",
        "                daemon=True,\n",
        "                name=\"AI-Mon-Thread\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            self._monitor_thread.start()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # --- loops ---\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _scan_loop(self):\n",
        "\n",
        "        # --- global scan lock to prevent duplicate engines scanning ---\n",
        "        global _GLOBAL_SCAN_LOCK\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            _GLOBAL_SCAN_LOCK\n",
        "        except NameError:\n",
        "            _GLOBAL_SCAN_LOCK = threading.Lock()\n",
        "\n",
        "        while True:\n",
        "\n",
        "            # ensure only one scan thread across all engines\n",
        "            if not _GLOBAL_SCAN_LOCK.acquire(blocking=False):\n",
        "                time.sleep(self._scan_interval)\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                # mark scan heartbeat BEFORE doing work\n",
        "                self._last_scan_time = time.time()\n",
        "\n",
        "                # ---- daily profit gate (stop/flat when target hit) ----\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    if 'profit_gate_tick' in globals() and callable(profit_gate_tick):\n",
        "                        if profit_gate_tick():\n",
        "                            time.sleep(2)\n",
        "                            continue\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    pass\n",
        "\n",
        "                # ---- prevent re-entrant scan ----\n",
        "                if getattr(self, \"_scan_running\", False):\n",
        "                    time.sleep(self._scan_interval)\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                self._scan_running = True\n",
        "\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    self._scan_once_guarded()\n",
        "                finally:\n",
        "                    self._scan_running = False\n",
        "\n",
        "            except Exception as e:\n",
        "                import traceback\n",
        "                print(\"SCAN LOOP ERROR:\", e)\n",
        "                traceback.print_exc()\n",
        "\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    self.cb.notify(f\"⚠️ AI scan thread error: {e}\")\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    pass\n",
        "\n",
        "                time.sleep(3)\n",
        "\n",
        "            finally:\n",
        "                # release global scan lock\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    _GLOBAL_SCAN_LOCK.release()\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    pass\n",
        "\n",
        "            time.sleep(self._scan_interval)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _monitor_loop(self):\n",
        "        print(\"👀 MONITOR LOOP TICK\", datetime.now())\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ---- initialize heartbeat tracking ----\n",
        "        self._last_monitor_beat = time.time()\n",
        "\n",
        "        while True:\n",
        "            loop_start = time.time()\n",
        "\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                # ---- broker exit sweeper ----\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    if 'check_exits_once' in globals() and callable(check_exits_once):\n",
        "                        check_exits_once()\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    pass\n",
        "\n",
        "                # ---- profit gate check ----\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    if 'profit_gate_tick' in globals() and callable(profit_gate_tick):\n",
        "                        if profit_gate_tick():\n",
        "                            time.sleep(2)\n",
        "                            continue\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    pass\n",
        "\n",
        "                # ---- core monitor logic ----\n",
        "                self._monitor_once_guarded()\n",
        "                # ---- broker vs local reconciliation ----\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    if 'reconcile_positions' in globals() and callable(reconcile_positions):\n",
        "                        reconcile_positions()\n",
        "                except Exception as e:\n",
        "                    try:\n",
        "                        self.cb.notify(f\"⚠️ Reconciliation error: {e}\")\n",
        "                    except Exception:\n",
        "                        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "            except Exception as e:\n",
        "                self.cb.notify(f\"⚠️ AI monitor error: {e}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            # ---- monitor heartbeat update ----\n",
        "            self._last_monitor_beat = time.time()\n",
        "\n",
        "            # ---- detect monitor delay ----\n",
        "            elapsed = self._last_monitor_beat - loop_start\n",
        "            if elapsed > (self._mon_interval * 3):\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    self.cb.notify(f\"⚠️ Monitor lag detected ({elapsed:.2f}s)\")\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    pass\n",
        "\n",
        "            # ---- detect scan thread stall ----\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                if hasattr(self, \"_last_scan_time\"):\n",
        "                    if time.time() - self._last_scan_time > 60:\n",
        "                        if not hasattr(self, \"_last_scan_warn\") or time.time() - self._last_scan_warn > 120:\n",
        "                            self.cb.notify(\"⚠️ Scan thread stalled (>60s no activity)\")\n",
        "                            self._last_scan_warn = time.time()\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "            time.sleep(self._mon_interval)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    # --- core scan ---\n",
        "    def _scan_once_guarded(self):\n",
        "        print(\"\\n=== SCAN DEBUG START ===\")\n",
        "        print(\"Function:\", \"_scan_once_guarded\")\n",
        "        print(\"Time:\", datetime.now())\n",
        "        print(\"========================\")\n",
        "        with self._lock:\n",
        "            last = None\n",
        "            print(\"THREAD ID:\", threading.get_ident())\n",
        "            print(\"OPEN POSITIONS:\", list(self._open.keys()))\n",
        "            print(\"TRADED TODAY:\", self._traded_today)\n",
        "            now = dt.datetime.now(IST)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if now.time() < dt.time(9,16):\n",
        "                return\n",
        "            if self._profit_gate_enforce():\n",
        "                return\n",
        "            now = self.cb.get_now_ist()\n",
        "            self._last_scan_time = time.time()\n",
        "            today = self.cb.get_now_ist().date()\n",
        "            if getattr(self, \"_trade_date\", None) != today:\n",
        "                self._trade_date = today\n",
        "                self._trades_today = 0\n",
        "                self._traded_today.clear()\n",
        "            # DEBUG BLOCK\n",
        "            if False:\n",
        "                print(\"---- ENGINE DEBUG ----\")\n",
        "                print(\"AI enabled:\", self._ai_enabled)\n",
        "                print(\"AUTO_MODE:\", globals().get(\"AUTO_MODE\"))\n",
        "                print(\"Open positions:\", len(self._open))\n",
        "                print(\"----------------------\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            # --- global gates ---\n",
        "            if not self._ai_enabled:\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\"AI disabled\", {})\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "            if self._cooldown_until and now < self._cooldown_until:\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\"Cooldown active\", {\"until\": self._cooldown_until.isoformat()})\n",
        "                return\n",
        "            print(\"Trades today:\", self._trades_today)\n",
        "            print(\"Max trades allowed:\", self.cfg.max_auto_trades_per_day)\n",
        "            print(\"Cooldown until:\", self._cooldown_until)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if self._trades_today >= self.cfg.max_auto_trades_per_day:\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\"Auto trade limit reached\", {\"max\": self.cfg.max_auto_trades_per_day})\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "            # --- daily loss cap (REALIZED P&L only; avoid false trips from large open P&L) ---\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    realized_today = float(self.db.today_pnl())\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    realized_today = 0.0\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    # fallback if helper exists\n",
        "                    realized_today = float(self._realized_today())\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    realized_today = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "            if self._daily_loss_cap > 0.0 and realized_today <= -abs(self._daily_loss_cap):\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\n",
        "                    \"Daily loss cap hit\",\n",
        "                    {\"cap\": self._daily_loss_cap, \"realized\": realized_today}\n",
        "                )\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    self.cb.notify(\n",
        "                        f\"⛔ Daily loss cap reached (REALIZED ₹{realized_today:.0f} ≤ -₹{abs(self._daily_loss_cap):.0f}) — flattening & pausing.\"\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "                    if hasattr(self.cb, \"flatten_all\"):\n",
        "                        self.cb.flatten_all(\"daily_loss_cap\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                    # --- local ledger sweep so /pos and /pl reflect CLOSED immediately ---\n",
        "                    try:\n",
        "                        if self._open:\n",
        "                            syms = [v['symbol'] for v in self._open.values()]\n",
        "                            ltp_df = self.cb.get_ltp_batch(syms)\n",
        "                            to_close = []\n",
        "                            for tid, st in list(self._open.items()):\n",
        "                                sym   = st[\"symbol\"]\n",
        "                                qty   = int(st[\"qty\"])\n",
        "                                entry = float(st[\"entry\"])\n",
        "                                try:\n",
        "                                    last = float(ltp_df.loc[sym, \"ltp\"])\n",
        "                                except Exception:\n",
        "                                    last = entry  # fallback if quote missing\n",
        "                                pnl = (last - entry) * qty * lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "                                self.db.update_trade_exit(tid, last, pnl, \"CLOSED\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                                to_close.append(tid)\n",
        "                            for tid in to_close:\n",
        "                                self._open.pop(tid, None)\n",
        "                            self.cb.notify(\"🧹 Local ledger closed remaining OPEN rows after loss-cap.\")\n",
        "                    except Exception:\n",
        "                        pass\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    pass\n",
        "\n",
        "                self._loss_tripped = True\n",
        "                self._pause_reason = \"loss_cap\"\n",
        "                self._ai_enabled = False\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "            # --- market window gate ---\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                if \"can_trade_now\" in globals() and callable(can_trade_now) and not can_trade_now():\n",
        "                    self._note_decision(\"Market closed\", {\"status\": market_status_text()})\n",
        "                    return\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                # best-effort guard; continue if helper absent\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "            # --- choose idea ---\n",
        "            regime = self.regime_engine.detect()\n",
        "            self.cb.current_regime = regime\n",
        "\n",
        "            idea = None\n",
        "            diag = {\"regime\": regime}\n",
        "\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                idea = self.router.route(regime)\n",
        "                if idea:\n",
        "                    print(\n",
        "                        f\"AI IDEA → {idea.symbol} | conf={idea.confidence:.2f} \"\n",
        "                        f\"| SL={idea.sl} | TP={idea.tp} | strategy={idea.strategy}\"\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "            except Exception as e:\n",
        "                self.cb.notify(f\"⚠️ Router error: {e}\")\n",
        "                idea = None\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not idea:\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    result = self.router.selector.choose()\n",
        "                    if result:\n",
        "                        idea, extra_diag = result\n",
        "                        if isinstance(extra_diag, dict):\n",
        "                            diag.update(extra_diag)\n",
        "                except Exception as e:\n",
        "                    self.cb.notify(f\"⚠️ Selector error: {e}\")\n",
        "                    idea = None\n",
        "            diag = {\"regime\": regime}\n",
        "\n",
        "            self._last_decision = {\"idea\": dataclasses.asdict(idea) if idea else None, \"diag\": diag}\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not idea:\n",
        "                self._note_decision(diag.get(\"reason\", \"No idea\"), diag)\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "            if idea.confidence < self.cfg.confidence_threshold:\n",
        "                print(\"\\n🚫 TRADE BLOCKED - CONFIDENCE TOO LOW\")\n",
        "                print(\"Symbol:\", idea.symbol)\n",
        "                print(\"Confidence:\", idea.confidence)\n",
        "                print(\"Threshold:\", self.cfg.confidence_threshold)\n",
        "                print(\"Diagnostic:\", diag)\n",
        "                print(\"=====================================\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\"Confidence below threshold\",\n",
        "                    {\"confidence\": idea.confidence,\n",
        "                    \"threshold\": self.cfg.confidence_threshold, **diag})\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "            # --- allocation from confidence ---\n",
        "            if len(self._open) >= self.cfg.max_concurrent_positions:\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\"Max concurrent positions reached\", {})\n",
        "                return\n",
        "            alloc_pct = self._alloc_pct_from_conf(idea.confidence)\n",
        "            cash_now  = float(getattr(self, \"_remaining_capital\", 0.0))\n",
        "\n",
        "            # ---- fetch price first ----\n",
        "            last_series = self.cb.get_ltp_batch([idea.symbol]).loc[idea.symbol, \"ltp\"]\n",
        "            print(\"RAW LTP SERIES:\", last_series)\n",
        "\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                last = float(last_series)\n",
        "            except:\n",
        "                last = float(\"nan\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not np.isfinite(last) or last <= 0:\n",
        "                print(\"⚠️ Invalid LTP — skipping\")\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not np.isfinite(last) or last <= 0:\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\"Invalid LTP\", {\"symbol\": idea.symbol, \"ltp\": str(last_series)})\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "            # ---- capital guard ----\n",
        "            lot = lot_size_for(idea.symbol)\n",
        "            min_lot_cost = last * lot\n",
        "            print(\"💰 DEBUG CAPITAL CHECK\")\n",
        "            print(\"Capital:\", cash_now)\n",
        "            print(\"Lot cost:\", min_lot_cost)\n",
        "            print(\"Symbol:\", idea.symbol)\n",
        "            print(\"LTP:\", last)\n",
        "            print(\"Lot size:\", lot)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if cash_now < min_lot_cost:\n",
        "\n",
        "                self.cb.notify(\n",
        "                    f\"💰 Capital exhausted. Remaining ₹{cash_now:.2f} < lot cost ₹{min_lot_cost:.2f}. \"\n",
        "                    f\"Pausing scan until capital frees.\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\n",
        "                    \"Insufficient capital for 1 lot\",\n",
        "                    {\"remaining\": cash_now, \"lot_cost\": min_lot_cost}\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "                self._ai_enabled = False\n",
        "                return\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\n",
        "                    \"Insufficient capital for 1 lot\",\n",
        "                    {\"remaining\": cash_now, \"lot_cost\": min_lot_cost}\n",
        "                )\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "            alloc_cash = cash_now * alloc_pct\n",
        "            if alloc_cash <= 0:\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\"No remaining capital\", {\"alloc_pct\": alloc_pct})\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "                        # Get correct lot size from universe\n",
        "            lot = lot_size_for(idea.symbol)\n",
        "\n",
        "            denom = last * lot\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not np.isfinite(denom) or denom <= 0:\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\"Invalid sizing denominator\", {\"denom\": float(denom)})\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "            # Compute LOTS only\n",
        "            lots = int(np.floor(alloc_cash / (last * lot)))\n",
        "            if last is None or np.isnan(last) or last <= 0:\n",
        "                print(\"⚠️ Invalid LTP, skipping trade\")\n",
        "                return\n",
        "            if lot <= 0:\n",
        "                print(\"⚠️ Invalid lot size\")\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "            if lots < 1:\n",
        "                if cash_now >= (last * lot):\n",
        "                    lots = 1\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    self._note_decision(\n",
        "                        \"Allocation too small for 1 lot\",\n",
        "                        {\"alloc_cash\": alloc_cash, \"lot_cost\": last * lot}\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "                    return\n",
        "\n",
        "            qty_units = lots * lot\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "            # ---- ATR-Based SL Adjustment ----\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                candles = self.cb.get_candles(idea.symbol, 5, 50)\n",
        "                df = pd.DataFrame(candles)\n",
        "\n",
        "                df[\"tr\"] = np.maximum(\n",
        "                    df[\"high\"] - df[\"low\"],\n",
        "                    np.maximum(\n",
        "                        abs(df[\"high\"] - df[\"close\"].shift(1)),\n",
        "                        abs(df[\"low\"] - df[\"close\"].shift(1))\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "                atr = df[\"tr\"].rolling(14).mean().iloc[-1]\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                atr = None\n",
        "\n",
        "            ATR_MULTIPLIER = 1.2\n",
        "\n",
        "            if atr and np.isfinite(atr):\n",
        "                if idea.side == \"BUY\":\n",
        "                    idea.sl = float(last - (atr * ATR_MULTIPLIER))\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    idea.sl = float(last + (atr * ATR_MULTIPLIER))\n",
        "\n",
        "            # ---- RR Enforcement (Institutional Guard) ----\n",
        "            entry = float(last)\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(\"DEBUG PRICE VALUES\")\n",
        "            print(\"symbol:\", idea.symbol)\n",
        "            print(\"last:\", last)\n",
        "            print(\"idea.sl:\", idea.sl)\n",
        "            print(\"idea.tp:\", idea.tp)\n",
        "\n",
        "            # --- SL / TP safety guard ---\n",
        "            # --- SL / TP safety guard ---\n",
        "            # --- SL / TP safety guard (clean single source) ---\n",
        "            sl = float(idea.sl) if idea.sl and np.isfinite(idea.sl) else round(entry * 0.90, 2)\n",
        "            tp = float(idea.tp) if idea.tp and np.isfinite(idea.tp) else round(entry * 1.20, 2)\n",
        "\n",
        "            idea.sl = sl\n",
        "            idea.tp = tp\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "            risk  = abs(entry - sl)\n",
        "            reward = abs(tp - entry)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if risk <= 0:\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\"Invalid SL distance\", {\"entry\": entry, \"sl\": sl})\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "            rr = reward / risk\n",
        "\n",
        "            MIN_RR = 1.5  # institutional threshold\n",
        "\n",
        "            if rr < MIN_RR:\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\n",
        "                    \"Rejected: Low RR\",\n",
        "                    {\"rr\": round(rr, 2), \"min_required\": MIN_RR}\n",
        "                )\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"[ENTRY_BEFORE_PLACE] symbol={idea.symbol} qty_to_place={qty_units}\")\n",
        "            self._positions[idea.symbol] = True\n",
        "\n",
        "            # ===== DUPLICATE TRADE PROTECTION =====\n",
        "            if idea.symbol in self._traded_today:\n",
        "                print(\"⚠️ Already traded today:\", idea.symbol)\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "            if idea.symbol in self._positions:\n",
        "                print(\"⚠️ Position already open:\", idea.symbol)\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "            # LOCK symbol immediately\n",
        "            self._traded_today.add(idea.symbol)\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(\"\\n=== ORDER DEBUG ===\")\n",
        "            print(\"symbol:\", idea.symbol)\n",
        "            print(\"side:\", idea.side)\n",
        "            print(\"qty_units:\", qty_units)\n",
        "            print(\"entry:\", entry)\n",
        "            print(\"sl:\", sl)\n",
        "            print(\"tp:\", tp)\n",
        "            print(\"====================\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            lot = lot_size_for(idea.symbol)\n",
        "            resp = self.cb.place_order(idea.symbol, idea.side, idea.qty * lot)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not resp or not resp.get(\"ok\"):\n",
        "                # unlock if order rejected\n",
        "                self._traded_today.discard(idea.symbol)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not resp or not bool(resp.get(\"ok\")):\n",
        "                # unlock if broker rejected\n",
        "                self._traded_today.discard(idea.symbol)\n",
        "                self._note_decision(\"Order rejected\", {\"resp\": resp})\n",
        "                return\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "            # --- persist + in-memory state ---\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "\n",
        "                print(\"\\n=== DB INSERT DEBUG ===\")\n",
        "                print(\"entry:\", entry)\n",
        "                print(\"sl:\", sl, type(sl))\n",
        "                print(\"tp:\", tp, type(tp))\n",
        "                print(\"========================\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                tid = self.db.insert_trade({\n",
        "                    \"ts\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n",
        "                    \"symbol\": idea.symbol,\n",
        "                    \"side\": idea.side,\n",
        "                    \"qty\": int(lots),\n",
        "                    \"entry_price\": entry,\n",
        "                    \"sl\": sl,\n",
        "                    \"tp\": tp,\n",
        "                    \"strategy\": idea.strategy,\n",
        "                    \"confidence\": idea.confidence,\n",
        "                    \"status\": \"OPEN\",\n",
        "                    \"reason\": idea.reason\n",
        "                })\n",
        "\n",
        "            except Exception as e:\n",
        "                self.cb.notify(f\"⚠️ Trade DB error: {e}\")\n",
        "                return\n",
        "            self._open[tid] = {\n",
        "                \"symbol\": idea.symbol,\n",
        "                \"side\": idea.side,\n",
        "                \"qty\": int(lots),\n",
        "                \"entry\": entry,\n",
        "                \"sl\": sl,\n",
        "                \"tp\": tp,\n",
        "                \"strategy\": idea.strategy,\n",
        "                \"confidence\": idea.confidence,\n",
        "                \"placed_at\": now,\n",
        "                \"resp\": resp\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "            self._trades_today += 1\n",
        "            self._traded_today.add(idea.symbol)\n",
        "\n",
        "            # Capital lock uses LOTS * lot_size\n",
        "            self._remaining_capital -= (lots * lot) * entry\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                self.cb.notify(\n",
        "                    f\"✅ AI BUY {idea.symbol} x{int(lots)} @~{last:.2f} | conf {idea.confidence:.2f} \"\n",
        "                    f\"| SL {idea.sl}, TP {idea.tp}\\nReason: {idea.reason}\\nDiag: {diag}\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            except Exception as e:\n",
        "                print(\"TELEGRAM SEND ERROR:\", e)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _alloc_pct_from_conf(self, conf: float) -> float:\n",
        "        T = self.cfg.confidence_threshold\n",
        "        A, B = self.cfg.first_trade_alloc_hint, self.cfg.max_alloc_per_trade\n",
        "        if conf <= T: return 0.0\n",
        "        if conf >= 1.0: return B\n",
        "        frac = (conf - T) / max(1e-9, (1.0 - T))\n",
        "        return float(A + frac * (B - A))\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _note_decision(self, reason: str, details: Dict[str, Any]):\n",
        "        self.db.insert_decision(reason, details)\n",
        "        msg = f\"🤖 No trade: {reason}\"\n",
        "        if \"confidence\" in details and \"threshold\" in details:\n",
        "            msg += f\" (conf {details['confidence']:.2f} < {details['threshold']:.2f})\"\n",
        "        self.cb.notify(msg)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _resume_open_trades(self):\n",
        "        rows = self.db.fetch_history(200)\n",
        "\n",
        "        for r in rows:\n",
        "            if r[\"status\"] == \"OPEN\":\n",
        "\n",
        "                entry = float(r.get(\"entry_price\") or 0)\n",
        "                sl    = float(r.get(\"sl\") or 0)\n",
        "                tp    = float(r.get(\"tp\") or 0)\n",
        "                qty   = int(r.get(\"qty\") or 0)\n",
        "\n",
        "                self._open[r[\"id\"]] = {\n",
        "                    \"symbol\": r[\"symbol\"],\n",
        "                    \"side\": r[\"side\"],\n",
        "                    \"qty\": qty,\n",
        "                    \"entry\": entry,\n",
        "                    \"sl\": sl,\n",
        "                    \"tp\": tp,\n",
        "                    \"strategy\": r.get(\"strategy\", \"UNKNOWN\"),\n",
        "                    \"confidence\": float(r.get(\"confidence\") or 0),\n",
        "                    \"placed_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc),\n",
        "\n",
        "                    # initialize monitor flags so monitor loop doesn't crash\n",
        "                    \"_armed_riskfree\": False,\n",
        "                    \"_armed_runner\": False,\n",
        "                    \"_tp_extended\": False\n",
        "                }\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _starting_capital(self) -> float:\n",
        "        v = getattr(self, \"_start_capital\", 0.0)\n",
        "        return float(v) if np.isfinite(v) and v > 0 else 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _net_pnl_today(self) -> tuple[float, float]:\n",
        "        \"\"\"Returns (realized_today, net_today=realized+open).\"\"\"\n",
        "        realized = float(self.db.today_pnl())\n",
        "        if not self._open:\n",
        "            return realized, realized\n",
        "        syms = [v[\"symbol\"] for v in self._open.values()]\n",
        "        ltp_df = self.cb.get_ltp_batch(syms)\n",
        "        open_pl = 0.0\n",
        "        for st in self._open.values():\n",
        "            sym, qty, entry = st[\"symbol\"], int(st[\"qty\"]), float(st[\"entry\"])\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                last = float(ltp_df.loc[sym, \"ltp\"])\n",
        "                if np.isfinite(last):\n",
        "                    open_pl += (last - entry) * qty * lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "        return realized, realized + open_pl\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _profit_gate_enforce(self) -> bool:\n",
        "        \"\"\"Return True when gate is ACTIVE; flatten and pause if configured.\"\"\"\n",
        "        if self.cfg.profit_target_pct <= 0:\n",
        "            return False\n",
        "        realized, net = self._net_pnl_today()\n",
        "        base = max(0.0, float(getattr(self, \"_start_capital\", 0.0)))\n",
        "        if base <= 0:\n",
        "            return False\n",
        "        target_abs = base * float(self.cfg.profit_target_pct)\n",
        "        measure = net if self.cfg.profit_gate_on_net else realized\n",
        "        tripped = (measure >= target_abs)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if tripped and not getattr(self, \"_profit_tripped\", False):\n",
        "            self._profit_tripped = True\n",
        "            self.cb.notify(f\"✅ Profit target hit: {measure:.2f} ≥ {target_abs:.2f} \"\n",
        "                           f\"({self.cfg.profit_target_pct*100:.1f}% of ₹{base:.0f}) — mode={self.cfg.profit_halt_mode}\")\n",
        "            # pause AI entries regardless of mode\n",
        "            self._ai_enabled = False\n",
        "            # flatten if requested\n",
        "            if self.cfg.profit_halt_mode.lower() in (\"flat\",\"close\",\"squareoff\"):\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    self.cb.flatten_all(\"profit_target_hit\")\n",
        "                except Exception as e:\n",
        "                    self.cb.notify(f\"⚠️ flatten_all failed: {e}\")\n",
        "        return bool(getattr(self, \"_profit_tripped\", False))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # --- monitor & trailing ---\n",
        "    def _monitor_once_guarded(self):\n",
        "      TP_EXT_FACTOR_LOCAL = 0.30\n",
        "      with self._lock:\n",
        "          if self._profit_gate_enforce():\n",
        "              return\n",
        "          if not self._open:\n",
        "              return\n",
        "          syms = [v[\"symbol\"] for v in self._open.values()]\n",
        "          ltp_df = self.cb.get_ltp_batch(syms)\n",
        "\n",
        "          to_close = []\n",
        "          for tid, st in list(self._open.items()):\n",
        "              sym, qty, entry = st[\"symbol\"], st[\"qty\"], float(st[\"entry\"])\n",
        "              sl, tp = float(st[\"sl\"]), float(st[\"tp\"])\n",
        "              try:\n",
        "                  last = float(ltp_df.loc[sym, \"ltp\"])\n",
        "              except Exception:\n",
        "                  continue\n",
        "\n",
        "              # --- “near-target trailing” parameters ---\n",
        "              R      = max(tp - entry, entry * 0.1)\n",
        "              arm_at = entry + 0.75 * (tp - entry)\n",
        "              lock_at= entry + 0.90 * (tp - entry)\n",
        "              be_min = entry + 0.20 * (tp - entry)\n",
        "              tight  = entry + 0.60 * (tp - entry)\n",
        "\n",
        "              st.setdefault(\"_armed_riskfree\", False)\n",
        "              st.setdefault(\"_armed_runner\", False)\n",
        "              st.setdefault(\"_tp_extended\", False)\n",
        "              # ---- Momentum Reversal Exit ----\n",
        "              try:\n",
        "                  candles = self.cb.get_candles(sym, 5, 50)\n",
        "                  df_m = pd.DataFrame(candles)\n",
        "                  close_m = df_m[\"close\"].astype(float)\n",
        "                  ema_fast = close_m.ewm(span=20, adjust=False).mean().iloc[-1]\n",
        "                  ema_slow = close_m.ewm(span=50, adjust=False).mean().iloc[-1]\n",
        "\n",
        "                  if st.get(\"side\",\"BUY\") == \"BUY\" and ema_fast < ema_slow:\n",
        "                      exit_side = \"SELL\"\n",
        "                      self.cb.place_order(sym, exit_side, qty)\n",
        "                      pnl = (last - entry) * qty * lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "                      self.db.update_trade_exit(tid, last, pnl, \"CLOSED\")\n",
        "                      self.cb.notify(f\"🔁 Momentum reversal exit {sym} @ {last:.2f}\")\n",
        "                      to_close.append(tid)\n",
        "                      continue\n",
        "              except Exception:\n",
        "                  pass\n",
        "\n",
        "              # 1) catastrophic guard (optional)\n",
        "              # 1) Catastrophic guard (optional hard floor)\n",
        "              catastrophic_sl = entry * 0.70   # 30% premium crash protection\n",
        "\n",
        "              if last <= catastrophic_sl:\n",
        "                  exit_side = \"SELL\" if st.get(\"side\",\"BUY\") == \"BUY\" else \"BUY\"\n",
        "                  try:\n",
        "                      self.cb.place_order(sym, exit_side, qty)\n",
        "                  except Exception as e:\n",
        "                      self.cb.notify(f\"⚠️ Catastrophic exit failed {sym}: {e}\")\n",
        "                      continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                  pnl = (last - entry) * qty * lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "                  self.db.update_trade_exit(tid, last, pnl, \"CLOSED\")\n",
        "                  self.cb.notify(f\"🛑 Catastrophic exit {sym} id {tid} @ {last:.2f} | PnL {pnl:.2f}\")\n",
        "                  to_close.append(tid)\n",
        "                  continue\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "              # 2) Arm trailing only near target\n",
        "              if (not st[\"_armed_riskfree\"]) and last >= arm_at:\n",
        "                  new_sl = max(sl, be_min)\n",
        "                  if new_sl > sl:\n",
        "                      st[\"sl\"] = new_sl\n",
        "                      self.cb.notify(f\"🔐 Trailed {sym} SL to {new_sl:.2f} (risk-free) [id {tid}]\")\n",
        "                  st[\"_armed_riskfree\"] = True\n",
        "                  sl = st[\"sl\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "              if st[\"_armed_riskfree\"] and (not st[\"_armed_runner\"]) and last >= lock_at:\n",
        "                  new_sl = max(st[\"sl\"], tight)\n",
        "                  if new_sl > st[\"sl\"]:\n",
        "                      st[\"sl\"] = new_sl\n",
        "                      self.cb.notify(f\"🔐 Tightened {sym} SL to {new_sl:.2f} near TP [id {tid}]\")\n",
        "                  st[\"_armed_runner\"] = True\n",
        "                  sl = st[\"sl\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "              # 3) Momentum “runner” — extend TP if strong\n",
        "              try:\n",
        "                  candles = self.cb.get_candles(sym, 5, 120)\n",
        "                  _df = pd.DataFrame(candles)\n",
        "                  ema20 = _df[\"close\"].astype(float).ewm(span=20, adjust=False).mean().iloc[-1]\n",
        "                  chg   = (float(_df[\"close\"].iloc[-1]) - float(_df[\"open\"].iloc[-20])) / max(1e-9, float(_df[\"open\"].iloc[-20]))\n",
        "                  strong = (last > ema20) and (chg > 0.01)\n",
        "              except Exception:\n",
        "                  strong = False\n",
        "\n",
        "              if st[\"_armed_runner\"] and (not st[\"_tp_extended\"]) and strong:\n",
        "                  ext = TP_EXT_FACTOR_LOCAL * (tp - entry)   # +30% of distance to TP (using local var)\n",
        "                  st[\"tp\"] = round(tp + ext, 2)\n",
        "                  st[\"_tp_extended\"] = True\n",
        "                  self.cb.notify(f\"🏃 Extending TP for {sym} to {st['tp']:.2f} (momentum strong) [id {tid}]\")\n",
        "                  tp = st[\"tp\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "                  # ---- push the revised TP to broker, if your bridge exposes it ----\n",
        "                  try:\n",
        "                      if 'set_tp' in globals() and callable(set_tp):\n",
        "                          set_tp(sym, float(tp))\n",
        "                  except Exception:\n",
        "                      pass\n",
        "\n",
        "              # 4) Hard SL/TP checks\n",
        "              # 4) Hard SL/TP checks (side-aware)\n",
        "\n",
        "              side = st.get(\"side\", \"BUY\")\n",
        "\n",
        "              if side == \"BUY\":\n",
        "                  sl_hit = last <= st[\"sl\"]\n",
        "                  tp_hit = last >= tp\n",
        "              else:\n",
        "                  sl_hit = last >= st[\"sl\"]\n",
        "                  tp_hit = last <= tp\n",
        "\n",
        "              if sl_hit:\n",
        "                  try:\n",
        "                      exit_side = \"SELL\" if st.get(\"side\",\"BUY\") == \"BUY\" else \"BUY\"\n",
        "                      self.cb.place_order(sym, exit_side, qty)\n",
        "                  except Exception as e:\n",
        "                      self.cb.notify(f\"⚠️ Exit order failed for {sym}: {e}\")\n",
        "                      continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                  pnl = (st[\"sl\"] - entry) * qty * lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "                  self.db.update_trade_exit(tid, st[\"sl\"], pnl, \"CLOSED\")\n",
        "                  trade_value = st[\"qty\"] * entry * lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "                  self._remaining_capital += trade_value\n",
        "                  self._remaining_capital += pnl\n",
        "\n",
        "                  self.cb.notify(f\"🛑 SL hit {sym} id {tid} @ {st['sl']:.2f} | PnL {pnl:.2f}\")\n",
        "                  to_close.append(tid)\n",
        "                  continue\n",
        "\n",
        "              if tp_hit:\n",
        "                  try:\n",
        "                      exit_side = \"SELL\" if st.get(\"side\",\"BUY\") == \"BUY\" else \"BUY\"\n",
        "                      self.cb.place_order(sym, exit_side, qty)\n",
        "                  except Exception as e:\n",
        "                      self.cb.notify(f\"⚠️ Exit order failed for {sym}: {e}\")\n",
        "                      continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                  pnl = (tp - entry) * qty * lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "                  self.db.update_trade_exit(tid, tp, pnl, \"CLOSED\")\n",
        "                  trade_value = st[\"qty\"] * entry * lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "                  self._remaining_capital += trade_value\n",
        "                  self._remaining_capital += pnl\n",
        "\n",
        "                  self.cb.notify(f\"🏁 TP hit {sym} id {tid} @ {tp:.2f} | PnL {pnl:.2f}\")\n",
        "                  to_close.append(tid)\n",
        "                  continue\n",
        "\n",
        "          for tid in to_close:\n",
        "              self._open.pop(tid, None)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ---------------- Optional PTB command handlers ----------------\n",
        "    def get_handlers(self):\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            from telegram.ext import CommandHandler\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            return []\n",
        "        return [\n",
        "            CommandHandler(\"ai_on\", self._cmd_ai_on),\n",
        "            CommandHandler(\"ai_off\", self._cmd_ai_off),\n",
        "            CommandHandler(\"scan\", self._cmd_scan),\n",
        "            CommandHandler(\"monitor\", self._cmd_monitor),\n",
        "            CommandHandler(\"ai_suggest\", self._cmd_ai_suggest),\n",
        "            CommandHandler(\"why\", self._cmd_why),\n",
        "            CommandHandler(\"pl\", self._cmd_pl),\n",
        "            CommandHandler(\"history\", self._cmd_history),\n",
        "            CommandHandler(\"eod_summary\", self._cmd_eod),\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    async def _cmd_ai_on(self, u, c): self.enable_ai(True); await u.effective_message.reply_text(\"🤖 AI enabled.\")\n",
        "    async def _cmd_ai_off(self, u, c): self.enable_ai(False); await u.effective_message.reply_text(\"⏸️ AI disabled.\")\n",
        "    async def _cmd_scan(self, u, c):\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            n = int(c.args[0]) if c.args else self._scan_interval\n",
        "            self.set_scan_interval_seconds(n)\n",
        "        except:\n",
        "            return await u.effective_message.reply_text(\"Usage: /scan <seconds>\")\n",
        "        await u.effective_message.reply_text(f\"🕒 AI scan interval set to {self._scan_interval}s.\")\n",
        "    async def _cmd_monitor(self, u, c):\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            n = int(c.args[0]) if c.args else self._mon_interval\n",
        "            self.set_monitor_interval_seconds(n)\n",
        "        except:\n",
        "            return await u.effective_message.reply_text(\"Usage: /monitor <seconds>\")\n",
        "        await u.effective_message.reply_text(f\"🕒 Trade monitor interval set to {self._mon_interval}s.\")\n",
        "    async def _cmd_ai_suggest(self, u, c):\n",
        "        regime = self.regime_engine.detect()\n",
        "        idea = None\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            idea = self.router.route(regime)\n",
        "            if idea:\n",
        "                print(\n",
        "                    f\"AI IDEA → {idea.symbol} | conf={idea.confidence:.2f} \"\n",
        "                    f\"| SL={idea.sl} | TP={idea.tp} | strategy={idea.strategy}\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "        except Exception as e:\n",
        "            return await u.effective_message.reply_text(f\"⚠️ Router error: {e}\")\n",
        "        diag = {\"regime\": regime}\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not idea:\n",
        "            return await u.effective_message.reply_text(f\"🤖 No trade idea: {diag.get('reason','n/a')} | {diag}\")\n",
        "        await u.effective_message.reply_text(\n",
        "            f\"💡 AI suggests: BUY {idea.symbol} qty~{idea.qty} | conf {idea.confidence:.2f} | SL {idea.sl} | TP {idea.tp}\\nDiag: {diag}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    async def _cmd_why(self, u, c):\n",
        "        import json as _j\n",
        "        await u.effective_message.reply_text(_j.dumps(self._last_decision, indent=2, default=str)[:3500])\n",
        "    async def _cmd_pl(self, u, c):\n",
        "        await u.effective_message.reply_text(f\"📈 Today's P&L: {self.db.today_pnl():.2f}\")\n",
        "    async def _cmd_history(self, u, c):\n",
        "        n = int(c.args[0]) if c.args else 10\n",
        "        rows = self.db.fetch_history(n)\n",
        "        if not rows:\n",
        "            return await u.effective_message.reply_text(\"No trades yet.\")\n",
        "        out = [f\"#{r['id']} {r['ts']} {r['symbol']} {r['side']} x{r['qty']} @ {r['entry_price']} -> {r['status']} \"\n",
        "               f\"exit {r.get('exit_price','')} pnl {r.get('pnl','')} | {r['strategy']} conf {r['confidence']:.2f}\" for r in rows]\n",
        "        await u.effective_message.reply_text(\"\\n\".join(out)[:3500])\n",
        "    async def _cmd_eod(self, u, c):\n",
        "        rows = self.db.fetch_history(200)\n",
        "        if not rows:\n",
        "            return await u.effective_message.reply_text(\"No trades yet.\")\n",
        "        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n",
        "        by = df.groupby(\"strategy\")[\"pnl\"].sum().sort_values(ascending=False)\n",
        "        await u.effective_message.reply_text(\"🧾 EOD Summary (by strategy):\\n\" + by.to_string()[:3500])\n",
        "'''\n",
        "with open(\"ai_strategist.py\",\"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n",
        "    f.write(code)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"✅ ai_strategist.py written (thread-only).\")"
      ]

        "AI_DB_DIR = os.getenv(\"AI_DB_DIR\", f\"{MOUNT}/MyDrive/trading\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def _is_drive_mounted() -> bool:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        return os.path.ismount(MOUNT) and os.path.exists(f\"{MOUNT}/MyDrive\")\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "# Refuse to run unless Drive is mounted\n",
        "if not _is_drive_mounted():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"Google Drive not mounted at /gdrive. Run the mount cell first.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "AI_DB_PATH = os.path.join(AI_DB_DIR, \"ai_trades.sqlite\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Refuse to create a new DB if missing\n",
        "if not os.path.exists(AI_DB_PATH):\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(f\"DB file missing on Drive: {AI_DB_PATH}. \"\n",
        "                       \"Place the existing DB there; will not create a new one.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---- Time helper (IST) ----\n",
        "IST = dt.timezone(dt.timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))\n",
        "def _now_ist():\n",
        "    return dt.datetime.now(IST)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---- notifier stub (Step 6 will rebind to notify_quiet) ----\n",
        "def _notify_stub(text, *_, **__):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        tg_send(str(text or \"\"))\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        print(str(text or \"\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---- Universe getter ----\n",
        "def _get_universe():\n",
        "    if \"UNIVERSE\" in globals() and isinstance(UNIVERSE, list) and UNIVERSE:\n",
        "        return UNIVERSE\n",
        "    if \"DF_UNIVERSE\" in globals() and isinstance(DF_UNIVERSE, pd.DataFrame) and not DF_UNIVERSE.empty:\n",
        "        return DF_UNIVERSE[\"symbol\"].tolist()\n",
        "    return []\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---- LTP/OHLC SANITIZERS (no column collisions) ----\n",
        "def _ltp_for_ai(symbols):\n",
        "    df = ltp_batch(symbols).copy()\n",
        "    if \"symbol\" in df.columns:\n",
        "        df = df.set_index(\"symbol\")\n",
        "    if \"LTP\" in df.columns and \"ltp\" not in df.columns:\n",
        "        df = df.rename(columns={\"LTP\": \"ltp\"})\n",
        "    return df[[\"ltp\"]].astype(float)\n",
        "\n",
        "def _ohlc_for_ai(symbols):\n",
        "    df = ohlc_batch(symbols).copy()\n",
        "    if \"symbol\" in df.columns:\n",
        "        df = df.set_index(\"symbol\")\n",
        "    df = df.rename(columns={\"open\":\"o\",\"high\":\"h\",\"low\":\"l\",\"close\":\"c\"})\n",
        "    df = df.drop(columns=[c for c in (\"ltp\",\"exchange\",\"symbolToken\") if c in df.columns], errors=\"ignore\")\n",
        "    keep = [c for c in (\"o\",\"h\",\"l\",\"c\") if c in df.columns]\n",
        "    return df[keep].astype(float)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---- Candles adapter ----\n",
        "def _candles_for_ai(symbol: str, tf_min: int, count: int):\n",
        "    return last_candles(symbol, tf_min, count)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---- simple notify used inside this step (Step 6 will override with quiet) ----\n",
        "def _notify(msg: str):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        tg_send(msg)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        print(msg)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================== Lot size + budget/resume helpers ===================\n",
        "\n",
        "def _db_close_latest_open_trade(sym: str, exit_price: float, status: str = \"CLOSED\") -> bool:\n",
        "    \"\"\"Close the most recent OPEN trade for `sym` in the AI DB.\"\"\"\n",
        "    import sqlite3\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        path = engine.db.path\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    con = sqlite3.connect(path); con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "    cur = con.cursor()\n",
        "    row = cur.execute(\n",
        "        \"SELECT id, qty, entry_price, side FROM trades \"\n",
        "        \"WHERE symbol=? AND status='OPEN' ORDER BY ts DESC LIMIT 1\",\n",
        "        (sym,)\n",
        "    ).fetchone()\n",
        "    con.close()\n",
        "    if not row:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    tid   = int(row[\"id\"])\n",
        "    qty   = int(row[\"qty\"] or 0)\n",
        "    entry = float(row[\"entry_price\"] or 0.0)\n",
        "    side  = (row[\"side\"] or \"BUY\").upper()\n",
        "    pnl   = (exit_price - entry) * qty * (1 if side == \"BUY\" else -1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # This calls your patched update_trade_exit hook, which also removes from positions\n",
        "    engine.db.update_trade_exit(tid, float(exit_price), float(pnl), status)\n",
        "    return True\n",
        "\n",
        "def _db_sum_realized_today(path: str) -> float:\n",
        "    import sqlite3, datetime as _dt\n",
        "\n",
        "    today = _dt.date.today().isoformat()\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(path) as con:\n",
        "        cur = con.cursor()\n",
        "\n",
        "        # 1) Prefer stored P&L (counts CLOSED + DRY because we use status!='OPEN')\n",
        "        row = cur.execute(\n",
        "            \"SELECT COALESCE(SUM(pnl),0) FROM trades \"\n",
        "            \"WHERE status!='OPEN' AND ts LIKE ?||'%'\",   # <-- key change\n",
        "            (today,)\n",
        "        ).fetchone()\n",
        "        total = float(row[0] or 0.0)\n",
        "        if abs(total) > 1e-9:\n",
        "            return total\n",
        "\n",
        "        # 2) Fallback: recompute from entry/exit/side with proper lot→units\n",
        "        total = 0.0\n",
        "        for sym, side, qty, ent, ex in cur.execute(\n",
        "            \"\"\"SELECT symbol, side, qty, entry_price, exit_price\n",
        "               FROM trades\n",
        "               WHERE status!='OPEN' AND ts LIKE ?||'%'\"\"\",  # <-- key change\n",
        "            (today,)\n",
        "        ):\n",
        "            if ent is None or ex is None:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "            lot  = lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "            q    = int(qty or 0)\n",
        "            units = q if (lot > 1 and q >= lot and q % lot == 0) else q * max(1, lot)\n",
        "            ent  = float(ent); ex = float(ex)\n",
        "            mult = -1.0 if (side or \"BUY\").upper() == \"SELL\" else 1.0\n",
        "            total += (ex - ent) * units * mult\n",
        "\n",
        "    return float(total)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _db_capital_in_use(path: str) -> float:\n",
        "    import sqlite3\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    used = 0.0\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(path) as con:\n",
        "        cur = con.cursor()\n",
        "        for sym, qty, ent in cur.execute(\n",
        "            \"SELECT symbol, qty, entry_price FROM trades WHERE status='OPEN'\"\n",
        "        ):\n",
        "            lot = lot_size_for(sym); q = int(qty or 0)\n",
        "            units = q if (lot > 1 and q >= lot and q % lot == 0) else q * max(1, lot)\n",
        "            used += float(ent or 0.0) * units\n",
        "    return float(used)\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- DB inspect/backup/clear used by /db* commands ---------------------\n",
        "def _db_path() -> str:\n",
        "    try: return getattr(engine.db, \"path\")\n",
        "    except Exception: return AI_DB_PATH\n",
        "\n",
        "def _db_debug_dump(n: int = 5, which: str = \"last\") -> str:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        path = getattr(engine.db, \"path\")\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return \"DB not ready.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    import sqlite3\n",
        "    try:    used = float(_db_capital_in_use(path))\n",
        "    except: used = 0.0\n",
        "    try:    realized = float(_db_sum_realized_today(path))\n",
        "    except: realized = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(path) as con:\n",
        "        con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "        cur = con.cursor()\n",
        "        header = f\"DB: {path} | used=₹{used:.2f} realized_today=₹{realized:+.2f}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        w = (which or \"last\").lower()\n",
        "        if w == \"open\":\n",
        "            q = \"\"\"SELECT id, ts, symbol, side, qty, entry_price, sl, tp, status\n",
        "                   FROM trades WHERE status='OPEN' ORDER BY ts DESC LIMIT ?\"\"\"\n",
        "            rows = cur.execute(q, (n,)).fetchall()\n",
        "        elif w.startswith(\"id:\"):\n",
        "            rid = int(w.split(\":\", 1)[1])\n",
        "            rows = cur.execute(\"SELECT * FROM trades WHERE id=?\", (rid,)).fetchall()\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            q = \"\"\"SELECT id, ts, symbol, side, qty, entry_price, exit_price, pnl, status\n",
        "                   FROM trades ORDER BY ts DESC LIMIT ?\"\"\"\n",
        "            rows = cur.execute(q, (n,)).fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "    def fmt(row):\n",
        "        d = dict(row)\n",
        "        return (\"#{id} {ts} {symbol} {side} x{qty} \"\n",
        "                \"in={entry_price} out={exit_price} pnl={pnl} [{status}]\").format(\n",
        "            id=d.get(\"id\",\"-\"), ts=d.get(\"ts\",\"-\"), symbol=d.get(\"symbol\",\"-\"),\n",
        "            side=d.get(\"side\",\"-\"), qty=d.get(\"qty\",\"-\"),\n",
        "            entry_price=d.get(\"entry_price\",\"-\"), exit_price=d.get(\"exit_price\",\"-\"),\n",
        "            pnl=d.get(\"pnl\",\"-\"), status=d.get(\"status\",\"-\"),\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    out = [fmt(r) for r in rows] if rows else [\"(no rows)\"]\n",
        "    return header + \"\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(out[:200])\n",
        "\n",
        "def _db_backup() -> str:\n",
        "    import os, shutil, datetime as _dt\n",
        "    src = _db_path()\n",
        "    if not os.path.exists(src): return \"DB not found.\"\n",
        "    stamp = _dt.datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y%m%d_%H%M%S\")\n",
        "    dst = f\"{os.path.splitext(src)[0]}.backup_{stamp}.sqlite\"\n",
        "    shutil.copy2(src, dst)\n",
        "    return f\"Backup created: {dst}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def _db_vacuum(path: str):\n",
        "    import sqlite3\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=10.0) as con:\n",
        "        # harmless if not in WAL\n",
        "        try: con.execute(\"PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE);\")\n",
        "        except Exception: pass\n",
        "        con.execute(\"VACUUM;\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _db_clear(date_str: str | None = None, clear_all: bool = False) -> str:\n",
        "    import sqlite3, datetime as dt\n",
        "    path = _db_path()\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=5.0) as con:\n",
        "        cur = con.cursor()\n",
        "        total_before = cur.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM trades\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "        if clear_all:\n",
        "            cur.execute(\"DELETE FROM trades\")\n",
        "            deleted = total_before\n",
        "            note = \"all rows\"\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            if not date_str:\n",
        "                date_str = dt.date.today().isoformat()\n",
        "            cur.execute(\"DELETE FROM trades WHERE ts LIKE ?||'%'\", (date_str,))\n",
        "            deleted = cur.rowcount\n",
        "            note = f\"date {date_str}\"\n",
        "        total_after = cur.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM trades\").fetchone()[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    try: ai_budget_recalc_tick()\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    _db_vacuum(path)\n",
        "    return f\"DB clear OK: deleted {deleted} ({note}). Rows now: {total_after}. File: {path}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Profit target helpers (accepts 10.0 or 0.10) ---\n",
        "def _pct_to_ratio(p):\n",
        "    p = float(p)\n",
        "    return p/100.0 if p > 1.0 else p\n",
        "\n",
        "def _pct_display(p):\n",
        "    p = float(p)\n",
        "    return p if p > 1.0 else p*100.0\n",
        "\n",
        "def profit_target_rupees(pct: float, floor_rupees: float = 0.0) -> float:\n",
        "    snap = _budget_snapshot()\n",
        "    init = float(snap.get(\"init\", 0.0))\n",
        "    ratio = _pct_to_ratio(pct)\n",
        "    target = max(float(floor_rupees or 0.0), init * ratio)\n",
        "    # optional: persist for UI\n",
        "    try: _meta_set(_today_key(\"profit_target_rupees\"), target)\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    return float(target)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Unified budget snapshot (used by /status and friends) --------------------\n",
        "def _budget_snapshot():\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        path = engine.db.path\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return {\"init\": 0.0, \"used\": 0.0, \"realized\": 0.0, \"remaining\": 0.0}\n",
        "\n",
        "    used = _db_capital_in_use(path)\n",
        "    try:    realized = float(_realized_today())\n",
        "    except: realized = float(_db_sum_realized_today(path))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # *** read today’s persisted budget FIRST ***\n",
        "    init = None\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        init = float(_meta_get(_today_key(\"budget_init\"), 0.0) or 0.0)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        init = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not (init and init > 0):\n",
        "        # fall back to in-memory mirror (if already set)\n",
        "        init = float(globals().get(\"AI_START_BUDGET\", 0.0))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not (init and init > 0):\n",
        "        # final fallback: derive from used/realized/remaining mirrors if available\n",
        "        init = float(getattr(engine, \"_remaining_capital\", 0.0)) + used - realized\n",
        "\n",
        "    # keep the in-memory mirror in sync\n",
        "    globals()[\"AI_START_BUDGET\"] = init\n",
        "    remaining = max(0.0, init + realized - used)\n",
        "    return {\"init\": init, \"used\": used, \"realized\": realized, \"remaining\": remaining}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- NEW: one true source of remaining for ALL call-sites ---------------------\n",
        "def remaining_budget() -> float:\n",
        "    \"\"\"Fresh remaining ₹ using the same formula as /status (init + realized - used).\"\"\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        ai_budget_recalc_tick()  # keep mirrors fresh\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "    snap = _budget_snapshot()    # {'init','used','realized','remaining'}\n",
        "    return float(snap.get(\"remaining\", 0.0))\n",
        "\n",
        "def ai_budget_recalc_tick():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Recompute remaining capital from DB + in-memory DRY positions.\"\"\"\n",
        "    # 1) DB 'used'\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        path = engine.db.path\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        path = None\n",
        "\n",
        "    used_db = 0.0\n",
        "    if path:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            used_db = _db_capital_in_use(path)  # sums OPEN rows with entry_price\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            used_db = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 2) In-memory DRY 'used'\n",
        "    used_dry = 0.0\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        for s, p in (positions or {}).items():\n",
        "            ent = float(p.get(\"entry\", 0.0))\n",
        "            q_lots = int(p.get(\"qty\", 0))\n",
        "            used_dry += ent * q_lots * lot_size_for(s)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    used = used_db\n",
        "    if globals().get(\"DRY_RUN\", False) or (used_dry > used_db):\n",
        "        used = used_db + used_dry\n",
        "\n",
        "    start = float(globals().get(\"AI_START_BUDGET\", 0.0)) or 0.0\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        realized = float(_realized_today())  # Step 6 combiner if available\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        realized = float(_db_sum_realized_today(path)) if path else 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    remaining = max(0.0, start + realized - used)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try: engine.set_remaining_capital(remaining)\n",
        "    except Exception: engine._remaining_capital = remaining\n",
        "\n",
        "    engine._budget_used       = used\n",
        "    engine._budget_realized   = realized\n",
        "    engine._budget_remaining  = remaining\n",
        "\n",
        "        # --- keep profit-halt flag in sync (no notify here) ---\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        pct = getattr(engine.cfg, \"profit_target_pct\", 10.0)  # accepts 10 or 0.10\n",
        "        thr = profit_target_rupees(pct)                       # uses init budget\n",
        "        engine._profit_halt = bool(realized >= thr)\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            _meta_set(_today_key(\"profit_halt\"), engine._profit_halt)\n",
        "            _meta_set(_today_key(\"profit_target_rupees\"), thr)\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def resume_from_db():\n",
        "    import sqlite3, datetime as dt\n",
        "    global positions\n",
        "    positions = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    path = getattr(engine.db, \"path\")\n",
        "    con = sqlite3.connect(path)\n",
        "    con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "    cur = con.cursor()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Resume only OPEN rows with an entry price\n",
        "    rows = cur.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "        SELECT symbol, qty, entry_price, sl, tp\n",
        "        FROM trades\n",
        "        WHERE status='OPEN' AND entry_price IS NOT NULL\n",
        "        ORDER BY ts ASC\n",
        "    \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "    con.close()\n",
        "\n",
        "    positions = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    for r in rows:\n",
        "        s = r[\"symbol\"]\n",
        "        ent_raw = r[\"entry_price\"]\n",
        "        if ent_raw is None:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "        ent = float(ent_raw)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Coalesce SL/TP for safety\n",
        "        sl = 0.0 if r[\"sl\"] is None else float(r[\"sl\"])\n",
        "        tp = 0.0 if r[\"tp\"] is None else float(r[\"tp\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "        lot   = lot_size_for(s)\n",
        "        q_db  = int(r[\"qty\"] or 0)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Normalize: store/print in LOTS\n",
        "        if lot > 1 and q_db >= lot and q_db % lot == 0:\n",
        "            q_lots = q_db // lot\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            q_lots = q_db\n",
        "        units  = q_lots * lot\n",
        "\n",
        "        positions[s] = {\n",
        "            \"qty\": int(q_lots),                  # LOTS for display/logic\n",
        "            \"entry\": ent,\n",
        "            \"sl\": sl,\n",
        "            \"tp\": tp,\n",
        "            \"rupees_locked\": ent * units,        # lock cash in UNITS\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    # reflect positions in memory if you keep a mirror\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        engine._positions = positions\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    # refresh budget mirrors (used / realized / remaining)\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        ai_budget_recalc_tick()\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        _notify(f\"♻️ Resumed {len(rows)} open trade(s).\")\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================== ORDER BRIDGE (adds budget gate + lots→units) =================\n",
        "_prev_place = globals().get(\"place_order\", None)\n",
        "\n",
        "def ai_place_order_bridge(sym: str, side: str, qty_lots: int) -> dict:\n",
        "\n",
        "    side = (side or \"BUY\").upper()\n",
        "    lots_requested = int(qty_lots)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ---- HARD SAFETY GATES ----\n",
        "    if not globals().get(\"ENGINE_ENABLED\", True):\n",
        "        return {\"ok\": False, \"err\": \"engine_disabled\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not globals().get(\"AUTO_MODE\", False):\n",
        "        return {\"ok\": False, \"err\": \"auto_off\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ---- DRY MODE ----\n",
        "    if DRY_RUN:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            df = ltp_batch([sym])\n",
        "            ltp = float(df[\"ltp\"].iloc[0])\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            return {\"ok\": False, \"err\": \"No LTP for DRY\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "        _acct_open_local(sym, side, lots_requested, ltp, dry=True)\n",
        "        return {\"ok\": True, \"dry\": True, \"price\": ltp}\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ---- DUPLICATE POSITION PROTECTION ----\n",
        "    if sym in getattr(engine, \"_positions\", {}):\n",
        "        print(f\"⚠️ Trade already active for {sym} — skipping\")\n",
        "        return {\"ok\": False, \"err\": \"already_open\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ---- LIVE MODE ----\n",
        "    order_id = None # Initialize order_id\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        # Snapshot expected price BEFORE order\n",
        "        df = ltp_batch([sym])\n",
        "        expected_price = float(df[\"ltp\"].iloc[0])\n",
        "        if engine._trades_today >= 5:\n",
        "            print(\"⛔ Max trades reached\")\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"🚨 LIVE ORDER REQUEST → {sym} {side} {lots_requested} lot\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # BROKER_PLACE_ORDER expects lots as qty_lots\n",
        "        resp = BROKER_PLACE_ORDER(sym, side, lots_requested, \"MKT\")\n",
        "        ok = bool(resp.get(\"ok\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not ok:\n",
        "            print(f\"❌ LIVE ORDER FAILED → {resp}\")\n",
        "            return resp\n",
        "\n",
        "        broker_data = resp.get(\"resp\", {})\n",
        "        order_id = broker_data.get(\"data\", {}).get(\"orderid\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"✅ LIVE ORDER ACCEPTED → OrderID: {order_id}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ===== RECORD TRADE =====\n",
        "        engine._traded_today.add(sym)\n",
        "\n",
        "        engine._positions[sym] = {\n",
        "            \"entry\": expected_price,\n",
        "            \"qty\": lots_requested\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        engine._trades_today += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(f\"❌ LIVE EXECUTION ERROR → {e}\")\n",
        "        return {\"ok\": False, \"err\": str(e)}\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ---- Verify Fill ----\n",
        "    actual_price = None\n",
        "    filled_lots = 0 # Initialize filled_lots\n",
        "    lot_size = lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
