        "        for m in j.get(\"marketState\", []):\n",
        "            if m.get(\"market\") == segment_name:\n",
        "                status = (m.get(\"marketStatus\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n",
        "                td = m.get(\"tradeDate\")\n",
        "                if status == \"open\":   return (True,  f\"Open — NSE {segment_name} ({td})\")\n",
        "                if status == \"closed\": return (False, f\"Closed — NSE {segment_name} ({td})\")\n",
        "                return (None, f\"{m.get('marketStatus')} — NSE {segment_name} ({td})\")\n",
        "        return (None, \"NSE status: segment not found\")\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        return (None, f\"NSE status error: {e}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def market_open_now():\n",
        "    if datetime.now(IST).weekday() >= 5:\n",
        "        NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"open\"] = False; NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"reason\"] = \"Closed — Weekend\"; return False\n",
        "    if nse_is_trading_holiday(datetime.now(IST).date(), segment=\"FO\"):\n",
        "        NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"open\"] = False; NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"reason\"] = \"Closed — NSE trading holiday (FO)\"; return False\n",
        "    nse_open, nse_reason = nse_market_status_open(segment_name=\"Capital Market\")\n",
        "    if nse_open is False:\n",
        "        NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"open\"] = False; NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"reason\"] = nse_reason or \"Closed — NSE\"; return False\n",
        "    nowt = datetime.now(IST).time()\n",
        "    if not (_t(MKT_START) <= nowt <= _t(MKT_END)):\n",
        "        NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"open\"] = False; NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"reason\"] = \"Closed — Outside trading hours\"; return False\n",
        "    if dtime(9, 15) <= nowt < dtime(9, 25):\n",
        "        NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"open\"] = True; NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"reason\"] = \"Open — First-bar grace window\"; return True\n",
        "    if HOLIDAY_CHECK_CANDLES and len(DF_UNIVERSE):\n",
        "        probes = DF_UNIVERSE[\"symbol\"].head(6).tolist(); fresh_any = False\n",
        "        for s in probes:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                if _last_bar_is_today(s, 5): fresh_any = True; break\n",
        "            except Exception: pass\n",
        "        if not fresh_any:\n",
        "            if nse_open is True:\n",
        "                NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"open\"] = True; NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"reason\"] = \"Open — NSE says open; candles delayed\"; return True\n",
        "            NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"open\"] = False; NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"reason\"] = \"Closed — No fresh candles across probes\"; return False\n",
        "    NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"open\"] = True; NSE_STATUS_CACHE[\"reason\"] = nse_reason or \"Open\"; return True\n",
        "\n",
        "def can_trade_now(): return market_open_now()\n",
        "def market_status_text(force=False) -> str:\n",
        "    if force: NSE_STATUS_CACHE.update({\"ts\": 0.0})\n",
        "    wk = datetime.now(IST).strftime(\"%A\"); open_now = market_open_now()\n",
        "    reason = NSE_STATUS_CACHE.get(\"reason\", \"Unknown\"); flag = \"🟢 OPEN\" if open_now else \"🔴 CLOSED\"\n",
        "    return f\"{flag} ({wk}) — {reason}\"\n",
        "# --- Market-hours guard + anti-spam (only warn for MANUAL attempts) ---\n",
        "from datetime import datetime, time as dtime, timedelta, timezone\n",
        "import time as _time\n",
        "\n",
        "IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))\n",
        "NSE_OPEN  = dtime(9, 15)\n",
        "NSE_CLOSE = dtime(15, 30)\n",
        "\n",
        "def _is_market_open(now_ist=None):\n",
        "    now_ist = now_ist or datetime.now(IST)\n",
        "    if now_ist.weekday() >= 5:  # Sat/Sun\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "    t = now_ist.time()\n",
        "    return (t >= NSE_OPEN) and (t <= NSE_CLOSE)\n",
        "\n",
        "_MCLOSED_LAST_SENT = 0.0\n",
        "_MCLOSED_COOLDOWN_SEC = 20.0  # adjust if you want\n",
        "\n",
        "def _maybe_warn_market_closed(*, source=\"AUTO\", send_func=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Show 'market closed' ONLY for MANUAL attempts.\n",
        "    AUTO engine never notifies. Has cooldown to avoid spam.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    global _MCLOSED_LAST_SENT\n",
        "    if source != \"MANUAL\":\n",
        "        return\n",
        "    if (_time.time() - _MCLOSED_LAST_SENT) < _MCLOSED_COOLDOWN_SEC:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "    _MCLOSED_LAST_SENT = _time.time()\n",
        "    send_func = send_func or (lambda s: print(s))\n",
        "    send_func(\"⛔ Market closed (holiday/weekend) — order skipped.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Budget helpers & DRY mirrors --------------------------------------------\n",
        "def _remaining_capital():\n",
        "    try:    return float(getattr(engine, \"_remaining_capital\", 0.0))\n",
        "    except: return 0.0\n",
        "def _budget_use(amount: float):\n",
        "    b = getattr(engine, \"budget\", None)\n",
        "    if b and hasattr(b, \"use\"): b.use(amount)\n",
        "    else: setattr(engine, \"_remaining_capital\", max(0.0, _remaining_capital() - float(amount)))\n",
        "def _budget_release(amount: float):\n",
        "    b = getattr(engine, \"budget\", None)\n",
        "    if b and hasattr(b, \"release\"): b.release(amount)\n",
        "    else: setattr(engine, \"_remaining_capital\", _remaining_capital() + float(amount))\n",
        "\n",
        "def _now_ist_iso(): return datetime.now(IST).isoformat()\n",
        "def dry_log_entry(sym, qty_lots, price):\n",
        "    lots = int(qty_lots); units = lots * lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "    DRY_LOG.append({\"ts\": _now_ist_iso(), \"symbol\": sym, \"qty_lots\": lots, \"units\": units,\n",
        "                    \"entry_price\": float(price), \"exit_price\": None, \"pnl\": None,\n",
        "                    \"status\": \"OPEN\", \"reason\": \"entry\"})\n",
        "\n",
        "def dry_log_exit(sym, qty_lots, exit_price, reason=\"exit\"):\n",
        "    lots = int(qty_lots); units = lots * lot_size_for(sym); exit_px = float(exit_price)\n",
        "    for r in reversed(DRY_LOG):\n",
        "        if r.get(\"symbol\") == sym and r.get(\"exit_price\") is None:\n",
        "            ent = float(r.get(\"entry_price\") or 0.0)\n",
        "            r[\"exit_price\"] = exit_px; r[\"exit_ts\"] = _now_ist_iso()\n",
        "            r[\"status\"] = \"CLOSED\"; r[\"reason\"] = reason; r.setdefault(\"units\", units)\n",
        "            pnl = (exit_px - ent) * int(r[\"units\"]); r[\"pnl\"] = pnl\n",
        "            globals()[\"DRY_REALIZED\"] = float(globals().get(\"DRY_REALIZED\", 0.0) + pnl)\n",
        "            return\n",
        "    DRY_LOG.append({\"ts\": _now_ist_iso(), \"symbol\": sym, \"qty_lots\": lots, \"units\": units,\n",
        "                    \"entry_price\": None, \"exit_price\": exit_px, \"pnl\": None,\n",
        "                    \"status\": \"CLOSED\", \"reason\": reason})\n",
        "\n",
        "def _acct_open_local(sym: str, side: str, qty_lots: int, price: float, dry: bool = True):\n",
        "    global positions\n",
        "    lots = int(qty_lots); px = float(price)\n",
        "    p = positions.get(sym, {\"qty\": 0, \"entry\": 0.0})\n",
        "    old_units = int(p[\"qty\"]) * lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "    new_units = lots * lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "    tot_units = old_units + new_units\n",
        "    new_entry = (p[\"entry\"] * old_units + px * new_units) / tot_units if tot_units else 0.0\n",
        "    existing_sl = p.get(\"sl_order_id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    positions[sym] = {\n",
        "        \"qty\": p[\"qty\"] + lots,\n",
        "        \"entry\": new_entry,\n",
        "        \"sl_order_id\": existing_sl\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    try: _budget_use(px * new_units)\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    if dry:\n",
        "        try: dry_log_entry(sym, lots, px)\n",
        "        except Exception: pass\n",
        "    try: ai_budget_recalc_tick()\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "\n",
        "# === Online learning (simple, session-scoped) =================================\n",
        "VOTE_MIN_BASE = int(os.environ.get(\"VOTE_MIN_BASE\", \"1\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "LEARN = {\n",
        "    \"vote_min\": VOTE_MIN_BASE,      # dynamic min votes required\n",
        "    \"vote_min_until\": 0.0,          # epoch when the bump expires\n",
        "    \"sym_penalty_until\": {},        # {symbol: epoch} -> skip symbol until time\n",
        "    \"sym_stats\": {},                # {symbol: {\"wins\": int, \"losses\": int, \"loss_streak\": int}}\n",
        "    \"mistake_counts\": {},           # {\"tag\": int}\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "def _acct_update_position(symbol: str, updates: dict):\n",
        "    global positions\n",
        "\n",
        "    if symbol not in positions:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    positions[symbol].update(updates)\n",
        "    return True\n",
        "\n",
        "def _now_s():\n",
        "    import time\n",
        "    return time.time()\n",
        "\n",
        "def current_min_votes() -> int:\n",
        "    if _now_s() >= LEARN.get(\"vote_min_until\", 0.0):\n",
        "        LEARN[\"vote_min\"] = VOTE_MIN_BASE\n",
        "        print(\"DEBUG vote_min =\", LEARN.get(\"vote_min\"))\n",
        "    return int(LEARN[\"vote_min\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "def is_penalized(sym: str) -> bool:\n",
        "    return _now_s() < LEARN[\"sym_penalty_until\"].get(sym, 0.0)\n",
        "\n",
        "def _bump_votes_temp(minutes: int, new_min: int):\n",
        "    LEARN[\"vote_min\"] = max(new_min, VOTE_MIN_BASE)\n",
        "    LEARN[\"vote_min_until\"] = max(LEARN.get(\"vote_min_until\", 0.0), _now_s() + 60*minutes)\n",
        "\n",
        "def _penalize_symbol(sym: str, minutes: int):\n",
        "    LEARN[\"sym_penalty_until\"][sym] = max(LEARN[\"sym_penalty_until\"].get(sym, 0.0), _now_s() + 60*minutes)\n",
        "\n",
        "def _tag(tag: str):\n",
        "    LEARN[\"mistake_counts\"][tag] = LEARN[\"mistake_counts\"].get(tag, 0) + 1\n",
        "\n",
        "def _mistake_tags(sym: str, entry_px: float, exit_px: float, entry_ts, exit_ts, reason: str):\n",
        "    tags = []\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        t = (entry_ts.astimezone(IST).time() if hasattr(entry_ts, 'astimezone') else entry_ts)\n",
        "        if dtime(12, 0) <= t <= dtime(13, 30): tags.append(\"midday_chop\")\n",
        "        if dtime(15, 15) <= t <= dtime(15, 30): tags.append(\"late_entry\")\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        dur_s = (exit_ts - entry_ts).total_seconds()\n",
        "        if reason == \"SL\" and dur_s is not None and dur_s < 10*60: tags.append(\"fast_failure\")\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        df = candles1m(sym, n=30)\n",
        "        if not df.empty:\n",
        "            s20 = sma(df[\"close\"].to_numpy(), 20)\n",
        "            if len(s20) >= 2 and s20[-1] < s20[-2]: tags.append(\"counter_trend\")\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "    if exit_px < entry_px: tags.append(\"loss\")\n",
        "    if reason == \"TP\": tags.append(\"took_profit\")\n",
        "    return tags\n",
        "\n",
        "def learn_from_trade(sym: str, entry_px: float, exit_px: float, entry_ts, exit_ts, reason: str):\n",
        "    pnl_units = exit_px - entry_px\n",
        "    win = pnl_units > 0\n",
        "    st = LEARN[\"sym_stats\"].setdefault(sym, {\"wins\": 0, \"losses\": 0, \"loss_streak\": 0})\n",
        "    if win:\n",
        "        st[\"wins\"] += 1\n",
        "        st[\"loss_streak\"] = 0\n",
        "        LEARN[\"vote_min\"] = VOTE_MIN_BASE\n",
        "        LEARN[\"vote_min_until\"] = 0.0\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        st[\"losses\"] += 1\n",
        "        st[\"loss_streak\"] += 1\n",
        "        if st[\"loss_streak\"] >= 2:\n",
        "            _bump_votes_temp(minutes=30, new_min=VOTE_MIN_BASE + 1)\n",
        "            _penalize_symbol(sym, minutes=45)\n",
        "    for tag in _mistake_tags(sym, entry_px, exit_px, entry_ts, exit_ts, reason):\n",
        "        _tag(tag)\n",
        "\n",
        "def learn_state_text() -> str:\n",
        "    mins_left = max(0, int((LEARN.get(\"vote_min_until\", 0.0) - _now_s())/60))\n",
        "    penal_syms = [f\"{s}({int((t-_now_s())/60)}m)\" for s, t in LEARN[\"sym_penalty_until\"].items() if t > _now_s()]\n",
        "    return (f\"Learn: vote_min={current_min_votes()} (decays in {mins_left}m) | \"\n",
        "            f\"penalized={', '.join(penal_syms) if penal_syms else 'none'} | \"\n",
        "            f\"mistakes={LEARN.get('mistake_counts',{})}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def _acct_close_local(sym: str, qty_lots: int, exit_price: float, dry: bool = True):\n",
        "    global positions\n",
        "    lots = int(qty_lots); px = float(exit_price)\n",
        "    if sym not in positions: return\n",
        "    try: _budget_release(px * lots * lot_size_for(sym))\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    cur = positions.get(sym, {\"qty\": 0, \"entry\": 0.0})\n",
        "    new_qty = max(0, int(cur[\"qty\"]) - lots)\n",
        "    if new_qty == 0:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            learn_from_trade(\n",
        "                sym,\n",
        "                float(cur.get(\"entry\", 0.0)),\n",
        "                float(px),\n",
        "                cur.get(\"ts\"),\n",
        "                datetime.now(IST),\n",
        "                reason=\"manual_close\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "        positions.pop(sym, None)\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        positions[sym][\"qty\"] = new_qty\n",
        "    if dry:\n",
        "        try: dry_log_exit(sym, lots, px, reason=\"exit\")\n",
        "        except Exception: pass\n",
        "    try: ai_budget_recalc_tick()\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "\n",
        "# Install DRY shims (preserve originals if present)\n",
        "_orig_dry_log_entry = globals().get(\"dry_log_entry\")\n",
        "_orig_dry_log_exit  = globals().get(\"dry_log_exit\")\n",
        "def _shim_dry_log_entry(sym, qty_lots, entry_price, *a, **k):\n",
        "    try: _acct_open_local(sym, \"BUY\", int(qty_lots), float(entry_price), dry=True)\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    if callable(_orig_dry_log_entry): return _orig_dry_log_entry(sym, qty_lots, entry_price, *a, **k)\n",
        "    globals().setdefault(\"DRY_LOG\", []).append({\"symbol\": sym, \"side\": \"BUY\", \"qty\": int(qty_lots),\n",
        "                                                \"entry_price\": float(entry_price), \"status\": \"OPEN\"}); return {\"ok\": True}\n",
        "def _shim_dry_log_exit(sym, qty_lots, exit_price, *a, **k):\n",
        "    try: _acct_close_local(sym, int(qty_lots), float(exit_price), dry=True)\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    if callable(_orig_dry_log_exit): return _orig_dry_log_exit(sym, qty_lots, exit_price, *a, **k)\n",
        "    globals().setdefault(\"DRY_LOG\", []).append({\"symbol\": sym, \"side\": \"SELL\", \"qty\": int(qty_lots),\n",
        "                                                \"exit_price\": float(exit_price), \"status\": \"CLOSED\"}); return {\"ok\": True}\n",
        "dry_log_entry = _shim_dry_log_entry\n",
        "dry_log_exit  = _shim_dry_log_exit\n",
        "\n",
        "def _flat_all_local(reason: str = \"manual\"):\n",
        "    if not positions: return\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        syms = list(positions.keys())\n",
        "        df = ltp_batch(syms) if syms else pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "        for s in syms:\n",
        "            lots = int(positions.get(s, {}).get(\"qty\", 0))\n",
        "            if lots <= 0: continue\n",
        "            try: px = float(df.loc[s, \"ltp\"])\n",
        "            except Exception: px = float(positions[s][\"entry\"])\n",
        "            _acct_close_local(s, lots, px, dry=True)\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    try: tg_send(f\"🧹 Squared off {len(syms)} pos — {reason}.\")\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Dynamic entry/exit & budget ---------------------------------------------\n",
        "def _required_cash(sym: str, qty_lots: int, ltp: float | None) -> float:\n",
        "    if ltp is None: return float(\"inf\")\n",
        "    return float(ltp) * lot_size_for(sym) * int(qty_lots)\n",
        "\n",
        "def entry(sym, qty):\n",
        "    if not can_trade_now(): return f\"Market closed: {market_status_text()}\"\n",
        "    if len(positions) >= MAX_OPEN_POSITIONS: return \"Max positions reached.\"\n",
        "    df = ltp_batch([sym]); l = df[\"ltp\"].iloc[0]\n",
        "    if l is None: return \"No LTP.\"\n",
        "    need = _required_cash(sym, qty, l); have = remaining_budget()\n",
        "    if need > have + 1e-6: return f\"Insufficient funds (need ₹{need:.0f}, have ₹{have:.0f})\"\n",
        "    print(f\"[ENTRY] symbol={sym} qty_input={qty}\")\n",
        "    #po = place_order(sym, \"BUY\", qty, \"MKT\")\n",
        "    units = qty * lot_size_for(sym)\n",
        "    po = place_order(sym, \"BUY\", units, \"MKT\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not po.get(\"ok\"): return f\"Order fail: {po}\"\n",
        "    # ---- Safe SL/TP calculation ----\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        sl = float(l * (1 - RISK_SL_PCT))\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        sl = float(l * 0.98)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        tp = float(l * (1 + RISK_TP_PCT))\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        tp = float(l * 1.02)\n",
        "    positions[sym] = {\"qty\":int(qty),\"side\":\"BUY\",\"entry\":float(l),\"sl\":float(sl),\"tp\":float(tp),\n",
        "                      \"ts\":datetime.now(),\"cooldown_until\":datetime.now()+timedelta(seconds=COOLDOWN_SEC)}\n",
        "    rupees_locked = _required_cash(sym, qty, l)\n",
        "    positions[sym][\"rupees_locked\"] = rupees_locked\n",
        "    try: _budget_use(rupees_locked)\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    tg_send(f\"🟢 BUY {sym} @ {l:.2f} (SL {sl:.2f} / TP {tp:.2f}) {'[DRY]' if DRY_RUN else ''}\")\n",
        "    if DRY_RUN:\n",
        "        try: dry_log_entry(sym, int(qty), float(l))\n",
        "        except Exception: pass\n",
        "    return \"OK\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def _fetch_exit_checks(sym: str):\n",
        "    l = None\n",
        "    try: dfp = ltp_batch([sym]); l = dfp[\"ltp\"].iloc[0]\n",
        "    except Exception: l = None\n",
        "    hi = lo = None\n",
        "    if l is None:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            c = candles1m(sym, n=1)\n",
        "            if not c.empty: hi = float(c[\"high\"].iloc[-1]); lo = float(c[\"low\"].iloc[-1])\n",
        "        except Exception: pass\n",
        "    return l, hi, lo\n",
        "\n",
        "def check_exits_once():\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not positions: return\n",
        "    syms = list(positions.keys())\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        df = ltp_batch(syms); mp = dict(zip(df[\"symbol\"], df[\"ltp\"]))\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        mp = {}\n",
        "    for s, p in list(positions.items()):\n",
        "        ltp = mp.get(s); hi = lo = None\n",
        "        if ltp is None: _, hi, lo = _fetch_exit_checks(s)\n",
        "        sl, tp = float(p[\"sl\"]), float(p[\"tp\"])\n",
        "        BUFFER = 0.002  # 0.2% buffer to avoid noise hits\n",
        "\n",
        "        tp_touched = (\n",
        "            (ltp is not None and ltp >= tp * (1 - BUFFER)) or\n",
        "            (hi is not None and hi >= tp * (1 - BUFFER))\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        sl_touched = (\n",
        "            (ltp is not None and ltp <= sl * (1 + BUFFER)) or\n",
        "            (lo is not None and lo <= sl * (1 + BUFFER))\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if tp_touched or sl_touched:\n",
        "            exit_px = float(ltp if ltp is not None else (tp if tp_touched else sl))\n",
        "            if DRY_RUN:\n",
        "                dry_log_exit(s, p[\"qty\"], exit_px, reason=(\"TP\" if tp_touched else \"SL\"))\n",
        "            ai_ledger_exit(s, exit_px, status=\"CLOSED\")\n",
        "            tg_send((\"🏁 TP hit \" if tp_touched else \"🛑 SL hit \") +\n",
        "                    f\"{s} @ {exit_px:.2f} (entry {p['entry']:.2f}) {'[DRY]' if DRY_RUN else ''}\")\n",
        "            try: _budget_release(p.get(\"rupees_locked\", p[\"entry\"] * p[\"qty\"] * lot_size_for(s)))\n",
        "            except Exception: pass\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                learn_from_trade(\n",
        "                    s,\n",
        "                    float(p.get(\"entry\", 0.0)),\n",
        "                    float(exit_px),\n",
        "                    p.get(\"ts\"),\n",
        "                    datetime.now(IST),\n",
        "                    reason=(\"TP\" if tp_touched else \"SL\")\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "            positions.pop(s, None)\n",
        "# --- Quiet notifier -----------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "import re\n",
        "_SUPPRESS_FOR = {\n",
        "    \"No trade: No merged data\": 300,\n",
        "    \"No trade: No remaining capital\": 300,\n",
        "    \"No trade: Duplicate trade blocked\": 180,\n",
        "    \"No trade: AI disabled\": 600,\n",
        "}\n",
        "_SUPPRESS_ANY_WINDOW = 10\n",
        "_last_sent = {}\n",
        "_lead_icon_re = re.compile(r\"^\\s*(?:[\\W_]\\ufe0f?)+\\s*\")\n",
        "def _strip_lead_icon(msg: str) -> str: return _lead_icon_re.sub(\"\", str(msg or \"\"))\n",
        "def _noisy_event(msg: str) -> bool:\n",
        "    keep = (\"BUY\",\"SELL\",\"EXIT\",\"TP\",\"SL\",\"filled\",\"placeOrder\",\"error\",\"⚠️\",\"🛑\",\"🔴\",\"🟢\")\n",
        "    return any(k in msg for k in keep)\n",
        "\n",
        "def notify_quiet(text):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        ai_callbacks.notify = notify_quiet\n",
        "        if 'engine' in globals(): engine.callbacks.notify = notify_quiet\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    msg = str(text or \"\"); base = _strip_lead_icon(msg); now = time.time()\n",
        "    if _noisy_event(msg): tg_send(msg); return\n",
        "    if globals().get(\"AI_MUTE\", False) and base.startswith(\"No trade:\"): return\n",
        "    for prefix, wait in _SUPPRESS_FOR.items():\n",
        "        if base.startswith(prefix):\n",
        "            last = _last_sent.get(prefix, 0.0)\n",
        "            if now - last < wait: return\n",
        "            _last_sent[prefix] = now; tg_send(msg); return\n",
        "    any_key = f\"any:{hash(base)}\"; last = _last_sent.get(any_key, 0.0)\n",
        "    if now - last < _SUPPRESS_ANY_WINDOW: return\n",
        "    _last_sent[any_key] = now; tg_send(msg)\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        ai_callbacks.notify = notify_quiet\n",
        "        if hasattr(engine, \"set_callbacks\"): engine.set_callbacks(ai_callbacks)\n",
        "        else: engine.callbacks.notify = notify_quiet\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Budget / style helpers already in earlier steps (we reuse) ---------------\n",
        "def ai_budget_set(amt: float):\n",
        "    global AI_BUDGET_INIT, AI_BUDGET_DATE, AI_START_BUDGET, HALTED_REASON, HALT_NOTE\n",
        "    amt = float(amt)\n",
        "    try: engine.set_remaining_capital(amt)\n",
        "    except Exception: engine._remaining_capital = amt\n",
        "    try: engine._starting_capital = amt\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    AI_BUDGET_INIT = amt; AI_START_BUDGET = amt; AI_BUDGET_DATE = datetime.now(IST).date()\n",
        "    if HALTED_REASON == \"profit\" and not _profit_target_hit():\n",
        "        HALTED_REASON, HALT_NOTE = None, \"\"\n",
        "    return f\"💰 AI budget for {AI_BUDGET_DATE} set to ₹{amt:.0f}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def ai_budget_show():\n",
        "    try: ai_budget_recalc_tick()\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    s = _budget_snapshot()\n",
        "    return (f\"🧳 Budget {dt.date.today().isoformat()}: \"\n",
        "            f\"init ₹{s['init']:.0f}, used ₹{s['used']:.0f}, realized ₹{s['realized']:.0f}, remaining ₹{s['remaining']:.0f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def ai_budget_reset():\n",
        "    engine.set_remaining_capital(float(globals().get(\"AI_BUDGET_INIT\", 0.0)))\n",
        "    return ai_budget_show()\n",
        "\n",
        "def ai_alloc(first_pct: float | None = None, max_pct: float | None = None):\n",
        "    if first_pct is not None: engine.cfg.first_trade_alloc_hint = float(first_pct)\n",
        "    if max_pct   is not None: engine.cfg.max_alloc_per_trade  = float(max_pct)\n",
        "    a = engine.cfg.first_trade_alloc_hint; b = engine.cfg.max_alloc_per_trade\n",
        "    return f\"📊 Alloc curve set → first={a:.2f}, max={b:.2f}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def ai_losscap_set(amt: float | None = None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    /losscap <amount>\n",
        "    - Updates the daily loss cap.\n",
        "    - If the cap is INCREASED and today's NET P&L is now within the new cap,\n",
        "      auto-resume AI (and clear cooldown) only if we were paused due to loss-cap.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    # query mode\n",
        "    if amt is None:\n",
        "        return f\"🛑 Daily loss cap: ₹{getattr(engine, '_daily_loss_cap', 0.0):.0f}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    # remember the old cap, set the new cap\n",
        "    old_cap = float(getattr(engine, \"_daily_loss_cap\", 0.0))\n",
        "    new_cap = float(amt)\n",
        "    if hasattr(engine, \"set_daily_loss_cap\"):\n",
        "        engine.set_daily_loss_cap(new_cap)\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        setattr(engine, \"_daily_loss_cap\", new_cap)\n",
        "\n",
        "    resumed = False\n",
        "\n",
        "    # only try to resume if user increased the cap\n",
        "    if new_cap > old_cap:\n",
        "        # figure out today's NET P&L using whatever your engine exposes\n",
        "        net_today = 0.0\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            if hasattr(engine, \"_net_pnl_today\"):\n",
        "                # expected to return (realized_today, net_today) or similar\n",
        "                tup = engine._net_pnl_today()\n",
        "                net_today = float(tup[1] if isinstance(tup, (list, tuple)) and len(tup) > 1 else float(tup))\n",
        "            elif hasattr(engine, \"today_pnl\"):\n",
        "                pnl = engine.today_pnl()  # expected dict {'net': ...}\n",
        "                net_today = float(pnl.get(\"net\", 0.0)) if isinstance(pnl, dict) else float(pnl)\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass  # default 0\n",
        "\n",
        "        # we only auto-resume if we were paused by loss-cap logic\n",
        "        loss_tripped  = bool(getattr(engine, \"_loss_tripped\", False))\n",
        "        pause_reason  = str(getattr(engine, \"_pause_reason\", \"\")) if hasattr(engine, \"_pause_reason\") else \"\"\n",
        "        profit_halt   = (globals().get(\"HALTED_REASON\") == \"profit\")  # don't override a profit latch\n",
        "\n",
        "        within_new_cap = net_today > -abs(new_cap)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if loss_tripped and within_new_cap and not profit_halt:\n",
        "            # clear latches and cooldown\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                setattr(engine, \"_loss_tripped\", False)\n",
        "                if hasattr(engine, \"_pause_reason\"):\n",
        "                    setattr(engine, \"_pause_reason\", \"\")\n",
        "                if hasattr(engine, \"_cooldown_until\"):\n",
        "                    setattr(engine, \"_cooldown_until\", 0)\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "            # enable AI back\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                if hasattr(engine, \"enable_ai\"):\n",
        "                    engine.enable_ai(True)\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    setattr(engine, \"_ai_enabled\", True)\n",
        "                resumed = True\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                resumed = False\n",
        "\n",
        "            # optional: quiet notify (best-effort)\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                if \"notify_quiet\" in globals():\n",
        "                    notify_quiet(f\"▶️ Loss cap raised to ₹{new_cap:.0f}; NET ₹{net_today:.0f} within limit — AI resumed.\")\n",
        "                elif \"tg_send\" in globals():\n",
        "                    tg_send(f\"▶️ Loss cap raised to ₹{new_cap:.0f}; AI resumed.\")\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    msg = f\"🛑 Daily loss cap set to ₹{new_cap:.0f}\"\n",
        "    if resumed:\n",
        "        msg += \" | ▶️ AI resumed\"\n",
        "    elif not getattr(engine, \"_ai_enabled\", True):\n",
        "        msg += \" (AI still paused; use /ai_on or /resume if needed.)\"\n",
        "    return msg\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "BUDGET_MODE = os.environ.get(\"BUDGET_MODE\", \"STATIC\").strip().upper()\n",
        "BROKERAGE_PER_TRADE = float(os.environ.get(\"BROKERAGE_PER_TRADE\", \"0\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Robust start budget ------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "def _as_float_safe(v):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if callable(v): v = v()\n",
        "        return float(v)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "if not isinstance(globals().get(\"AI_START_BUDGET\"), (int, float)) or globals().get(\"AI_START_BUDGET\", 0) <= 0:\n",
        "    eng = globals().get(\"engine\")\n",
        "    candidates = [\n",
        "        _as_float_safe(os.environ.get(\"AI_START_BUDGET\")),\n",
        "        _as_float_safe(os.environ.get(\"INIT_BUDGET\")),\n",
        "        _as_float_safe(os.environ.get(\"BUDGET_INIT\")),\n",
        "        _as_float_safe(globals().get(\"INIT_BUDGET\")),\n",
        "        _as_float_safe(globals().get(\"BUDGET_INIT\")),\n",
        "        _as_float_safe(getattr(eng, \"_starting_capital\", None) if eng is not None else None),\n",
        "        _as_float_safe(getattr(eng, \"_remaining_capital\", None) if eng is not None else None),\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "    AI_START_BUDGET = next((x for x in candidates if isinstance(x, (int, float)) and x and x > 0), 10000.0)\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Profit target ------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# --- Profit target (NET-aware) ------------------------------------------------\n",
        "PROFIT_TARGET_PCT = float(os.environ.get(\"PROFIT_TARGET_PCT\", \"20\"))\n",
        "PROFIT_GATE_ON_NET = True   # gate on NET (realized + open), not realized-only\n",
        "HALTED_REASON = None\n",
        "HALT_NOTE = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def _realized_today() -> float:\n",
        "    \"\"\"Today's realized P&L in ₹ (IST window, BUY/SELL sign, lot-sized).\n",
        "    Reads DB; falls back to DRY mirror only if DB is unavailable.\"\"\"\n",
        "    import os, math\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        db_path = getattr(engine.db, \"path\", None)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        db_path = None\n",
        "\n",
        "    if db_path and os.path.exists(db_path):\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            val = float(_db_sum_realized_today(db_path))\n",
        "            return val if math.isfinite(val) else 0.0\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Fallback only if DB not usable\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        return float(globals().get(\"DRY_REALIZED\", 0.0) or 0.0)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _open_pl_now() -> float:\n",
        "    \"\"\"Sum open P&L for DB OPEN rows using live LTP.\"\"\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        db_path = engine.db.path\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    import sqlite3, pandas as pd\n",
        "    con = sqlite3.connect(db_path); con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "    rows = con.cursor().execute(\n",
        "        \"SELECT symbol, qty, entry_price FROM trades WHERE status='OPEN'\"\n",
        "    ).fetchall()\n",
        "    con.close()\n",
        "    if not rows:\n",
        "        return 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    df = pd.DataFrame([dict(r) for r in rows])\n",
        "    df[\"qty\"] = df[\"qty\"].astype(int)\n",
        "    df[\"entry_price\"] = df[\"entry_price\"].astype(float)\n",
        "    df[\"lot\"] = df[\"symbol\"].apply(lot_size_for)\n",
        "    df[\"units\"] = df[\"qty\"] * df[\"lot\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # live quotes (fallback to entry if quote is missing)\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        ltps = ltp_batch(df[\"symbol\"].tolist()).set_index(\"symbol\")[\"ltp\"].astype(float)\n",
        "        df[\"ltp\"] = df[\"symbol\"].map(ltps.to_dict())\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        df[\"ltp\"] = None\n",
        "    df[\"ltp\"] = df[\"ltp\"].fillna(df[\"entry_price\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    return float(((df[\"ltp\"] - df[\"entry_price\"]) * df[\"units\"]).sum())\n",
        "\n",
        "def _profit_target_hit():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Return (hit, target_abs, realized, metric_used).\"\"\"\n",
        "    start = float(globals().get(\"AI_START_BUDGET\", 0.0))\n",
        "    if PROFIT_TARGET_PCT <= 0 or start <= 0:\n",
        "        return (False, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)\n",
        "\n",
        "    target = (PROFIT_TARGET_PCT / 100.0) * start\n",
        "    realized = _realized_today()\n",
        "    metric = realized + (_open_pl_now() if PROFIT_GATE_ON_NET else 0.0)\n",
        "    return (metric >= target, target, realized, metric)\n",
        "\n",
        "def profit_gate_tick(flatten: bool = True):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Shim that reflects the new profit-halt logic; no notify/flatten.\"\"\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        ai_budget_recalc_tick()\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        return bool(getattr(engine, \"_profit_halt\", False))\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        # Fallback compute (no messages)\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            pct = getattr(engine.cfg, \"profit_target_pct\", 10.0)   # accepts 10 or 0.10\n",
        "            thr = profit_target_rupees(pct)\n",
        "            realized = float(_db_sum_realized_today(engine.db.path))\n",
        "            halted = realized >= thr\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                engine._profit_halt = halted\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "            return halted\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            return False\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def profit_target_set(pct: float | None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Show or set the daily profit target percent.\"\"\"\n",
        "    global PROFIT_TARGET_PCT, HALTED_REASON, HALT_NOTE\n",
        "    if pct is None:\n",
        "        start = float(globals().get(\"AI_START_BUDGET\", 0.0))\n",
        "        target = (PROFIT_TARGET_PCT/100.0) * start if start > 0 else 0.0\n",
        "        hit, _, _, metric = _profit_target_hit()\n",
        "        status = 'HALTED' if HALTED_REASON=='profit' else ('reached ✅' if hit else 'active')\n",
        "        what = \"Net today\" if PROFIT_GATE_ON_NET else \"Realized today\"\n",
        "        return (f\"🎯 Profit target: {PROFIT_TARGET_PCT:.1f}% (₹{target:.0f} of ₹{start:.0f}). \"\n",
        "                f\"{what} ₹{metric:.0f}. Status: {status}.\")\n",
        "    # normalize: allow both \"10\" (10%) and \"0.10\" (10%)\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        pct = float(pct)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pct = 0.0\n",
        "    if 0.0 < pct < 1.0:\n",
        "        pct *= 100.0\n",
        "    PROFIT_TARGET_PCT = max(0.0, float(pct))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # if previously halted but now below target, allow resume\n",
        "    hit, *_ = _profit_target_hit()\n",
        "    if HALTED_REASON == \"profit\" and not hit:\n",
        "        HALTED_REASON, HALT_NOTE = None, \"\"\n",
        "    return profit_target_set(None)\n",
        "\n",
        "def resume_trading():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Manual clear of the profit-halt.\"\"\"\n",
        "    global HALTED_REASON, HALT_NOTE\n",
        "    HALTED_REASON = None; HALT_NOTE = \"\"\n",
        "    return \"▶️ Resumed. Profit gate cleared.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- HELP ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "HELP = (\n",
        "\"🤖 BOT COMMANDS (implemented):\\n\"\n",
        "\"/help • /status • /status_full • /pos • /pl • /flat • /auto_on • /auto_off • /dry_on • /dry_off • /live_sec N\\n\"\n",
        "\"/mkt • /market • /market_status\\n\"\n",
        "\"Universe: /ul <NIFTY|BANKNIFTY|FINNIFTY|ALL> • /ul_all • /nifty • /banknifty • /finnifty\\n\"\n",
        "\"Price: /ltp SYM1 [SYM2..] • /ohlc SYM1 [SYM2..]\\n\"\n",
        "\"Orders: /place SYMBOL QTY SIDE [MKT|LMT] [PRICE]\\n\"\n",
        "\"AI: /ai_on • /ai_off • /scan <sec> • /monitor <sec> • /ai_suggest • /why • /why_not SYMBOL • /history [n] • /eod_summary\\n\"\n",
        "\"Budget: /budget [₹] • /budget_mode STATIC|REINVEST • /budget_reset • /alloc <first max> • /losscap <₹>\\n\"\n",
        "\"Profit: /profit_target <pct> • /resume\\n\"\n",
        "\"Style: /style [scalp|momentum] • /be_at <R>\\n\"\n",
        "\"Perf: /perf <range> • /mistakes <range> • /stats\\n\"\n",
        "\"DB: /db [n|open|id:<id>] · /db_backup · /db_clear today | /db_clear YYYY-MM-DD | /db_clear all YES\\n\"\n",
        "\"Quiet: /mute on|off|toggle  (alias: /quiet)\\n\"\n",
        "\"Signals: /signals • /signals_on • /signals_off\\n\"\n",
        "\"Capture Data: /capture_data • /eod_capture — Capture today’s market data (1m/5m/15m)\\n\"\n",
        "\"/backtest <start_date> <end_date> [mode] [capital] — Run backtest (mode: rolling | fixed)\\n\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "def _ai_enabled() -> bool:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if hasattr(engine, \"is_ai_enabled\"): return bool(engine.is_ai_enabled())\n",
        "        if hasattr(engine, \"ai_enabled\"):\n",
        "            v = getattr(engine, \"ai_enabled\")\n",
        "            return bool(v() if callable(v) else v)\n",
        "        return bool(getattr(engine, \"_ai_enabled\", False))\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---- Allowlist + confirmations ----------------------------------------------\n",
        "def _parse_allowlist(s: str) -> set[str]:\n",
        "    return {x.strip() for x in str(s or \"\").split(\",\") if x.strip()}\n",
        "\n",
        "ALLOWLIST = _parse_allowlist(os.environ.get(\"TELEGRAM_ALLOWLIST\", \"\"))\n",
        "if CHAT_ID: ALLOWLIST.add(str(CHAT_ID))\n",
        "def is_allowed(chat_id) -> bool: return not ALLOWLIST or str(chat_id) in ALLOWLIST\n",
        "\n",
        "# ask_confirm/_confirmed are defined in earlier step; we use them here.\n",
        "\n",
        "def apply_boot_defaults():\n",
        "    global AUTO_MODE, PROFIT_TARGET_PCT, BUDGET_MODE, STYLE\n",
        "    AUTO_MODE = True; PROFIT_TARGET_PCT = DEFAULTS[\"PROFIT_TARGET_PCT\"]\n",
        "    BUDGET_MODE = DEFAULTS[\"BUDGET_MODE\"]\n",
        "    ai_budget_set(DEFAULTS[\"START_BUDGET\"])\n",
        "    set_underlyings(DEFAULTS[\"UNIVERSE\"])\n",
        "    STYLE = DEFAULTS[\"STYLE\"].upper(); _apply_style(SCALP if STYLE==\"SCALP\" else MOMO)\n",
        "    try: engine.enable_ai(True)\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        resume_from_db()\n",
        "        tg_send(\"♻️ Rebuilt open positions & budget from DB.\")\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(\"[reseed] skipped:\", e)\n",
        "\n",
        "def status_text(full: bool = False) -> str:\n",
        "    auto = \"ON\" if AUTO_MODE else \"OFF\"\n",
        "    dry  = \"ON\" if DRY_RUN else \"OFF\"\n",
        "    ai   = \"ON\" if _ai_enabled() else \"OFF\"\n",
        "    live = LIVE_POLL_SEC; posn = len(positions); uni  = len(UNIVERSE)\n",
        "    try: uls = \", \".join(sorted(set(DF_UNIVERSE[\"underlying\"].astype(str).unique())))\n",
        "    except Exception: uls = \"?\"\n",
        "    try: ai_budget_recalc_tick()\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    s = _budget_snapshot()\n",
        "    start, used, realized, rem = float(s[\"init\"]), float(s[\"used\"]), float(s[\"realized\"]), float(s[\"remaining\"])\n",
        "    halt = (HALTED_REASON or \"none\")\n",
        "    style_str = (f\"{STYLE} | RR {RR_LOW:.2f}-{RR_HIGH:.2f} | ATR_SL {ATR_SL_LOW:.2f}-{ATR_SL_HIGH:.2f} | \"\n",
        "                 f\"TRAIL_K {TRAIL_K:.2f} | BE@{BE_AT_R:.2f}R\")\n",
        "    lines = [\n",
        "        f\"Status | AUTO={auto} | AI={ai} | DRY={dry} | live={live}s | pos={posn} | universe={uni} ({uls})\",\n",
        "        f\"Budget: mode={BUDGET_MODE} | init ₹{start:.0f} | used ₹{used:.0f} | realized ₹{realized:.0f} | remaining ₹{rem:.0f}\",\n",
        "        f\"Profit target: {PROFIT_TARGET_PCT:.1f}% | halt={halt}\",\n",
        "        f\"Style: {style_str}\",\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "    if full:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            scan = int(engine.get_scan_interval_seconds()); mon  = int(engine.get_monitor_interval_seconds())\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"AI intervals: scan={scan}s, monitor={mon}s\")\n",
        "        except Exception: pass\n",
        "        lines.append(f\"Risk: MAX_QTY={MAX_QTY}, MAX_OPEN_POSITIONS={MAX_OPEN_POSITIONS}, COOLDOWN_SEC={COOLDOWN_SEC}\")\n",
        "        lines.append(f\"Market: {MKT_START}-{MKT_END} IST\")\n",
        "    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ==== Telegram cleanup DB (unchanged) ----------------------------------------\n",
        "import sqlite3, datetime as _dt\n",
        "_TELE_BOT   = os.environ.get(\"TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN\", \"\").strip()\n",
        "_TELE_CHAT  = os.environ.get(\"TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID\", \"\").strip()\n",
        "_IST        = _dt.timezone(_dt.timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))\n",
        "_TG_DB_PATH = \"bot_runtime.sqlite\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def _tgdb_exec(sql, args=()):\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(_TG_DB_PATH) as conn:\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"PRAGMA journal_mode=DELETE;\")\n",
        "        conn.execute(\"PRAGMA synchronous=FULL;\")\n",
        "        cur = conn.cursor()\n",
        "        cur.execute(\"\"\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS telegram_msgs(\n",
        "                         id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n",
        "                         chat_id TEXT, msg_id INTEGER, d TEXT\n",
        "                       )\"\"\")\n",
        "        cur.execute(sql, args); conn.commit(); return cur.fetchall()\n",
        "\n",
        "def set_tp(sym: str, new_tp: float):\n",
        "    p = positions.get(sym)\n",
        "    if not p:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "    old = float(p[\"tp\"])\n",
        "    p[\"tp\"] = float(new_tp)\n",
        "    tg_send(f\"🔧 Revised TP for {sym}: {old:.2f} → {float(new_tp):.2f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def tg_send_logged(text: str, disable_notification: bool = False):\n",
        "    if not _TELE_BOT or not _TELE_CHAT:\n",
        "        print(\"[tg] skipped send (missing TELEGRAM creds):\", str(text)[:120]); return None\n",
        "    url = f\"https://api.telegram.org/bot{_TELE_BOT}/sendMessage\"\n",
        "    payload = {\"chat_id\": _TELE_CHAT, \"text\": str(text),\n",
        "               \"disable_notification\": bool(disable_notification), \"parse_mode\": \"HTML\"}\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        r = SESSION.post(url, json=payload, timeout=20); js = r.json()\n",
        "        if not js.get(\"ok\"): print(\"[tg] send failed:\", js); return None\n",
        "        mid = (js.get(\"result\") or {}).get(\"message_id\")\n",
        "        if mid is not None:\n",
        "            today = _dt.datetime.now(_IST).date().isoformat()\n",
        "            _tgdb_exec(\"INSERT INTO telegram_msgs(chat_id, msg_id, d) VALUES(?,?,?)\", (_TELE_CHAT, int(mid), today))\n",
        "        return mid\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(\"[tg] send exception:\", e); return None\n",
        "\n",
        "def telegram_cleanup_if_morning():\n",
        "    now = _dt.datetime.now(_IST)\n",
        "    cutoff = now.replace(hour=9, minute=15, second=0, microsecond=0)\n",
        "    if now >= cutoff: return\n",
        "    today = now.date().isoformat()\n",
        "    rows  = _tgdb_exec(\"SELECT chat_id, msg_id, d FROM telegram_msgs WHERE d <> ?\", (today,))\n",
        "    if not rows: return\n",
        "    del_url = f\"https://api.telegram.org/bot{_TELE_BOT}/deleteMessage\"; deleted, failed = 0, 0\n",
        "    for chat_id, msg_id, d in rows:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            r = SESSION.post(del_url, json={\"chat_id\": chat_id, \"message_id\": int(msg_id)}, timeout=15)\n",
        "            try: ok = bool(r.json().get(\"ok\"))\n",
        "            except Exception: ok = False\n",
        "            if ok: deleted += 1\n",
        "            else:  failed  += 1\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            failed += 1\n",
        "    _tgdb_exec(\"DELETE FROM telegram_msgs WHERE d <> ?\", (today,))\n",
        "    print(f\"[tg] cleanup done (pre-09:15 IST): deleted={deleted}, failed={failed}, kept=today\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---- Boot & banner -----------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "print(\"✅ Dynamic SL/TP patch loaded: ATR+swing entry, BE, trailing, and no-carry EOD flat.\")\n",
        "print(f\"🤖 Engine online. AUTO={'ON' if AUTO_MODE else 'OFF'} DRY={'ON' if DRY_RUN else 'OFF'} live={LIVE_POLL_SEC}s | universe={len(UNIVERSE)}\")\n",
        "try: telegram_cleanup_if_morning()\n",
        "except Exception as _e: print(\"[tg] cleanup skipped:\", _e)\n",
        "tg_send(\"Bot online. /help for commands.\")\n",
        "apply_boot_defaults()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "import time\n",
        "\n",
        "BOOTSTRAP_SYMBOLS = UNIVERSE[:20]\n",
        "\n",
        "for sym in BOOTSTRAP_SYMBOLS:\n",
        "    tok = TOKMAP.get(sym)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if tok:\n",
        "        df = bootstrap_historical(tok)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if df is not None:\n",
        "            SYMBOL_DATA[str(tok)] = df\n",
        "\n",
        "        time.sleep(0.35)   # prevent Angel rate limit\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"✅ Historical bootstrap complete\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Helpers for text parsing -------------------------------------------------\n",
        "def _parse_syms_list(tail: str):\n",
        "    syms = [s.strip().upper() for s in tail.replace(\",\", \" \").split() if s.strip()]\n",
        "    return [s for s in syms if s]\n",
        "\n",
        "# =============== SINGLE, CRASH-SAFE TELEGRAM COMMAND ROUTER ===================\n",
        "def _status_text_router(full: bool = False) -> str:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if 'send_status_from_db_s3' in globals():\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                return send_status_from_db_s3(return_text=True, universe_sz=len(UNIVERSE)) if not full else status_text(True)\n",
        "            except TypeError:\n",
        "                send_status_from_db_s3(universe_sz=len(UNIVERSE))\n",
        "                return \"\"\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "    return status_text(full)\n",
        "\n",
        "def _positions_text_router() -> str:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if 'acct_snapshot_from_db_s3' in globals():\n",
        "            snap = acct_snapshot_from_db_s3()\n",
        "            if snap and snap.get(\"pos_count\", 0) > 0:\n",
        "                return \"Positions:\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(snap.get(\"lines\", []))\n",
        "            return \"No positions.\"\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        out = handle_ai_command(\"/pos\"); return out or \"No positions.\"\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return \"No positions.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def _pnl_text_router() -> str:\n",
        "    \"\"\"P&L today: realized (CLOSED+DRY) + live mark-to-market for OPEN; net = realized + open.\"\"\"\n",
        "    # Always use the engine DB so /pl matches /status_full and /pos\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        db_path = engine.db.path\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return \"P&L: DB not ready.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    # --- 1) Realized today (CLOSED + DRY etc.; i.e., anything that's not OPEN) ---\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        realized = float(_db_sum_realized_today(db_path))  # we already made this count status!='OPEN'\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        realized = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    # --- 2) Open P&L from OPEN rows using live LTP ---\n",
        "    import sqlite3, pandas as pd\n",
        "    open_pl = 0.0\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        con = sqlite3.connect(db_path); con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "        rows = con.cursor().execute(\n",
        "            \"SELECT symbol, side, qty, entry_price FROM trades WHERE status='OPEN'\"\n",
        "        ).fetchall()\n",
        "        con.close()\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        rows = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if rows:\n",
        "        df = pd.DataFrame([dict(r) for r in rows])\n",
        "        if not df.empty:\n",
        "            # normalize and compute units\n",
        "            df[\"symbol\"] = df[\"symbol\"].astype(str)\n",
        "            df[\"side\"]   = df[\"side\"].fillna(\"BUY\").str.upper()\n",
        "            df[\"qty\"]    = pd.to_numeric(df[\"qty\"], errors=\"coerce\").fillna(0).astype(int)\n",
        "            df[\"entry_price\"] = pd.to_numeric(df[\"entry_price\"], errors=\"coerce\").fillna(0.0)\n",
        "\n",
        "            # lot → units\n",
        "            df[\"lot\"]   = df[\"symbol\"].apply(lot_size_for).astype(int)\n",
        "            df[\"units\"] = df[\"qty\"] * df[\"lot\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "            # get live LTPs (fallback to entry price if anything is missing)\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                ltps = ltp_batch(df[\"symbol\"].unique().tolist()).set_index(\"symbol\")[\"ltp\"]\n",
        "                ltps = pd.to_numeric(ltps, errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "                df[\"ltp\"] = df[\"symbol\"].map(ltps.to_dict())\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                df[\"ltp\"] = None\n",
        "            df[\"ltp\"] = pd.to_numeric(df[\"ltp\"], errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "            df[\"ltp\"] = df[\"ltp\"].fillna(df[\"entry_price\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "            # BUY: (ltp - entry) * units, SELL: (entry - ltp) * units\n",
        "            buy_mask  = df[\"side\"].eq(\"BUY\")\n",
        "            sell_mask = df[\"side\"].eq(\"SELL\")\n",
        "            df[\"pnl_open\"] = 0.0\n",
        "            df.loc[buy_mask,  \"pnl_open\"] = (df.loc[buy_mask,  \"ltp\"] - df.loc[buy_mask,  \"entry_price\"]) * df.loc[buy_mask,  \"units\"]\n",
        "            df.loc[sell_mask, \"pnl_open\"] = (df.loc[sell_mask, \"entry_price\"] - df.loc[sell_mask, \"ltp\"]) * df.loc[sell_mask, \"units\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "            open_pl = float(df[\"pnl_open\"].sum())\n",
        "\n",
        "    # --- 3) Summary ---\n",
        "    fees = 0.0\n",
        "    net  = realized + open_pl - fees\n",
        "\n",
        "    # nice formatting (open can be negative)\n",
        "    return (\"📊 P&L today:\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"• Realized: ₹{realized:.2f}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"• Open:     ₹{open_pl:+.2f}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"• Fees:     ₹{fees:.2f}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"• Net:      ₹{net:.2f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Backtest DB + Drive paths (drop-in block) ----------\n",
        "def _bt_db_path():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Use the SAME SQLite DB the engine uses (single source of truth).\"\"\"\n",
        "    return getattr(engine.db, \"path\", AI_DB_PATH)\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Drive paths for backtest ingest/archiving (works in both Colab mounts) ---\n",
        "import os\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "\n",
        "def _drive_root():\n",
        "    if os.path.ismount(\"/gdrive\"):                  # some notebooks mount as /gdrive\n",
        "        return \"/gdrive\"\n",
        "    if os.path.exists(\"/content/drive/MyDrive\"):    # classic Colab mount\n",
        "        return \"/content/drive\"\n",
        "    return \"/gdrive\"\n",
        "\n",
        "BACKTEST_DIR = f\"{_drive_root()}/MyDrive/trading/backtests\"\n",
        "ARCHIVE_DIR  = os.path.join(BACKTEST_DIR, \"archive\")\n",
        "os.makedirs(ARCHIVE_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "ARCHIVE_ROOT = ARCHIVE_DIR  # alias used later in code\n",
        "\n",
        "# === BACKTEST HELPERS: ingest CSVs into DB and archive processed files ===\n",
        "import re, shutil\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    import pandas as pd\n",
        "except ImportError:\n",
        "    import sys, subprocess\n",
        "    subprocess.run([sys.executable, \"-m\", \"pip\", \"install\", \"pandas\", \"-q\"], check=False)\n",
        "    import pandas as pd\n",
        "\n",
        "def _bt_parse_date_from_filename(fname: str):\n",
        "    # e.g., GFDLNFO_OPTIONS_01072025.csv -> 01-07-2025\n",
        "    m = re.findall(r\"(\\d{8})\", fname)\n",
        "    if not m:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "    for cand in reversed(m):\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            return datetime.strptime(cand, \"%d%m%Y\")\n",
        "        except ValueError:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "    return None\n",
        "\n",
        "def _bt_normalize_bhavcopy(df_raw):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Accepts either:\n",
        "      A) NSE OPTIONS bhavcopy (instrument/symbol/expiry_dt/strike_pr/option_typ/open/high/low/close/ttl_trd_qnty)\n",
        "      B) Simple OHLC with columns like: Ticker, Date, Time, Open, High, Low, Close, Volume\n",
        "    Returns columns: ['symbol','expiry','strike','option_type','date','open','high','low','close','volume']\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    import pandas as pd\n",
        "    df = df_raw.copy()\n",
        "    df.columns = [c.strip().lower() for c in df.columns]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ---------- Mode A: OPTIONS bhavcopy ----------\n",
        "    need_a = {\"instrument\",\"symbol\",\"expiry_dt\",\"strike_pr\",\"option_typ\",\"open\",\"high\",\"low\",\"close\"}\n",
        "    if need_a.issubset(df.columns):\n",
        "        out = df[list(need_a)].rename(columns={\n",
        "            \"expiry_dt\": \"expiry\",\n",
        "            \"strike_pr\": \"strike\",\n",
        "            \"option_typ\": \"option_type\",\n",
        "        })\n",
        "        out[\"volume\"] = pd.to_numeric(df.get(\"ttl_trd_qnty\", 0), errors=\"coerce\").fillna(0).astype(\"Int64\")\n",
        "        # 'date' will be supplied by filename/mtime later if missing\n",
        "        out[\"date\"] = pd.NaT\n",
        "        return out[[\"symbol\",\"expiry\",\"strike\",\"option_type\",\"date\",\"open\",\"high\",\"low\",\"close\",\"volume\"]]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ---------- Mode B: simple OHLC ----------\n",
        "    def pick(*alts):\n",
        "        for a in alts:\n",
        "            if a in df.columns: return a\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    c_sym   = pick(\"ticker\",\"symbol\",\"tradingsymbol\",\"ts\")\n",
        "    c_date  = pick(\"date\",\"dt\")\n",
        "    c_time  = pick(\"time\",\"timestamp\",\"time_ist\")\n",
        "    c_open  = pick(\"open\",\"o\")\n",
        "    c_high  = pick(\"high\",\"h\")\n",
        "    c_low   = pick(\"low\",\"l\")\n",
        "    c_close = pick(\"close\",\"c\")\n",
        "    c_vol   = pick(\"volume\",\"vol\",\"qty\",\"ttl_trd_qnty\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Minimal requirements for this layout\n",
        "    if not (c_sym and c_date and c_open and c_close):\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    out = pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "    out[\"symbol\"] = df[c_sym].astype(str)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Combine Date + Time if we have both; else use Date\n",
        "    if c_time:\n",
        "        dt_str = df[c_date].astype(str).str.strip() + \" \" + df[c_time].astype(str).str.strip()\n",
        "        dt_parsed = pd.to_datetime(dt_str, dayfirst=True, errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        dt_parsed = pd.to_datetime(df[c_date].astype(str), dayfirst=True, errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "    out[\"date\"] = dt_parsed.dt.date\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Prices / volume\n",
        "    out[\"open\"]  = pd.to_numeric(df[c_open],  errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "    out[\"high\"]  = pd.to_numeric(df[c_high],  errors=\"coerce\") if c_high  else pd.NA\n",
        "    out[\"low\"]   = pd.to_numeric(df[c_low],   errors=\"coerce\")  if c_low   else pd.NA\n",
        "    out[\"close\"] = pd.to_numeric(df[c_close], errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "    out[\"volume\"]= pd.to_numeric(df[c_vol],   errors=\"coerce\").fillna(0).astype(\"Int64\") if c_vol else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Fields not present in this layout\n",
        "    out[\"expiry\"]      = pd.NaT\n",
        "    out[\"strike\"]      = pd.NA\n",
        "    out[\"option_type\"] = pd.NA\n",
        "\n",
        "    return out[[\"symbol\",\"expiry\",\"strike\",\"option_type\",\"date\",\"open\",\"high\",\"low\",\"close\",\"volume\"]]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def ingest_backtest_files(move_to_archive: bool = True):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Load any CSV in BACKTEST_DIR into backtest_ohlc (insert-or-ignore), then archive the file.\"\"\"\n",
        "    os.makedirs(ARCHIVE_ROOT, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "    files = [f for f in os.listdir(BACKTEST_DIR) if f.lower().endswith(\".csv\")]\n",
        "    if not files:\n",
        "        print(\"📂 No CSVs to ingest.\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    import sqlite3\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(_bt_db_path()) as con:\n",
        "        cur = con.cursor()\n",
        "        for fname in sorted(files):\n",
        "            fpath = os.path.join(BACKTEST_DIR, fname)\n",
        "            if os.path.isdir(fpath):  # skip dirs\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"➡️  Processing {fname} …\")\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                raw = pd.read_csv(fpath)\n",
        "            except Exception as e:\n",
        "                print(f\"  ⚠️  Read error: {e}\")\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            df = _bt_normalize_bhavcopy(raw)\n",
        "            if df is None:\n",
        "                print(\"  ⚠️  Missing required columns; skipped.\")\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "            # ---------- CLEAN & FILL DATES ----------\n",
        "            # If file name has a date, use it to fill any missing/NaT dates\n",
        "            file_dt = _bt_parse_date_from_filename(fname)\n",
        "            if file_dt is None:\n",
        "                file_dt = datetime.fromtimestamp(os.path.getmtime(fpath))\n",
        "            file_day = file_dt.date()\n",
        "\n",
        "            # Coerce to proper dtypes\n",
        "            df[\"expiry\"] = pd.to_datetime(df[\"expiry\"], errors=\"coerce\").dt.date\n",
        "            df[\"date\"]   = pd.to_datetime(df[\"date\"],   errors=\"coerce\").dt.date\n",
        "            df.loc[df[\"date\"].isna(), \"date\"] = file_day\n",
        "\n",
        "            # Keep only essential columns and rows for this CSV shape\n",
        "            df = df[[\"symbol\",\"expiry\",\"strike\",\"option_type\",\"date\",\"open\",\"high\",\"low\",\"close\",\"volume\"]]\n",
        "            df = df.dropna(subset=[\"symbol\",\"date\",\"open\",\"close\"])  # DO NOT require expiry/strike/option_type here\n",
        "\n",
        "            if df.empty:\n",
        "                print(\"  ⚠️  No valid rows after normalization; skipped.\")\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "            # stage + INSERT OR IGNORE for idempotent loads\n",
        "            cur.execute(\"DROP TABLE IF EXISTS backtest_ohlc_stage;\")\n",
        "            df.to_sql(\"backtest_ohlc_stage\", con, if_exists=\"replace\", index=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "            # add timeframe column if it doesn't exist\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                cur.execute(\"ALTER TABLE backtest_ohlc ADD COLUMN timeframe TEXT DEFAULT '1d';\")\n",
        "            except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "            # create unique index if not present\n",
        "            cur.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_backtest_unique\n",
        "                ON backtest_ohlc(symbol, expiry, strike, option_type, date, timeframe);\n",
        "            \"\"\")\n",
        "            cur.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "                INSERT INTO backtest_ohlc\n",
        "                    (symbol, expiry, strike, option_type, date, time,\n",
        "                    open, high, low, close, volume, open_interest, timeframe)\n",
        "                SELECT symbol, expiry, strike, option_type, date, time,\n",
        "                      open, high, low, close, volume, open_interest, timeframe\n",
        "                FROM backtest_ohlc_stage\n",
        "                ON CONFLICT(symbol, expiry, strike, option_type, date, timeframe)\n",
        "                DO UPDATE SET\n",
        "                    time = excluded.time,\n",
        "                    open = excluded.open,\n",
        "                    high = excluded.high,\n",
        "                    low = excluded.low,\n",
        "                    close = excluded.close,\n",
        "                    volume = excluded.volume,\n",
        "                    open_interest = excluded.open_interest;\n",
        "            \"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            inserted = cur.rowcount or 0\n",
        "            con.commit()\n",
        "            cur.execute(\"DROP TABLE IF EXISTS backtest_ohlc_stage;\")\n",
        "            print(f\"  ✅ Inserted new rows: {inserted}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if move_to_archive:\n",
        "                # archive into /archive/YYYYMMDD/filename.csv\n",
        "                arch_day = file_dt.strftime(\"%Y%m%d\")\n",
        "                arch_dir = os.path.join(ARCHIVE_DIR, arch_day)\n",
        "                os.makedirs(arch_dir, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "                dest = os.path.join(arch_dir, fname)\n",
        "                if os.path.exists(dest):\n",
        "                    base, ext = os.path.splitext(fname)\n",
        "                    dest = os.path.join(arch_dir, f\"{base}_{int(datetime.now().timestamp())}{ext}\")\n",
        "                shutil.move(fpath, dest)\n",
        "                print(f\"  📦 Moved to archive: {dest}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"🎯 Ingest complete.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_backtest(start_date, end_date, mode=\"rolling\", init_capital=10000):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Improved backtest with SL/TP and slippage simulation.\"\"\"\n",
        "    import sqlite3\n",
        "\n",
        "    with sqlite3.connect(_bt_db_path()) as con:\n",
        "        q = f\"\"\"\n",
        "          SELECT date, symbol, strike, option_type, open, high, low, close\n",
        "          FROM backtest_ohlc\n",
        "          WHERE date BETWEEN '{start_date}' AND '{end_date}'\n",
        "          ORDER BY date;\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        df = pd.read_sql(q, con, parse_dates=[\"date\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    if df.empty:\n",
        "        return None, {\"error\":\"No data for given range.\"}\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "    capital = init_capital\n",
        "\n",
        "    SL_PCT = 0.02\n",
        "    TP_PCT = 0.03\n",
        "\n",
        "    SLIPPAGE_ENTRY = 0.003\n",
        "    SLIPPAGE_EXIT  = 0.005\n",
        "\n",
        "    for day, grp in df.groupby(\"date\"):\n",
        "        day_return = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "        for _, row in grp.iterrows():\n",
        "            entry = float(row[\"open\"])\n",
        "            high  = float(row[\"high\"])\n",
        "            low   = float(row[\"low\"])\n",
        "            close = float(row[\"close\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "            sl = entry * (1 - SL_PCT)\n",
        "            tp = entry * (1 + TP_PCT)\n",
        "\n",
        "            hit_sl = low <= sl\n",
        "            hit_tp = high >= tp\n",
        "\n",
        "            if hit_sl and hit_tp:\n",
        "                # assume TP hit first if close > entry\n",
        "                exit_price = tp if close > entry else sl\n",
        "\n",
        "            elif hit_sl:\n",
        "                exit_price = sl\n",
        "            elif hit_tp:\n",
        "                exit_price = tp\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                exit_price = close\n",
        "\n",
        "            # ---- Slippage Adjustment ----\n",
        "            entry = entry * (1 + SLIPPAGE_ENTRY)\n",
        "            exit_price = exit_price * (1 - SLIPPAGE_EXIT)\n",
        "\n",
        "            trade_ret = (exit_price - entry) / entry\n",
        "            day_return += trade_ret\n",
        "\n",
        "        if mode == \"rolling\":\n",
        "            pnl = capital * day_return\n",
        "            capital += pnl\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            pnl = init_capital * day_return\n",
        "            capital += pnl\n",
        "\n",
        "        results.append({\n",
        "            \"date\": day,\n",
        "            \"daily_ret\": day_return,\n",
        "            \"pnl\": pnl,\n",
        "            \"capital\": capital\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ---- AFTER LOOP ----\n",
        "    out = pd.DataFrame(results).sort_values(\"date\")\n",
        "    out[\"cum_pnl\"] = out[\"pnl\"].cumsum()\n",
        "    out[\"win\"] = (out[\"daily_ret\"] > 0).astype(int)\n",
        "\n",
        "    summary = {\n",
        "        \"TotalDays\": len(out),\n",
        "        \"Wins\": int(out[\"win\"].sum()),\n",
        "        \"Losses\": int(len(out) - out[\"win\"].sum()),\n",
        "        \"WinRate\": float(out[\"win\"].mean()*100),\n",
        "        \"TotalPNL\": float(out[\"cum_pnl\"].iloc[-1]),\n",
        "        \"FinalCapital\": float(out[\"capital\"].iloc[-1] if mode==\"rolling\"\n",
        "                              else init_capital + out[\"cum_pnl\"].iloc[-1]),\n",
        "        \"Mode\": mode,\n",
        "        \"Start\": str(pd.to_datetime(start_date).date()),\n",
        "        \"End\": str(pd.to_datetime(end_date).date()),\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    out.to_csv(f\"/content/backtest_result_{mode}.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "    return out, summary\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# === PERF / MISTAKES / STATS (adds engine.performance|mistakes|stats) ===\n",
        "def _rng_to_datetimes(rng: str):\n",
        "    from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n",
        "    IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))\n",
        "    today = datetime.now(IST).date()\n",
        "    def _at(d, h=0, m=0, s=0):\n",
        "        return datetime(d.year, d.month, d.day, h, m, s, tzinfo=IST)\n",
        "    rng = (rng or \"today\").strip().lower()\n",
        "    if \"..\" in rng:\n",
        "        a,b = [x.strip() for x in rng.split(\"..\",1)]\n",
        "        sa = datetime.fromisoformat(a+\"T00:00:00+05:30\")\n",
        "        sb = datetime.fromisoformat(b+\"T23:59:59+05:30\")\n",
        "        return sa, sb\n",
        "    if rng in (\"today\",\"td\"):        return _at(today,0,0), _at(today,23,59,59)\n",
        "    if rng in (\"yesterday\",\"yd\"):    d=today-timedelta(days=1); return _at(d,0,0), _at(d,23,59,59)\n",
        "    if rng in (\"week\",\"thisweek\"):\n",
        "        d0 = today - timedelta(days=today.weekday()); d1 = d0 + timedelta(days=6)\n",
        "        return _at(d0,0,0), _at(d1,23,59,59)\n",
        "    if rng in (\"month\",\"thismonth\"):\n",
        "        d0 = today.replace(day=1)\n",
        "        if d0.month == 12: d1 = d0.replace(year=d0.year+1, month=1, day=1) - timedelta(days=1)\n",
        "        else:              d1 = d0.replace(month=d0.month+1, day=1) - timedelta(days=1)\n",
        "        return _at(d0,0,0), _at(d1,23,59,59)\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        d = datetime.fromisoformat(rng).date()\n",
        "        return _at(d,0,0), _at(d,23,59,59)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return _at(today,0,0), _at(today,23,59,59)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _fetch_trades_between(start_dt, end_dt):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Return a DataFrame of CLOSED (non-OPEN) trades between start_dt..end_dt.\n",
        "    Uses a half-open [start, end) window. If the inputs are naive, we assume IST,\n",
        "    then convert the window to UTC because the DB stores UTC-naive ISO strings.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    import sqlite3, pandas as pd, os\n",
        "    from datetime import timezone, timedelta\n",
        "\n",
        "    # --- helpers ---\n",
        "    IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _to_utc_naive_iso(dt_aware):\n",
        "        # Treat naive datetimes as IST (trading-day expectation)\n",
        "        if dt_aware.tzinfo is None:\n",
        "            dt_aware = dt_aware.replace(tzinfo=IST)\n",
        "        return (\n",
        "            dt_aware.astimezone(timezone.utc)\n",
        "            .replace(tzinfo=None)\n",
        "            .strftime(\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S\")\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _to_utc_pd_ts(dt_aware):\n",
        "        if dt_aware.tzinfo is None:\n",
        "            dt_aware = dt_aware.replace(tzinfo=IST)\n",
        "        return pd.Timestamp(\n",
        "            dt_aware.astimezone(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Build UTC half-open window [start, end)\n",
        "    start_iso = _to_utc_naive_iso(start_dt)\n",
        "    end_iso   = _to_utc_naive_iso(end_dt)  # half-open achieved by '< end_iso' in SQL\n",
        "\n",
        "    path = getattr(engine.db, \"path\", None)\n",
        "    if path and os.path.exists(path):\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            con = sqlite3.connect(path); con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "            cur = con.cursor()\n",
        "            q = \"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT ts, symbol, side, qty, entry_price, exit_price, pnl, reason, status\n",
        "            FROM trades\n",
        "            WHERE status!='OPEN' AND ts >= ? AND ts < ?\n",
        "            ORDER BY ts ASC\n",
        "            \"\"\"\n",
        "            rows = cur.execute(q, (start_iso, end_iso)).fetchall()\n",
        "            con.close()\n",
        "            if not rows:\n",
        "                return pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "            df = pd.DataFrame([dict(r) for r in rows])\n",
        "            for c in (\"qty\",\"entry_price\",\"exit_price\",\"pnl\"):\n",
        "                if c in df.columns:\n",
        "                    df[c] = pd.to_numeric(df[c], errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "            return df\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass  # fall through to history fallback\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Fallback to fetch_history() if DB not reachable\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        rows = (engine.db.fetch_history(5000) or [])\n",
        "        if not rows:\n",
        "            return pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n",
        "        rename = {\"price_entry\":\"entry_price\",\"price_exit\":\"exit_price\",\"tradingsymbol\":\"symbol\"}\n",
        "        df.rename(columns={k:v for k,v in rename.items() if k in df.columns}, inplace=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"ts\" in df.columns:\n",
        "            # Filter in UTC using the same half-open window\n",
        "            df[\"ts\"] = pd.to_datetime(df[\"ts\"], errors=\"coerce\", utc=False)\n",
        "            start_utc = _to_utc_pd_ts(start_dt)\n",
        "            end_utc   = _to_utc_pd_ts(end_dt)\n",
        "            m = (df[\"ts\"] >= start_utc) & (df[\"ts\"] < end_utc)\n",
        "            df = df.loc[m].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "        for c in (\"qty\",\"entry_price\",\"exit_price\",\"pnl\"):\n",
        "            if c in df.columns:\n",
        "                df[c] = pd.to_numeric(df[c], errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "        return df\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _fmt_money(x):\n",
        "    try: return f\"₹{float(x):.2f}\"\n",
        "    except: return \"₹0.00\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def _perf_text(df, title):\n",
        "    import numpy as np\n",
        "    if df.empty: return f\"{title}: no trades.\"\n",
        "    CLOSED_STATUSES = {\"CLOSED\",\"FILLED\",\"DRY\",\"EXITED\",\"SQRD\",\"SQUAREDOFF\",\"FLAT\"}\n",
        "    if \"status\" not in df.columns:\n",
        "        df[\"status\"] = \"CLOSED\"\n",
        "    df[\"status\"] = df[\"status\"].astype(str).str.upper()\n",
        "    closed = df[df[\"status\"].isin(CLOSED_STATUSES)].copy()\n",
        "    # (optional but recommended) only count trades that actually have an exit price\n",
        "    if \"exit_price\" in closed.columns:\n",
        "        closed = closed[closed[\"exit_price\"].notna()].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if closed.empty: return f\"{title}: no CLOSED trades.\"\n",
        "    n = len(closed)\n",
        "    wins = (closed[\"pnl\"] > 0).sum()\n",
        "    wr = 100.0 * wins / max(1, n)\n",
        "    gross_win  = closed.loc[closed[\"pnl\"]>0, \"pnl\"].sum()\n",
        "    gross_loss = -closed.loc[closed[\"pnl\"]<0, \"pnl\"].sum()\n",
        "    pf = (gross_win / gross_loss) if gross_loss > 0 else float(\"inf\")\n",
        "    avg = closed[\"pnl\"].mean()\n",
        "    best = closed[\"pnl\"].max()\n",
        "    worst = closed[\"pnl\"].min()\n",
        "    by_sym = closed.groupby(\"symbol\")[\"pnl\"].sum().sort_values(ascending=False) if \"symbol\" in closed.columns else None\n",
        "    top = \", \".join([f\"{k}:{_fmt_money(v)}\" for k,v in (by_sym.head(3).items() if by_sym is not None else [])])\n",
        "    bot = \", \".join([f\"{k}:{_fmt_money(v)}\" for k,v in (by_sym.tail(3).items() if by_sym is not None else [])])\n",
        "    return (\n",
        "        f\"{title}\\n\"\n",
        "        f\"• Trades: {n} | Win%: {wr:.1f}% | PF: {pf:.2f}\\n\"\n",
        "        f\"• Gross: +{_fmt_money(gross_win)} / -{_fmt_money(gross_loss)} | Avg: {_fmt_money(avg)}\\n\"\n",
        "        f\"• Best: {_fmt_money(best)} | Worst: {_fmt_money(worst)}\\n\"\n",
        "        f\"• Top symbols: {top or 'n/a'}\\n\"\n",
        "        f\"• Weak symbols: {bot or 'n/a'}\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "def _mistakes_text(df, title):\n",
        "    import numpy as np, pandas as pd\n",
        "    if df.empty: return f\"{title}: no trades.\"\n",
        "    if \"status\" not in df.columns: df[\"status\"] = \"CLOSED\"\n",
        "    CLOSED_STATUSES = {\"CLOSED\",\"FILLED\",\"DRY\",\"EXITED\",\"SQRD\",\"SQUAREDOFF\",\"FLAT\"}\n",
        "    if \"status\" not in df.columns:\n",
        "        df[\"status\"] = \"CLOSED\"\n",
        "    df[\"status\"] = df[\"status\"].astype(str).str.upper()\n",
        "    closed = df[df[\"status\"].isin(CLOSED_STATUSES)].copy()\n",
        "    if \"exit_price\" in closed.columns:\n",
        "        closed = closed[closed[\"exit_price\"].notna()].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # now compute mistakes only from truly-closed trades\n",
        "    loss = closed[pd.to_numeric(closed[\"pnl\"], errors=\"coerce\") < 0].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if loss.empty: return f\"{title}: no losing trades.\"\n",
        "    by_reason = (\n",
        "        loss.groupby(loss[\"reason\"].fillna(\"unknown\"))[\"pnl\"]\n",
        "            .agg(count=\"count\", total=\"sum\")\n",
        "            .sort_values(\"total\")\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    worst_r = \"; \".join(\n",
        "        f\"{idx}: {cnt} trades, {_fmt_money(total)}\"\n",
        "        for idx, cnt, total in by_reason.head(5).itertuples()\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    by_sym = loss.groupby(\"symbol\")[\"pnl\"].sum().sort_values().head(5) if \"symbol\" in loss.columns else None\n",
