        "    worst_s = \"; \".join([f\"{k}:{_fmt_money(v)}\" for k,v in (by_sym.items() if by_sym is not None else [])])\n",
        "    return (\n",
        "        f\"{title}\\n\"\n",
        "        f\"• Loss reasons (worst first): {worst_r}\\n\"\n",
        "        f\"• Worst symbols: {worst_s or 'n/a'}\\n\"\n",
        "        f\"• Tip: review setups & exits for the above buckets.\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "def _lifetime_stats_text():\n",
        "    import sqlite3, pandas as pd\n",
        "    path = getattr(engine.db, \"path\", None)\n",
        "    if not path: return \"stats not available.\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        con = sqlite3.connect(path); con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "        # include DRY/CLOSED (anything not OPEN)\n",
        "        rows = con.execute(\"\"\"\n",
        "            SELECT date(ts) AS d, pnl, status\n",
        "            FROM trades\n",
        "            WHERE status!='OPEN'\n",
        "        \"\"\").fetchall()\n",
        "        con.close()\n",
        "        if not rows: return \"stats not available.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        df = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=[\"d\",\"pnl\",\"status\"])\n",
        "        df[\"pnl\"] = pd.to_numeric(df[\"pnl\"], errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # We already filtered status!='OPEN' in SQL, so just group by day\n",
        "        days = df.groupby(\"d\")[\"pnl\"].sum().sort_index()\n",
        "        total = df[\"pnl\"].sum()\n",
        "        best_day = days.max() if not days.empty else 0.0\n",
        "        worst_day = days.min() if not days.empty else 0.0\n",
        "        win_days = (days > 0).sum()\n",
        "        loss_days = (days < 0).sum()\n",
        "        wr_days = 100.0 * win_days / max(1, (win_days+loss_days))\n",
        "\n",
        "        return (\n",
        "            \"📈 Lifetime stats\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"• Net P&L: {_fmt_money(total)} | Days: {len(days)} | \"\n",
        "            f\"Win-days: {win_days} | Loss-days: {loss_days} | Win-day%: {wr_days:.1f}%\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"• Best day: {_fmt_money(best_day)} | Worst day: {_fmt_money(worst_day)}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return \"stats not available.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _perf_api(rng=\"today\"):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        s,e = _rng_to_datetimes(rng)\n",
        "        df = _fetch_trades_between(s,e)\n",
        "        return _perf_text(df, f\"🧪 Performance ({rng})\")\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        return f\"🧪 Performance ({rng}): n/a ({e})\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def _mistakes_api(rng=\"today\"):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        s,e = _rng_to_datetimes(rng)\n",
        "        df = _fetch_trades_between(s,e)\n",
        "        return _mistakes_text(df, f\"🧯 Mistakes ({rng})\")\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        return f\"🧯 Mistakes ({rng}): n/a ({e})\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def _stats_api():\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        return _lifetime_stats_text()\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        return f\"stats not available ({e})\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# attach to engine if possible\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    engine.performance = _perf_api\n",
        "    engine.mistakes    = _mistakes_api\n",
        "    engine.stats       = _stats_api\n",
        "except Exception:\n",
        "    pass\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_broker_positions_safe():\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if \"get_positions\" in globals():\n",
        "            return get_positions()  # your broker API\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(\"⚠️ Broker position fetch failed:\", e)\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "def _eod_summary_api():\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        s, e = _rng_to_datetimes(\"today\")\n",
        "        df = _fetch_trades_between(s, e)\n",
        "        import pandas as pd\n",
        "        if df.empty:\n",
        "            return \"EOD summary: no trades today.\"\n",
        "        df[\"pnl\"] = pd.to_numeric(df[\"pnl\"], errors=\"coerce\")\n",
        "        df[\"status\"] = df[\"status\"].astype(str).str.upper()\n",
        "        CLOSED_STATUSES = {\"CLOSED\",\"FILLED\",\"DRY\",\"EXITED\",\"SQRD\",\"SQUAREDOFF\",\"FLAT\"}\n",
        "        closed = df[df[\"status\"].isin(CLOSED_STATUSES)].copy()\n",
        "        # require exit_price to exist so we don't count partials\n",
        "        if \"exit_price\" in closed.columns:\n",
        "            closed = closed[closed[\"exit_price\"].notna()].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if closed.empty:\n",
        "            return \"EOD summary: no CLOSED trades today.\"\n",
        "        realized = float(closed[\"pnl\"].sum())\n",
        "        wins = int((closed[\"pnl\"] > 0).sum())\n",
        "        losses = int((closed[\"pnl\"] < 0).sum())\n",
        "        trades = len(closed)\n",
        "        fees = float(BROKERAGE_PER_TRADE) * trades\n",
        "        net = realized - fees\n",
        "        best = float(closed[\"pnl\"].max())\n",
        "        worst = float(closed[\"pnl\"].min())\n",
        "        return (\n",
        "            \"🧾 EOD Summary\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"• Trades: {trades} | Wins: {wins} | Losses: {losses}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"• Realized: {_fmt_money(realized)} | Fees: {_fmt_money(fees)} | Net: {_fmt_money(net)}\\n\"\n",
        "            f\"• Best trade: {_fmt_money(best)} | Worst trade: {_fmt_money(worst)}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        return f\"EOD summary not available ({e})\"\n",
        "# ---- Confirmation system ----\n",
        "_PENDING_ACTIONS = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "def ask_confirm(action_key, message):\n",
        "    _PENDING_ACTIONS[\"last\"] = action_key\n",
        "    return f\"{message}\\n\\nReply with /confirm to proceed.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def handle_confirm():\n",
        "    action = _PENDING_ACTIONS.pop(\"last\", None)\n",
        "    if not action:\n",
        "        return \"No pending action.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if action == \"flat\":\n",
        "        # Close all positions\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            engine.cb.flatten_all(\"MANUAL\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            return \"✅ All positions closed.\"\n",
        "        except Exception as e:\n",
        "            return f\"Error closing positions: {e}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if action == \"dry_off\":\n",
        "        globals()[\"DRY_RUN\"] = False\n",
        "        return \"🚨 DRY MODE OFF — LIVE TRADING ENABLED\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return \"Unknown action.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def route_command(text: str) -> str | None:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        t = (text or \"\").strip()\n",
        "        if not t: return None\n",
        "\n",
        "        # confirmation replies first\n",
        "        if '_confirmed' in globals():\n",
        "            if _confirmed(t, \"flat\"):\n",
        "                try: exit_all(\"user_confirm\")\n",
        "                except Exception as e: return f\"Close failed: {e}\"\n",
        "                return \"🧹 Closing all open positions…\"\n",
        "            if _confirmed(t, \"dry_off\"):\n",
        "                globals()[\"DRY_RUN\"] = False\n",
        "                return \"DRY OFF ⚠️ LIVE ORDERS ENABLED\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        parts = t.split(); cmd = parts[0].lower(); args = parts[1:]\n",
        "\n",
        "        # DB/maintenance delegated to engine's AI router early\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/db\", \"/db_clear\", \"/dbclear\", \"/db_backup\", \"/budget_dbg\"):\n",
        "            return handle_ai_command(text)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Core\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/help\", \"help\"):       return HELP\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/status\":               return _status_text_router(False)\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/status_full\":          return _status_text_router(True)\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/pos\":\n",
        "            # Prefer engine._positions; fall back to global positions\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                pos_map = dict(getattr(engine, \"_positions\", {}))\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pos_map = {}\n",
        "            if not pos_map and \"positions\" in globals():\n",
        "                pos_map = dict(positions or {})\n",
        "            if not pos_map:\n",
        "                return \"No positions.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            syms = list(pos_map.keys())\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                df_ltp = ltp_batch(syms)\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                df_ltp = pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "\n",
        "            lines = [\"Positions:\"]\n",
        "            total_open = 0.0\n",
        "            for s, p in pos_map.items():\n",
        "                q_lots = int(p.get(\"qty\", 0))\n",
        "                ent    = float(p.get(\"entry\", 0.0))\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    ltp = float(df_ltp.loc[s, \"ltp\"]) if (not df_ltp.empty and s in df_ltp.index) else ent\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    ltp = ent\n",
        "                units  = q_lots * lot_size_for(s)\n",
        "                pnl    = (ltp - ent) * units\n",
        "                total_open += pnl\n",
        "                lines.append(f\"{s} qty(lots)={q_lots} entry={ent:.2f} ltp={ltp:.2f} P/L={pnl:.2f}\")\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"Open P/L total: ₹{total_open:.2f}\")\n",
        "            return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/confirm\":    return handle_confirm()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/pl\",\"/pnl\"):          return _pnl_text_router()\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/mkt\",\"/market\",\"/market_status\"): return market_status_text(force=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Danger ops with confirmation\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/flat\":\n",
        "            return ask_confirm(\"flat\", \"⚠️ Close ALL positions now?\")\n",
        "        # Execution control\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/auto_on\":\n",
        "            AUTO_MODE = True\n",
        "            return \"AUTO MODE ON ✅\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/auto_off\":\n",
        "            AUTO_MODE = False\n",
        "            return \"AUTO MODE OFF ⛔\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ---- Dynamic Underlying Switch ----\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/trade\":\n",
        "            if len(parts) < 2:\n",
        "                return \"Usage: /trade NIFTY | BANKNIFTY | FINNIFTY | SENSEX\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            underlying = parts[1].upper()\n",
        "\n",
        "            if \"FULL_NORM\" not in globals():\n",
        "                return \"Universe not initialized. Run Step 3.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            available = set(FULL_NORM[\"underlying\"].unique())\n",
        "\n",
        "            if underlying not in available:\n",
        "                return f\"{underlying} not available in master.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            if positions:\n",
        "                return \"Close open positions before switching.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            rebuild_universe([underlying])\n",
        "            restart_ws()\n",
        "\n",
        "            return f\"AI now trading: {underlying} ✅\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Paper vs Live\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/dry_on\":\n",
        "            DRY_RUN = True\n",
        "            return \"DRY MODE ON (Paper)\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/dry_off\":\n",
        "            globals()[\"ORDERS_ENABLED\"] = True\n",
        "            globals()[\"DRY_RUN\"] = False\n",
        "            return \"LIVE MODE ENABLED ⚠️\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Signal printing only\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/signals_on\":\n",
        "            SIGNALS_PRINT = True\n",
        "            return \"Signals printing ON\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/signals_off\":\n",
        "            SIGNALS_PRINT = False\n",
        "            return \"Signals printing OFF\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/signals\":\n",
        "            if not EXTERNAL_SIGNALS:\n",
        "                return \"No signals yet.\"\n",
        "            rows = sorted(EXTERNAL_SIGNALS.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1][\"v\"], reverse=True)[:10]\n",
        "            lines = [f\"{s}: v={d['v']} · {d['why']} · {d['ts']}\" for s, d in rows]\n",
        "            return \"🧭 Signals (top):\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/live_sec\":\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                globals()[\"LIVE_POLL_SEC\"]=max(2,int(args[0])); return f\"Live interval set to {LIVE_POLL_SEC}s\"\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                return \"Usage: /live_sec N (>=2)\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Profit target\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/profit_target\":\n",
        "            import sqlite3, pandas as pd, numpy as np\n",
        "\n",
        "            # --- setter: accept \"/profit_target 20\" (percent) or \"/profit_target 0.2\" (fraction)\n",
        "            argstr = \" \".join(args).strip() if args else \"\"\n",
        "            if argstr:\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    new_pct = float(argstr)\n",
        "                    if new_pct > 1.0:  # given as percent\n",
        "                        new_pct = new_pct / 100.0\n",
        "                    # engine.cfg is already used below for reading; update it here:\n",
        "                    setattr(engine.cfg, \"profit_target_pct\", new_pct)\n",
        "                    # optional: clear any profit latch\n",
        "                    if globals().get(\"HALTED_REASON\") == \"profit\":\n",
        "                        globals()[\"HALTED_REASON\"] = None\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    return \"Usage: /profit_target <percent>  e.g. /profit_target 20\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            s   = _budget_snapshot()  # uses persisted budget for today\n",
        "            pct = float(getattr(engine.cfg, \"profit_target_pct\", 0.10) or 0.0)\n",
        "            gate_on_net = bool(getattr(engine.cfg, \"profit_gate_on_net\", True))\n",
        "            target = pct * float(s[\"init\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "            # realized today\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                realized = float(_realized_today())\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                realized = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "            # open P&L (from DB OPEN rows, using live LTP) — FIX: include side sign\n",
        "            open_pl = 0.0\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                con = sqlite3.connect(engine.db.path); con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n",
        "                rows = con.cursor().execute(\n",
        "                    \"SELECT symbol, qty, entry_price, side FROM trades WHERE status='OPEN'\"\n",
        "                ).fetchall()\n",
        "                con.close()\n",
        "                if rows:\n",
        "                    df = pd.DataFrame([dict(r) for r in rows])\n",
        "                    if not df.empty:\n",
        "                        df[\"qty\"] = df[\"qty\"].astype(int).abs()\n",
        "                        df[\"entry_price\"] = df[\"entry_price\"].astype(float)\n",
        "                        df[\"side\"] = df[\"side\"].astype(str).str.upper().fillna(\"BUY\")\n",
        "                        df[\"lot\"] = df[\"symbol\"].apply(lot_size_for)\n",
        "                        df[\"units\"] = df[\"qty\"] * df[\"lot\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "                        try:\n",
        "                            ltps = ltp_batch(df[\"symbol\"].tolist()).set_index(\"symbol\")[\"ltp\"].astype(float)\n",
        "                            df[\"ltp\"] = df[\"symbol\"].map(ltps.to_dict())\n",
        "                        except Exception:\n",
        "                            df[\"ltp\"] = None\n",
        "                        df[\"ltp\"] = df[\"ltp\"].fillna(df[\"entry_price\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "                        # BUY: (ltp - entry) * units | SELL: (entry - ltp) * units\n",
        "                        df[\"mult\"] = np.where(df[\"side\"] == \"SELL\", -1.0, 1.0)\n",
        "                        df[\"pnl_open\"] = (df[\"ltp\"] - df[\"entry_price\"]) * df[\"units\"] * df[\"mult\"]\n",
        "                        open_pl = float(df[\"pnl_open\"].sum())\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "            metric  = realized + open_pl if gate_on_net else realized\n",
        "            status  = \"reached ✅\" if metric >= target else \"active\"\n",
        "            metric_name = \"Net\" if gate_on_net else \"Realized\"\n",
        "            return (f\"🍀 Profit target: {pct*100:.1f}% (₹{target:.0f} of ₹{s['init']:.0f}). \"\n",
        "                    f\"{metric_name} today ₹{metric:.0f}. Status: {status}.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/resume\": return resume_trading()\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Budget / risk\n",
        "        # in handle_ai_command()\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/budget\":\n",
        "            if len(parts) == 2:\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    amt = float(parts[1])\n",
        "                    globals()[\"AI_START_BUDGET\"] = amt\n",
        "                    try: _meta_set(_today_key(\"budget_init\"), amt)  # <-- persist for today\n",
        "                    except Exception: pass\n",
        "                    ai_budget_recalc_tick()\n",
        "                    s = _budget_snapshot()\n",
        "                    return (f\"🧳 Budget set: init ₹{s['init']:.0f}, used ₹{s['used']:.0f}, \"\n",
        "                            f\"realized ₹{s['realized']:.0f}, remaining ₹{s['remaining']:.0f}\")\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    pass\n",
        "            s = _budget_snapshot()\n",
        "            return (f\"🧳 Budget {dt.date.today().isoformat()}: mode=STATIC | init ₹{s['init']:.0f}, \"\n",
        "                    f\"used ₹{s['used']:.0f}, realized ₹{s['realized']:.0f}, fees ₹0, \"\n",
        "                    f\"remaining ₹{s['remaining']:.0f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/budget_reset\":\n",
        "            try: path = engine.db.path\n",
        "            except Exception: return \"DB not ready.\"\n",
        "            base = float(globals().get(\"AI_START_BUDGET\", 0.0))\n",
        "            globals()[\"AI_START_BUDGET\"] = base - _db_sum_realized_today(path)\n",
        "            try: _meta_set(_today_key(\"budget_init\"), globals()[\"AI_START_BUDGET\"])  # <-- persist reset\n",
        "            except Exception: pass\n",
        "            ai_budget_recalc_tick()\n",
        "            s = _budget_snapshot()\n",
        "            return (f\"♻️ Budget reset for today. init ₹{s['init']:.0f}, used ₹{s['used']:.0f}, \"\n",
        "                    f\"realized ₹{s['realized']:.0f}, remaining ₹{s['remaining']:.0f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/budget_mode\":\n",
        "            if not args: return f\"Budget mode: {BUDGET_MODE}\"\n",
        "            bm = args[0].strip().upper()\n",
        "            if bm not in (\"STATIC\",\"REINVEST\"): return \"Use STATIC or REINVEST\"\n",
        "            globals()[\"BUDGET_MODE\"] = bm; return f\"Budget mode set: {bm}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/alloc\":\n",
        "            if not args: return ai_alloc(None, None)\n",
        "            if len(args)==2:\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    first=float(args[0]); maxp=float(args[1])\n",
        "                    if not (0<=first<=1 and 0<=maxp<=1): return \"Use 0..1 fractions, e.g. /alloc 0.35 0.70\"\n",
        "                    return ai_alloc(first, maxp)\n",
        "                except Exception: return \"Usage: /alloc <first 0..1> <max 0..1>\"\n",
        "            return \"Usage: /alloc <first 0..1> <max 0..1>\"\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/losscap\":\n",
        "            if not args: return ai_losscap_set(None)\n",
        "            try: return ai_losscap_set(float(args[0]))\n",
        "            except Exception: return \"Usage: /losscap <amount in ₹>\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Universe\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/ul\":      return set_underlyings(t.split(\" \",1)[1] if \" \" in t else \"\")\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/ul_all\":  return set_underlyings(\"ALL\")\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/nifty\",\"/banknifty\",\"/finnifty\"): return set_underlyings(cmd.replace(\"/\",\"\").upper())\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Style\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/style\":\n",
        "            if not args: return f\"Style: {STYLE}\"\n",
        "            st = args[0].strip().upper()\n",
        "            if st not in (\"SCALP\",\"MOMENTUM\",\"MOMO\"): return \"Use scalp|momentum\"\n",
        "            if st == \"MOMO\": st = \"MOMENTUM\"\n",
        "            globals()[\"STYLE\"] = \"SCALP\" if st==\"SCALP\" else \"MOMENTUM\"\n",
        "            _apply_style(SCALP if globals()[\"STYLE\"]==\"SCALP\" else MOMO)\n",
        "            return f\"Style set: {globals()['STYLE']}\"\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/be_at\":\n",
        "            if not args: return f\"BE_AT currently {BE_AT_R:.2f}R\"\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                globals()[\"BE_AT_R\"] = float(args[0]); return f\"BE_AT set to {BE_AT_R:.2f}R\"\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                return \"Usage: /be_at <R multiple>\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Prices\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/ltp\":\n",
        "            if not args: return \"Usage: /ltp SYMBOL [SYMBOL ...]\"\n",
        "            syms = _parse_syms_list(\" \".join(args))\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                df = ltp_batch(syms)\n",
        "                lines = [f\"{r['symbol']}: {fmt(r['ltp'])}\" for _, r in df.iterrows()]\n",
        "                return \"LTPs:\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "            except Exception as e:\n",
        "                return f\"ltp error: {e}\"\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/ohlc\":\n",
        "            if not args: return \"Usage: /ohlc SYMBOL [SYMBOL ...]\"\n",
        "            syms = _parse_syms_list(\" \".join(args))\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                df = ohlc_batch(syms)\n",
        "                lines = [f\"{r['symbol']}: O={fmt(r['open'])} H={fmt(r['high'])} L={fmt(r['low'])} C={fmt(r['close'])}\" for _, r in df.iterrows()]\n",
        "                return \"OHLC:\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "            except Exception as e:\n",
        "                return f\"ohlc error: {e}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Orders\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/place\":\n",
        "            if len(args) < 3: return \"Usage: /place SYMBOL QTY SIDE [MKT|LMT] [PRICE]\"\n",
        "            sym = args[0].upper()\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                qty  = int(args[1]); side = args[2].upper()\n",
        "                typ  = \"MKT\"; px = \"0\"\n",
        "                if len(args) >= 4: typ = args[3].upper()\n",
        "                if len(args) >= 5: px  = args[4]\n",
        "                if typ not in (\"MKT\",\"LMT\"): return \"ordertype must be MKT or LMT\"\n",
        "                if side not in (\"BUY\",\"SELL\"): return \"SIDE must be BUY or SELL\"\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                return \"Usage: /place SYMBOL QTY SIDE [MKT|LMT] [PRICE]\"\n",
        "            if side == \"BUY\" and not can_trade_now(): return f\"Market closed: {market_status_text()}\"\n",
        "            po = place_order(sym, side, qty, typ, px)\n",
        "            return f\"Order: {'OK' if po.get('ok') else 'FAIL'} {'[DRY]' if DRY_RUN else ''} {po}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        # AI controls\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/ai_on\":\n",
        "            try: engine.enable_ai(True); return \"AI ON ✅\"\n",
        "            except Exception: return \"AI enable failed.\"\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/ai_off\":\n",
        "            try: engine.enable_ai(False); return \"AI OFF ⏹️\"\n",
        "            except Exception: return \"AI disable failed.\"\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/scan\":\n",
        "            if not args:\n",
        "                try: return f\"scan interval={engine.get_scan_interval_seconds()}s\"\n",
        "                except Exception: return \"scan interval: n/a\"\n",
        "            try: sec = int(args[0]); engine.set_scan_interval_seconds(sec); return f\"scan={sec}s\"\n",
        "            except Exception: return \"Usage: /scan <sec>\"\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/monitor\":\n",
        "            if not args:\n",
        "                try: return f\"monitor interval={engine.get_monitor_interval_seconds()}s\"\n",
        "                except Exception: return \"monitor interval: n/a\"\n",
        "            try: sec = int(args[0]); engine.set_monitor_interval_seconds(sec); return f\"monitor={sec}s\"\n",
        "            except Exception: return \"Usage: /monitor <sec>\"\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/ai_suggest\":\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                out = (getattr(engine, \"ai_suggest\", None) or getattr(ai, \"suggest\", None))\n",
        "                res = out() if callable(out) else None\n",
        "                return str(res) if res is not None else \"No suggestion.\"\n",
        "            except Exception as e:\n",
        "                return f\"ai_suggest error: {e}\"\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/why\":\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                exp = (getattr(engine, \"last_explanation\", None) or getattr(ai, \"last_explanation\", None))\n",
        "                if callable(exp): exp = exp()\n",
        "                return str(exp) if exp else \"No explanation yet.\"\n",
        "            except Exception as e:\n",
        "                return f\"why error: {e}\"\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/history\":\n",
        "            n = 50\n",
        "            if len(args) >= 1:\n",
        "                try: n = int(args[0])\n",
        "                except Exception: pass\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                rows = engine.db.fetch_history(n) or []\n",
        "                if not rows: return \"No history.\"\n",
        "                lines = []\n",
        "                for r in rows:\n",
        "                    s = r.get(\"symbol\") or r.get(\"tradingsymbol\") or \"?\"\n",
        "                    lines.append(f\"{r.get('side','?')} {s} @ {r.get('price','?')} P/L {r.get('pnl','?')}\")\n",
        "                return \"History:\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(lines[:200])\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                return \"History not available.\"\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/eod_summary\":\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                f = getattr(engine.db, \"eod_summary\", None)\n",
        "                if callable(f):\n",
        "                    return str(f())\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "            # Fallback\n",
        "            return _eod_summary_api()\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Diagnostics / why_not\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/ai_status\",\"/ai_state\"): return f\"AI is {'ON' if _ai_enabled() else 'OFF'}.\"\n",
        "        if cmd == \"/why_not\":\n",
        "            if len(args)<1: return \"Usage: /why_not SYMBOL\"\n",
        "            s = args[0].strip().upper(); v,w = votes(s); req = 2\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                ldf = ltp_batch([s]); ltp = ldf[\"ltp\"].iloc[0] if not ldf.empty else None\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                ltp = None\n",
        "            need = _required_cash(s, MAX_QTY, ltp) if ltp is not None else float(\"inf\")\n",
        "            have = _remaining_capital()\n",
        "            budg_txt = (f\"need=₹{0 if math.isinf(need) else need:.0f} have=₹{have:.0f} \"\n",
        "                        f\"short=₹{max(0, (0 if math.isinf(need) else need) - have):.0f}\")\n",
        "            gates = [\n",
        "                f\"AUTO={'ON' if AUTO_MODE else 'OFF'}\",\n",
        "                f\"profit_halt={'YES' if HALTED_REASON=='profit' else 'NO'}\",\n",
        "                f\"can_trade={'YES' if can_trade_now() else 'NO'}\",\n",
        "                f\"positions={len(positions)}/{MAX_OPEN_POSITIONS}\",\n",
        "                f\"in_top12={'YES' if s in UNIVERSE[:12] else 'NO'}\",\n",
        "                f\"votes={v} need≥{req}  [{w if w else 'none'}]\",\n",
        "                f\"budget: {budg_txt}\"\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "            return \" | \".join(gates)\n",
        "        # /perf [/today|/yesterday|/week|/month]\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/perf\",):\n",
        "            rng = args[0] if args else \"today\"\n",
        "            return engine.performance(rng)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # /mistakes [/today|/yesterday|/week|/month]\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/mistakes\",):\n",
        "            rng = args[0] if args else \"today\"\n",
        "            return engine.mistakes(rng)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # /stats (lifetime)\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/stats\",):\n",
        "            return engine.stats()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/capture_data\", \"/eod_capture\"):\n",
        "            capture_eod_candles()\n",
        "            return \"📊 EOD data capture started (1m/5m/15m).\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "        # /bt_ingest — load CSVs from Drive and archive them\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/bt_ingest\",):\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                ingest_backtest_files()\n",
        "                return \"📥 Ingest complete. Files archived.\"\n",
        "            except Exception as e:\n",
        "                return f\"⚠️ Ingest error: {e}\"\n",
        "        # /bt_check 2025-07-01 2025-07-31\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/bt_check\",):\n",
        "            if len(args) < 2:\n",
        "                return \"Usage: /bt_check <YYYY-MM-DD> <YYYY-MM-DD>\"\n",
        "            s, e = args[0], args[1]\n",
        "            import sqlite3\n",
        "            db = getattr(engine.db, \"path\", AI_DB_PATH)\n",
        "            with sqlite3.connect(db) as con:\n",
        "                n = con.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM backtest_ohlc WHERE date BETWEEN ? AND ?\", (s, e)).fetchone()[0]\n",
        "                dmin, dmax = con.execute(\"SELECT MIN(date), MAX(date) FROM backtest_ohlc\").fetchone()\n",
        "            return f\"Rows in range {s}→{e}: {n}\\nTable range: {dmin} → {dmax}\\nDB: {db}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "        # /backtest 2025-07-01 2025-07-31 [rolling|fixed] [capital]\n",
        "        # or /backtest start=2025-07-01 end=2025-07-31 mode=rolling capital=10000\n",
        "        if cmd in (\"/backtest\",):\n",
        "            # defaults\n",
        "            start = end = None\n",
        "            mode = \"rolling\"\n",
        "            capital = 10000.0\n",
        "\n",
        "            # detect style\n",
        "            if any(\"=\" in a for a in args):\n",
        "                # key=value style\n",
        "                kv = {}\n",
        "                for a in args:\n",
        "                    if \"=\" in a:\n",
        "                        k, v = a.split(\"=\", 1)\n",
        "                        kv[k.strip().lower()] = v.strip()\n",
        "                start   = kv.get(\"start\", start)\n",
        "                end     = kv.get(\"end\", end)\n",
        "                mode    = kv.get(\"mode\", mode).lower()\n",
        "                if \"capital\" in kv:\n",
        "                    try: capital = float(kv[\"capital\"])\n",
        "                    except: pass\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                # positional style\n",
        "                if len(args) >= 1: start = args[0]\n",
        "                if len(args) >= 2: end   = args[1]\n",
        "                if len(args) >= 3: mode  = args[2].lower()\n",
        "                if len(args) >= 4:\n",
        "                    try: capital = float(args[3])\n",
        "                    except: pass\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not start or not end:\n",
        "                return (\"Usage:\\n\"\n",
        "                        \"/backtest <YYYY-MM-DD> <YYYY-MM-DD> [rolling|fixed] [capital]\\n\"\n",
        "                        \"or /backtest start=YYYY-MM-DD end=YYYY-MM-DD mode=rolling capital=10000\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            out, summary = run_backtest(start, end, mode, capital)\n",
        "            if summary and \"error\" not in summary:\n",
        "                return (\n",
        "                    \"📊 Backtest Results\\n\"\n",
        "                    f\"Range: {summary['Start']} → {summary['End']}  |  Mode: {summary['Mode']}\\n\"\n",
        "                    f\"Days: {summary['TotalDays']}  |  Wins: {summary['Wins']}  |  Losses: {summary['Losses']}  |  \"\n",
        "                    f\"WinRate: {summary['WinRate']:.1f}%\\n\"\n",
        "                    f\"Total P&L: ₹{summary['TotalPNL']:.2f}  |  Final Capital: ₹{summary['FinalCapital']:.2f}\\n\"\n",
        "                    f\"(CSV: /content/backtest_result_{mode}.csv)\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                return \"⚠️ No data for given range.\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Fallback to engine router\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            r = handle_ai_command(text)\n",
        "            if isinstance(r, str) and r: return r\n",
        "        except Exception: pass\n",
        "\n",
        "        return \"Unknown. /help\"\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        import traceback\n",
        "        tb = traceback.format_exc()\n",
        "        print(\"‼️ route_command crashed:\", e, tb)\n",
        "        return f\"⚠️ Command error: {e}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# Optional guard: if core engine AI is ON, force this cell to advisor-only\n",
        "def _core_ai_on() -> bool:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        return bool(engine.is_ai_enabled()) if hasattr(engine, \"is_ai_enabled\") else bool(getattr(engine, \"_ai_enabled\", False))\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "# ================= TELEGRAM WRAPPER =================\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "import requests\n",
        "\n",
        "def tg_get_updates(offset=None, timeout=10):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        url = f\"https://api.telegram.org/bot{BOT_TOKEN}/getUpdates\"\n",
        "        params = {\"timeout\": timeout, \"offset\": offset}\n",
        "        r = requests.get(url, params=params, timeout=timeout + 5)\n",
        "        js = r.json()\n",
        "        if not js.get(\"ok\"):\n",
        "            return []\n",
        "        return js.get(\"result\", [])\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(\"Telegram getUpdates error:\", e)\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- MAIN LOOP ---------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "offset = None\n",
        "_last_tick = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "while True:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        # 1) Telegram updates\n",
        "        for u in tg_get_updates(offset=offset, timeout=1):\n",
        "            offset = u[\"update_id\"] + 1\n",
        "\n",
        "            msg = u.get(\"message\") or u.get(\"edited_message\")\n",
        "            if not msg:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            chat_id = str((msg.get(\"chat\") or {}).get(\"id\"))\n",
        "            if not is_allowed(chat_id):\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            globals()[\"_CUR_CHAT_ID\"] = chat_id\n",
        "            txt = (msg.get(\"text\") or \"\").strip()\n",
        "            if not txt:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            resp = route_command(txt)\n",
        "            if isinstance(resp, str) and resp:\n",
        "                tg_send(resp)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # 2) Engine tick control\n",
        "        now = time.time()\n",
        "        if now - _last_tick < LIVE_POLL_SEC:\n",
        "            time.sleep(0.5)\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        _last_tick = now\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            ai_budget_recalc_tick()\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Profit guard\n",
        "        halted = profit_gate_tick()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if halted:\n",
        "            if AUTO_MODE:\n",
        "                AUTO_MODE = False\n",
        "                tg_send(HALT_NOTE or \"⏸️ Paused by profit guard.\")\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            # Auto-resume if previously paused by profit\n",
        "            if not AUTO_MODE and HALTED_REASON != \"manual\":\n",
        "                AUTO_MODE = True\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    tg_send(HALT_NOTE or \"⏸️ Paused by guard.\")\n",
        "                except Exception:\n",
        "                    pass\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not AUTO_MODE:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not can_trade_now():\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Advisor mode: compute signals\n",
        "        recenter_universe()\n",
        "        nifty_syms = [s for s in UNIVERSE if \"NIFTY\" in s]\n",
        "        sensex_syms = [s for s in UNIVERSE if \"SENSEX\" in s]\n",
        "\n",
        "        candidates = nifty_syms[:6] + sensex_syms[:6]\n",
        "        scored = []\n",
        "\n",
        "        for sym in candidates:\n",
        "            if sym in engine._positions:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            v, why = votes(sym)\n",
        "            print(f\"🧠 STRATEGY CHECK → {sym} | votes={v} | why={why}\")\n",
        "            scored.append((sym, v, why))\n",
        "\n",
        "        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n",
        "        update_signals_from_scored(scored)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # EOD guard\n",
        "        hh, mm = map(int, MKT_END.split(\":\"))\n",
        "        mins_to_close = (\n",
        "            datetime.now(IST).replace(\n",
        "                hour=hh, minute=mm, second=0, microsecond=0\n",
        "            ) - datetime.now(IST)\n",
        "        ).total_seconds() / 60.0\n",
        "\n",
        "        if mins_to_close <= 10:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Entry decisions\n",
        "        for sym, v, why in scored[:3]:\n",
        "\n",
        "            if sym in engine._positions:\n",
        "                print(\"⚠️ Position already open:\", sym)\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            if sym in engine._traded_today:\n",
        "                print(\"⚠️ Already traded today:\", sym)\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            res = ai_entry_check(sym, v, why)\n",
        "            ok = res.get(\"ok\", False)\n",
        "            _reason = res.get(\"reason\", \"\")\n",
        "            print(f\"🚦 AI ENTRY CHECK → {sym} | ok={ok} | reason={_reason}\")\n",
        "            if not ok:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not AUTO_MODE:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not ORDERS_ENABLED:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            if len(positions) >= MAX_OPEN_POSITIONS:\n",
        "                break\n",
        "            print(f\"🚀 ATTEMPTING ENTRY → {sym}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            # check BOTH local and engine positions\n",
        "            already_open = False\n",
        "\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                if sym in positions:\n",
        "                    already_open = True\n",
        "                if hasattr(engine, \"_positions\") and sym in engine._positions:\n",
        "                    already_open = True\n",
        "            except:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "            if already_open:\n",
        "                print(\"⚠️ Trade already active:\", sym)\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            msg = entry(sym, MAX_QTY)\n",
        "            if msg == \"OK\":\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "                print(\"Trades today:\", len(engine._traded_today))\n",
        "                tg_send(\n",
        "                    f\"🚀 Auto BUY {sym} | votes={v} \"\n",
        "                    f\"(need≥{current_min_votes()}) \"\n",
        "                    f\"size x{MAX_QTY}/{MAX_QTY} \"\n",
        "                    f\"{('[DRY]' if DRY_RUN else '')}\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "                engine._traded_today.add(sym)\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                print(\"Entry blocked:\", sym, msg)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Exit monitoring\n",
        "        if positions:\n",
        "            check_exits_once()\n",
        "\n",
        "    except KeyboardInterrupt:\n",
        "        print(\"Stopped.\")\n",
        "        break\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(\"Loop error:\", e)\n",
        "        time.sleep(2)"
      ]
    }
